Even from the available text, the useful distinction is likely among parse failures, structurally invalid certificates, coefficient mismatches, exhausted search, and premature stopping. That breakdown is more actionable than a single success number because it localizes whether the bottleneck is representation, tool coordination, or stopping behavior.3b:T43a,This is a skim paper rather than a deep-read paper. Start with the Introduction and the section on verified weighted-SOS search to understand the task boundary: exact verification on the output side, strategic search on the process side. Then read Figure 1 and Figure 2 carefully. They explain the separation between training and deployment, and why locally correct symbolic operations do not guarantee successful global certificate search. After that, go to the task table and the main results table, focusing on the differences among Base, SFT, Base+Tools, and Full.

If you only spend 20 to 30 minutes, extract three things: how the nine-task curriculum is decomposed, how simulated traces relate to native tool calls, and how terminal verification is defined. If you want one layer deeper, inspect the failure categories to see whether the model mainly fails by not finding the right search path or by failing to express a valid final certificate.

It is best read as a controlled study of tool coordination in a verifiable environment, not as a general mathematics agent paper.3c:T41a,Trajectory-level hindsight critique (TLHC) is added on top of GRPO. After each rollout, every failed trajectory is sent to a frozen judge (Qwen3.6-27B) together with the gold answer, producing a 1–2 sentence critique of what should have been searched differently. The critique is broadcast as a prefix to a frozen teacher (the student's initialization, also the GRPO KL anchor), whose log-probabilities supply an on-policy distillation (OPD) loss on the student's search-action tokens within failed trajectories. The OPD loss uses a clamp coefficient with a stop-gradient on the teacher-student logprob gap, so gradients flow only on tokens where the teacher is more confident than the student; a lower clamp restricts gradient flow and an upper clamp prevents outliers from dominating. Answer tokens are left to GRPO. Total objective is GRPO + λ_OPD·L_OPD with λ=0.01. The key design choice is that the judge sees the gold answer, making the critique directive rather than evaluative, and post-hoc generation removes the need for an online PRM.3d:T875,模型侧并不复杂，重点在实验设计。底座是一个 2B 参数的 frozen vision-language embedding model，只训练音频 connector 和每层 decoder 上的 modality-gated deep adapters；非音频输入时 gate 关闭，因此文本、图像、视频路径不被改动。嵌入是 Matryoshka-nested，默认读出 1024 维。预训练总语料约 888.8K clips，新增的 lexical-speech round 包含约 150K spoken-word clips、70K read-speech clips 和 audiobook 数据。训练中使用 frozen-text memory bank、false-negative masking 和 soft labels。

真正的创新在于作者如何排除其他解释，并把“判别轴”这个说法做成可检验机制。第一步，先观察 lexical speech round 带来的能力交换：关键词识别大涨，情感识别下滑。第二步，用一个 120 倍更小、但在设计上控制 lexical content 的 prosody corpus（Crema-D）做微调，证明情感能力可以快速恢复，因此不是容量不够。第三步，构造 matched-exposure negative control：用 29,428 条显式提到 emotion 的 mined clips，在相同 recipe 和 exposure 下微调，结果情感几乎不动，说明“caption 里写了情感”并不等于模型会学到情感。第四步，再排除 representational truncation 和 general degradation：前者只能解释一小部分差距，后者通过九任务板证明并不存在普遍退化。最后，作者提出 separability statistic，从语料本身估计某属性是否是区分负样本所必需的轴，并在同一批音频上做结构化干预：只改变语料组织方式，让 emotion 成为必须依赖的判别维度，情感能力就恢复；如果只是提高 caption 相似度而不改变判别需求，则恢复不了。这一步把相关性推进到了因果层面的证据。

和常见做法相比，这篇文章不是再发明一个 loss，也不是改模型容量，而是把预训练数据的“结构”拆成了更细的机制变量：同样的音频、同样的 exposure、甚至 caption 明确写出目标属性，都不足以保证属性进入表示；关键是 batch 内负样本能否在不看该属性的情况下被分开。3e:T735,最关键的结果有四组。第一组，加入 lexical-speech round 后，零样本 keyword spotting 提升 76 个点，而 speech emotion recognition 下降 14 个点。作者用的是 MAEB audio embedding benchmark / mteb 框架下的 SpeechCommands（35-way）和 Ravdess，评测方式都是把音频和类别文本映射到共享空间后做匹配。

第二组，容量假设被直接否掉。用只有 7,442 clips 的 Crema-D，从同一个 post-speech checkpoint 出发、固定 fine-tune recipe（batch 128，peak lr 3e-5，无 grad accumulation，关闭 false-negative masking 和 soft labels），只训 150 steps，emotion 从 0.211 恢复到 0.508，超过 pre-speech 的 0.348；同时 keyword 只降到 0.845，仍远高于 pre-speech 水平。训到 400 steps 时 trade-off 继续恶化，说明短 schedule 反而是更好的 operating point。作者还给了一个很直观的“交换率”：150 steps 左右大约是每损失 1 个 keyword 点，换回 6 个 emotion 点；150 到 400 steps 之间这个比率降到约 2。

第三组，数据量假设也被否掉。用 29,428 条 mined clips，caption 明确命名 emotion，且 exposure 匹配，结果 emotion 变化只有 -0.0007，几乎为零。这说明“更多带标签的数据”在这里没有触发目标属性学习。

第四组，机制验证。representational truncation 最多只能恢复约 18% 的差距；九任务板没有显示普遍性退化，因此情感下降不是被其他损伤掩盖。更重要的是，在同一音频上重组语料，让 emotion 成为 required discriminative axis 后，三种随机种子平均恢复 +0.089；而单纯提高 in-batch caption similarity 并不能带来同样效果。这个对照很关键，因为它把“相似文本更利于学情感”与“必须依赖情感才能区分负样本”区分开了。3f:T8d4,The model architecture is relatively standard; the novelty is in the experimental logic. The system starts from a frozen 2B vision-language embedding model, then trains only an audio connector—a query-based resampler—and modality-gated deep adapters attached to each decoder layer. The gate is closed for non-audio inputs, so text, image, and video pathways remain unchanged. The embedding is Matryoshka-nested with a default 1024-dimensional readout. Total pretraining data covers about 888.8K clips, and the added lexical-speech round contributes roughly 150K spoken-word clips, 70K read-speech clips, plus audiobook data. Pretraining uses a frozen-text memory bank, false-negative masking, and soft labels.

The paper’s real contribution is how it rules out alternative explanations and turns the “discriminative axis” idea into a testable mechanism. First, it establishes the capability trade-off induced by lexical speech: keyword recognition rises sharply while emotion recognition drops. Second, it uses a much smaller prosody-controlled corpus, Crema-D, to show that emotion can be recovered quickly, so the issue is not representational capacity. Third, it constructs a matched-exposure negative control using 29,428 mined clips whose captions explicitly name emotions; this produces essentially no emotion recovery, showing that explicit textual mention is not sufficient. Fourth, it rules out representational truncation and broad collateral degradation: truncation explains only a small fraction of the gap, and a nine-task board shows no general collapse. Finally, the authors define a separability statistic computed from the corpus itself to estimate whether an attribute is required for negative separation, and they validate the mechanism causally by restructuring the same audio so that emotion becomes the required discriminative axis. Emotion performance recovers under this intervention, whereas merely increasing in-batch caption similarity does not.

Compared with typical work, this is not a new loss or a larger model. It is a careful decomposition of “data quality” into a more precise variable: whether the training corpus structurally forces the model to rely on the target attribute during contrastive discrimination.40:T843,There are four key result blocks. First, after adding the lexical-speech round, zero-shot keyword spotting improves by 76 points, while speech-emotion recognition drops by 14 points. Evaluation is done on SpeechCommands (35-way keyword spotting) and Ravdess from the MAEB audio embedding benchmark in the MTEB framework, using shared-space matching between audio embeddings and class-text embeddings.

Second, the capacity explanation is directly rejected. Starting from the same post-speech checkpoint and using a fixed fine-tuning recipe—batch size 128, peak learning rate 3e-5, no gradient accumulation, false-negative masking and soft labels disabled—the authors fine-tune on Crema-D, a prosody-controlled corpus with only 7,442 clips. After just 150 steps, emotion rises from 0.211 to 0.508, surpassing the pre-speech level of 0.348, while keyword accuracy only falls to 0.845, still far above the pre-speech level. At 400 steps, the trade-off worsens, suggesting that the shorter schedule is the better operating point. The paper also quantifies the exchange rate: around 150 steps, roughly six emotion points are recovered per keyword point lost; between 150 and 400 steps, that rate drops to about two.

Third, the data-volume explanation is also rejected. Fine-tuning on 29,428 mined clips with captions that explicitly mention emotion, under matched exposure, changes emotion by only -0.0007—effectively zero. So more data, even with explicit attribute naming, does not trigger attribute learning.

Fourth, the mechanism is tested more directly. Representational truncation recovers only about 18% of the gap, and a nine-task board shows no broad degradation that could hide a positive effect. Most importantly, when the same audio is reorganized so that emotion becomes the required discriminative axis, emotion improves by +0.089 on average across three seeds. By contrast, simply increasing in-batch caption similarity does not produce the same recovery. This is the crucial control that separates “more similar text supervision” from “a corpus structure that forces reliance on emotion.”41:T500,For a skim-level read, focus on the causal chain rather than the audio specifics. Start with the Introduction and Section 4 to verify that the phenomenon is genuinely nontrivial: two speech tasks move in opposite directions under the same added supervision. Then read Sections 5 and 6 carefully. These are the core elimination steps: not capacity, not data volume. Pay close attention to Table 2, Figure 2, and Table 4, and map checkpoint choice, step count, corpus size, and metric movement. After that, jump to Section 9 for the separability statistic and then Section 11 for the intervention experiment; this is where the paper moves from observational evidence to a causal test. Tables 8 and 9 plus Figure 4 are the most informative artifacts. Sections 7 and 8 can be read quickly to confirm that truncation and collateral damage were checked.

If your work touches pretraining data or contrastive learning, the most useful questions to carry while reading are: how batch negatives determine which attributes survive in the representation; why explicit attribute naming in captions is still insufficient; and whether the separability statistic can transfer to image-text, video-text, speech-text, or broader data-mixture design. A solid skim should take about 25–35 minutes.42:T470,Experiments cover 4 models (Llama-3.1-8B, Mistral-7B, Qwen2.5-7B, Qwen3-8B), 2 tasks (SCBench-KV, SciFact), and 3 compression ratios (0.25/0.50/0.75). Across 16 model-task-ratio cells, forcing the wrong evidence route increases the wrong-answer margin in 13 cells, with no significantly negative cells and zero identity-replay label flips. Two competing patterns emerge: signal concentration (selectors retain Gold and Poison blocks far more often than Benign blocks) and integration loss (discarding blocks severs cross-block attention pathways, reducing content influence). Compression ratio is the key control variable: Δ shifts toward positive values (signal concentration dominates) at higher compression across all cells, with sign reversals in some cells. For example, Qwen3-8B on SCBench-KV shows Δ moving from -0.31 at c=0.25 to +0.93 at c=0.75. Cross-block attention ablation shows isolating a probe block from cross-block communication reduces its influence from 4.48 logits to exactly zero. The same directional pattern is confirmed across 8 cells when repeating the audit with KV-cache eviction methods (KVPress, SnapKV).43:T56b,The paper has three technical layers. First, it reframes memory retrieval as an implicit non-parametric policy and the stored utility score as a proxy reward. Under this view, writing high-scoring episodes and reusing them later is a form of policy improvement in memory space. The Echo Gap is then defined as a systematic mismatch between self-assessed reward and true success, amplified by retrieval frequency.

Second, the paper provides a diagnosis through the Error-Independence Assumption (EIA). The key claim is that a stronger verifier is not sufficient if its errors are correlated with the original self-grading bias. The recoverable benefit of correction depends on two quantities: how well verifier scores align with true utility, and how decorrelated verifier errors are from the original bias. This is the most useful conceptual contribution: correction requires not just more capability, but a different error channel.

Third, the paper proposes mitigation through per-memory de-inflation rather than global calibration. A label-blind global remapping of scores cannot identify which specific memories are overvalued. Instead, the authors argue for memory-specific demotion using auxiliary signals that are less likely to share the same bias. They instantiate this idea with an answer-free de-inflation method and test it in a Memento-style retrieve–inject–write loop.44:T55b,The experiments are split into bank-level diagnosis and end-to-end behavioral impact. At the memory-bank level, the paper shows that incorrect episodes are systematically over-rewarded across the studied model families and factual memory banks, and that the inflation correlates with future reuse. This is not mere random judge noise. More capable re-graders are often better on average, but their residual errors can still align with the original self-grading bias, which limits their corrective value.

At the agent level, the paper evaluates a faithful memory loop on BIRD text-to-SQL. The agent retrieves similar past episodes, injects them into context, solves the current task, and writes the new episode back without access to ground-truth labels at write time. Final evaluation uses official execution accuracy. The reported result is that self-graded write-back progressively corrupts the memory bank, while the proposed de-inflation method consistently improves over the self-graded baseline across random seeds. This shows that the Echo Gap is not only a scoring artifact; it translates into measurable behavioral degradation.

The provided content supports the qualitative findings and the structure of the empirical evidence, but it does not expose complete tables or trustworthy exact numbers. It would therefore be inappropriate to fabricate precise gains.45:T5fb,The model uses a 397B-A17B Qwen mixture-of-experts backbone. The observation is a screenshot, and the action space is native keyboard and mouse control. For long-horizon interaction, Qwen-CUA keeps 20 recent screenshots active and folds older screenshots into fixed-size blocks of ten. This preserves recent visual evidence while keeping the prompt prefix stable enough for KV-cache reuse. The rollout infrastructure runs on an Alibaba Cloud ECS fleet with access to nearly 100,000 vCPUs and tens of thousands of concurrent environments. The environment pool includes controllable mock web services, everyday desktop applications, long-tail applications, and professional software. The task set contains about 40,000 verifiable tasks covering environment operation, simulated-user interaction, and long workflows built through verifiable phase-state chaining. Human trajectories from personalized desktops and specialized software add supervision that pure synthetic environments would miss, with action-grounded step-level reasoning attached to the demonstrations. Training uses executable outcome rewards, the CUA-Gym environment design, Soft Adaptive Policy Optimization for stable updates over long reasoning-action sequences, and trajectory slicing to keep episode-level rewards usable when histories are image-heavy. The data loop is iterative: each trained model reveals unresolved SFT queries and weak domains, which then trigger teacher rerolls, new human trajectories, targeted data collection, and recalibrated RL tasks.46:T42e,The framework is built on Hunyuan3 and consists of two stages. First, agentic RL trains autonomous search behaviors such as planning, iterative retrieval, and evidence-based reasoning over dynamic environments. Second, cross-domain hybrid OPD is applied: large corpora are built for four domains—mathematics, coding, logical reasoning, and science—combining public benchmarks with internally curated or synthesized data; domain-specific expert teachers are trained on these corpora; then those teachers distill into the search-specialized model via on-policy distillation, transferring broad reasoning and knowledge capabilities while preserving the learned search policy. Difficulty-aware curriculum is used in teacher training and RL: problems are bucketed by empirical success rate of a reference policy into easy/medium/hard for math and coding, and medium/hard for logic and science. The design intentionally avoids naive post-RL SFT on generic data, which risks overwriting the search policy, and instead uses multi-expert OPD on states visited by the student.47:T46e,The attack is BCBI, or Boundary-Conditioned Backdoor Injection. The adversary fine-tunes a victim model and deploys selected agents with the poisoned version. Poisoned agents emit plausible evidence cues; each cue is insufficient alone, while cues from distinct peers jointly indicate that the hidden activation threshold has been reached. BCBI constructs counterfactual boundary pairs where task, role, round, and surrounding context are matched, but peer evidence moves from just below to just above the threshold. The output objective prefers benign behavior before the boundary and the adversarial target after it. A latent-space constraint organizes intermediate evidence states according to progression toward activation, making the transition sharper. The defense, LATTE, uses only clean interactions. It models hidden-state changes between adjacent communication rounds, learns a low-rank subspace of normal latent transitions, and screens each update before the response is released to peers. Suspicious updates are first quarantined and then, after repeated anomalies, isolated from both communication and final ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-01T04:17:14Z","category_id":"pretraining_data","cover_url":"/covers/2608_00432.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"深度研究（deep 的样本效率？","importance":"对预训练/持续预训练的贡献点很明确：把“图结构证据关系”转成一种可规模化的 continual pretraining 阶段（1B tokens），在不引入真实交互成本的前提下，让 base 模型学到更像研究员的导航决策（搜什么、看什么、如何逐步写）。它提供了一个可复用的接口：用离线图与文档内容合成 action-conditioned graph 或 Wikipedia hyperlink graph）。边表示自然关系（引用/链接）。\n- 核心构造：从一个 anchor 文档中抽取带引用/链接的段落 r 作为 target report；恢复其证据集合 Er（被引用/被链接且对段落内容有实质贡献的文档）；再从图拓扑邻域采样“相关但未被选中”的 alternatives，形成候选集。\n- 代理目标与动作空间：把段落内容归纳成 proxy research objective u（要研究/回答的问题），然后把监督渲染成统一的 search–open–write 轨迹。模型在给定 objective 与已观察证据历史的条件下，学习预测下一步该 search 什么、open 哪些文档、以及如何 write/整合证据。作者把这个训练目标称为 predictive navigation。\n- 关键取舍：不执行真实检索环境与 policy rollout；候选集与文档内容完全离线组装。这样牺牲了“真实交互分布”，换来极低的数据构造成本与可控的监督结构。\n- 两个实例化：DRP-Paper（学术引用图，Related Work 段落为 target report；引用论文为 evidence；邻近未引用论文为 alternatives）与 DRP-Web（Wikipedia 段落；过滤掉仅是顺带提及的链接，保留实质贡献链接为 evidence；邻近页面为 alternatives）。","experiments":"实验设置与结论（来自你提供的正文段落）：\n- 模型与训练：从 Qwen3-14B-Base 出发，分别做 1B tokens 的 DRP-Paper 或 DRP-Web continual pretraining，然后在 DR Tulu 的 13K GPT-5 生成 deep-research 轨迹上做 agent SFT；用不同数据预算评估样本效率。\n- 样本效率：在 DeepResearch Bench 上，低数据预算下（多次独立抽样的子集）DRP-Paper/DRP-Web 相比“无 DRP 的同等 SFT”稳定更好；用 1/4 轨迹时，DRP-Web 还能超过一个固定的 full-data Base checkpoint。\n- raw-document continual pretraining 与 evidence-mismatch 控制，支持收益来自“证据条件化的导航学习”，而不是仅仅多看了同域文本或学了 agent 格式。\n（注：你提供的片段未包含具体分数提升幅度、误差条、以及 DRP 数据的 token/样本构成细节；这些通常在实验表格与附录里。）","reading_guide":"泛读建议（30–45 SFT 预算（1/16、1/4 等）下的提升是否稳定、方差多大。\n- 必看控制实验：source-matched raw-CPT 与 evidence-mismatch，判断它是否真的在学“导航决策”而不是“领域暴露”。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Deep-research agents are typically trained with expensive environment-grounded tool-use trajectories (search, open documents, gather evidence, write reports, evaluate outcomes). The paper asks whether naturally occurring evidence structures in corpora (citations, hyperlinks) can be converted into rollout-free supervision for a pretraining stage that improves downstream agent SFT/RL sample efficiency.","importance":"The contribution is a practical continual-pretraining interface for agentic capabilities: use offline evidence graphs to synthesize action-conditioned trajectories at scale (1B tokens) without running a retrieval environment or collecting teacher rollouts. This paper proposes Pruned BPE, a post-training visibility-pruning and token-reallocation method that separates merge construction from model-visible vocabulary selection. After standard BPE training, tokens are evaluated by final exposure. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-01T19:34:44Z","category_id":"pretraining_data","cover_url":"/covers/2608_00837.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 BPE 词表分配里的一个老问题：BPE 的训练目标是逐步构造高频 merge，但语言模型真正看到的是最终编码后的可见 token 集。两者并不一致。很多 merge token 在训练过程中有用，只是作为更长 token 的中间节点存在；等最终编码完成后，这些 token 几乎不会真正出现在语料里。标准 BPE 仍然把它们放进模型可见词表，等于把有限的词表预算花在低暴露、低训练频次的 token 上。论文要解决的不是“怎么剪小词表”，而是“在保持可见词表大小不变的前提下，如何把可见词表从中间构造节点里解耦出来，重新分配给更值得暴露给模型的 token”。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要在 tokenizer 设计，而不是建模或训练 recipe。本工作提出了一个很具体也很实用的观点：BPE 的 merge 学习过程和 LM 的可见词表选择不必绑定。这个切分是有价值的，因为 tokenizer 往往被当成一次性离线组件，但它直接决定序列长度、embedding 参数利用率、不同语言脚本的分词质量，以及低频 token 的训练充分性。论文尤其点到了 byte-level BPE 在 Unicode 多字节脚本上的问题：中文、日文、韩文等更容易产生只对 merge 树有用、对模型无直接价值的中间 byte 片段。如果全文实验成立，这类后处理式词表重分配提供了一条低成本改进路径：不改模型结构，不改 BPE 主训练流程，只在 tokenizer 训练后做可见性筛选和 token 槽位重分配。量级上，它更像是 tokenizer 工程层面的稳健增益，而不是会改写 scaling 行为的大改动。","technical_approach":"方法分三步。第一步，先按标准 byte-level BPE 正常训练，得到完整 merge 树和一批按 merge 顺序生成的 token。这里没有改 BPE 的核心学习规则，也没有在训练时引入额外目标。第二步，训练完成后，用 fully encoded corpus 统计每个 learned token 的 final exposure，也就是在最终编码结果里它真正作为输出 token 出现的频次，而不是它在 merge 过程中被使用的次数。作者据此把 token 分成两类：可见 token 和 internal-only token。低 exposure token 仍保留在 merge 树里，继续作为内部构造节点参与更长 token 的形成，但不再分配给模型可见词表 ID。第三步，为了保持目标可见词表大小不变，作者继续在原 BPE 训练基础上 resumed training，训练到超过原目标词表大小，从后续候选 merge 中挑选 exposure 更好的 token，回填被剪掉的可见槽位。\n\n编码时的关键设计是：先按完整 merge 规则做标准 BPE 编码，得到最终 token 序列；如果其中某个 token 被标记为 internal-only，则递归展开成其可见子节点，直到输出序列只包含 visible vocabulary 中的 token。这样做保留了 merge 树的构造能力，但把模型接口限制在重新筛选后的可见词表上。论文强调这不是普通 vocabulary pruning，因为词表大小不变，merge 顺序也尽量保持；变化的是哪些 token 对模型可见。\n\n从方法取舍看，这个设计比“直接按频次删 token”更稳，因为它不破坏更长 token 的生成路径；也比“重新训练一个更大的 tokenizer 再截断”更可控，因为它显式区分了构造节点和可见节点。作者还做了 vocabulary-only evaluation，用共享的 exact minimum-token objective 去剥离 native BPE merge-order 编码差异，试图证明收益来自可见词表组成，而不只是编码器实现细节。这一点是全文里比较关键的论证。","experiments":"从给出的全文片段可以确认，实验重点不是大规模下游 LLM 预训练，而是 tokenizer 层面的词表质量评估与编码效果比较。作者提供了 shared exact minimum-token objective 的 vocabulary-only evaluation，用来在相同最短分词目标下比较标准 BPE 与 Pruned BPE 的可见词表组成差异。论文还强调实现支持 Unicode-aware pretokenization、自然语言与代码边界、CPU 并行训练、sharding 和 checkpoint，说明实验应覆盖多语种或至少包含非拉丁脚本场景。\n\n就方法预期而言，关键观察应包括几类：一是低 final exposure token 在标准 BPE 词表中占比不低；二是把这些 token 改成 internal-only 后，最终编码长度不会明显恶化，否则说明只是把问题转移到了更长序列；三是回填的新 token 在最终语料中暴露更高，词表利用率更好；四是在中文等多字节脚本上收益可能更明显，因为中间 byte 片段更多。当前提供的全文片段没有完整表格数字，无法可靠复述具体提升幅度、词表规模、阈值设置和不同语料上的 token 数变化，因此不应编造数值。精读时需要重点核对：final exposure 的分布图、不同阈值下 visible/internal 划分比例、编码长度变化、以及 vocabulary-only evaluation 的具体指标和绝对差值。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先抓住两个问题：为什么标准 BPE 会把中间 merge 节点错误地暴露给模型；作者如何在不破坏 merge 树的前提下把这些节点藏起来。阅读顺序上，先看 Introduction 里关于 final exposure 和 Unicode 多字节脚本的动机，再看 Proposed Method 中 internal-only token、resumed training、recursive expansion 这三处机制。然后直接找实验部分里三类图表：1）token exposure 分布；2）pruning threshold 与编码长度/词表利用率的关系；3）vocabulary-only evaluation。若时间有限，预计 20 到 30 分钟可以判断这是不是一个值得在自家 tokenizer pipeline 里复现的小改动。真正值得深挖的点有两个：其一，final exposure 是否足够，还是应该结合 PMI、跨语种覆盖率、embedding 更新频次等更贴近 LM 训练的指标；其二，递归展开后的分词序列是否会改变下游模型对形态边界和多语种字符边界的学习方式。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a mismatch inside standard BPE: merge learning optimizes for constructing frequent longer units, while the language model only cares about the final emitted tokens. Many learned merge tokens are useful only as intermediate nodes in the merge tree and rarely survive in the fully encoded corpus. Standard BPE still exposes all of them to the model-visible vocabulary, which wastes scarce vocabulary slots on low-exposure tokens that receive little direct training signal. The paper asks a narrower and more practical question than vocabulary compression: with the visible vocabulary size fixed, can we decouple merge construction from model-visible token selection and reallocate those slots to tokens that are actually emitted?","importance":"This is a tokenizer paper with direct relevance to pretraining efficiency and vocabulary design. Its main contribution is conceptual but practical: BPE merge learning and visible vocabulary exposure do not need to be the same object. That matters because tokenizer choices affect sequence length, embedding utilization, multilingual coverage, and how well token embeddings are trained. The motivation is especially plausible for byte-level BPE on UTF-8 text, where non-Latin scripts can generate many byte fragments that are necessary for building characters or words but are poor model-facing units. If the empirical results hold, the method offers a low-friction improvement path: no change to the LM architecture, no change to the core BPE training loop, only a post-training visibility filter plus slot reallocation. The likely impact is incremental but real, closer to a robust tokenizer engineering improvement than a change in language-model scaling behavior.","technical_approach":"$6","experiments":"$7","reading_guide":"For a skim, focus on two ideas: why standard BPE exposes the wrong tokens, and how the paper hides intermediate merge nodes without breaking the merge tree. Start with the motivation around final exposure and UTF-8 byte fragmentation in the introduction. Then read the method section for three mechanisms: internal-only tokens, resumed training beyond the target vocabulary size, and recursive expansion at encoding time. After that, jump to the experiments and look for three things: exposure histograms, the trade-off between pruning threshold and sequence length, and the vocabulary-only evaluation. In about 20 to 30 minutes, you should be able to decide whether this is worth reproducing in a tokenizer pipeline. The deeper questions are whether final exposure is the right criterion by itself, and whether recursive expansion changes the linguistic units the downstream LM ends up learning.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这个点抓得准：BPE 确实把不少词表浪费在“只参与构造、不该暴露给模型”的节点上；建议立刻做 internal ablation，看 token 数下降能否换成真实 pretrain gain。","en":"This targets a real inefficiency in BPE: too many vocabulary slots are spent on construction-only nodes; worth reproducing internally to see whether shorter sequences translate into actual pretraining gains."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Kenny Shao"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["tokenizer","BPE","vocabulary","token-efficiency","pretraining-data"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00837","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.00837","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.00144","title":"Leak It: A Probabilistic Approach to Training-Data Extraction from Black-Box Language Models","abstract":"Membership inference (MIA) on language models is usually summarised by an aggregate ROC-AUC, but such evaluations are confounded: model-free blind baselines separate members from non-members from surface text alone. We study black-box, sampling-based training-data leakage through a probabilistic lens, treating N samples from p(.|x) as an estimate of the output distribution and casting leakage signals as functionals of it. We extend the blind-baseline critique into the sampling regime: on WikiMIA","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-31T15:44:43Z","category_id":"pretraining_data","cover_url":"/covers/2608_00144.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL","cs.CR"],"summary":{"core_problem":"黑盒语言模型的成员推断（MIA）评估长期依赖聚合 ROC-AUC，但该指标已被证明会被成员/非成员的表面文本分布差异所混淆；同时现有采样类攻击缺乏统一的概率框架，且聚合指标无法捕捉单条训练数据的逐字泄露（如个人标识符提取）这一真实隐私危害。","importance":"对预训练数据隐私审计有直接方法价值：一是将采样类 MIA 统一为输出分布泛函估计的视角，澄清了采样信号在聚合分类上的真实增益边界；二是指出隐私审计应从聚合 AUC 转向逐文档提取率，这一指标更贴近版权合规、PII 泄露检测的实际需求。","technical_approach":"将给定前缀下的 N 次独立采样视为模型条件输出分布的经验估计，把三类采样泄露信号（与真实续写的平均重叠、极端重叠值、采样间自集中度）统一为该经验分布的泛函。沿用 Das 等人的盲基线校验范式，在成员/非成员同分布的基准上对比采样泛函与不访问模型的盲分类器（词袋、日期检测）的性能差异；进一步在包含真实邮箱、电话的 Pile 文档子集上做逐文档提取实验，通过错配前缀对照排除模型的通用生成能力干扰。","experiments":"WikiMIA 上盲词袋分类器 AUC 达 0.97（5% FPR 下 TPR 0.90），采样信号无额外增益；MIMIR 的 IID 切分上时间盲探针降至接近随机（AUC 0.56），但所有采样泛函相对残差盲基线的增量 AUC 95% 置信区间均包含 0，聚合分类无显著提升。在 500 条含真实标识符的 Pile 文档上，对 Pythia-6.9B 黑盒采样可逐字复现 83 条（16.6%）的标识符，其中邮箱提取率 21.3%；提取对温度、核采样参数不敏感，16 token 前缀即可触发，所需采样量与前缀长度负相关。","reading_guide":"重点阅读第 3 节的泛函统一框架与第 5 节的逐文档提取实验。第 4 节的盲基线对比用于建立评估规范，可快速浏览；附录中的采样预算与前缀长度的定量关系值得关注。预计阅读时间 40-50 分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Membership inference (MIA) on black-box language models has long relied on aggregate ROC-AUC, a metric confounded by surface-level distributional gaps between member and non-member documents. Existing sampling-based attacks also lack a unifying probabilistic framework, and aggregate metrics fail to capture the concrete privacy harm of verbatim extraction of individual training records such as personal identifiers.","importance":"The work provides actionable methodology for pretraining data privacy audits. It unifies sampling-based MIA under a functional-estimation lens and clarifies the marginal gain of sampling signals for aggregate classification. More importantly, it argues that privacy audits should shift from aggregate AUC to per-document extraction rate, which aligns with real-world requirements of copyright compliance and PII leakage detection.","technical_approach":"Treat N independent samples drawn under a given prefix as an empirical estimate of the model's conditional output distribution. Three sampling leakage signals — mean overlap with the held-out continuation, extreme overlap value, and self-concentration among samples — are formalized as functionals of this empirical distribution. Following the blind-baseline discipline from Das et al., sampling functionals are compared against model-free blind classifiers (bag-of-words, date detection) on benchmarks with identically distributed members and non-members. A controlled verbatim extraction experiment is run on a Pile subset containing real emails and phone numbers, with a mismatched-prefix control to rule out generic generation capability.","experiments":"On WikiMIA, a blind bag-of-words classifier reaches AUC 0.97 (TPR 0.90 at 5% FPR) and sampling adds no measurable gain. On the IID MIMIR split, a temporal blind probe collapses to near-chance (AUC 0.56), yet no sampling functional yields a significant incremental AUC over the residual blind baseline (95% CI includes 0). Across 500 Pile documents with real identifiers, black-box sampling from Pythia-6.9B reproduces the exact identifier for 83 documents (16.6%), with 21.3% extraction rate for emails. Extraction is insensitive to temperature and nucleus settings, triggers with a 16-token prefix, and the required sample budget scales inversely with prefix length.","reading_guide":"Focus on Section 3 for the unifying functional framework and Section 5 for the per-document extraction results. Existing audit techniques are limited, as they require intervention during data preparation or training and remain fragile under malicious obfuscations such as data paraphrasing and knowledge distillation.   We propose \\textit{Distribution Provenance Audit (DPA)}, a post-hoc framework for auditing data IP infringe","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-03T12:35:51Z","category_id":"pretraining_data","cover_url":"/covers/2608_02154.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"现有LLM微调阶段的数据知识产权审计方法存在明显缺陷：数据集水印、零知识证明等侵入式方法需要在数据发布或训练阶段提前介入，无法对已经发生的侵权行为做事后审计；成员推理攻击等事后方法依赖单条样本的似然或n-gram匹配信号，在对手使用释义、知识蒸馏等手段擦除逐字痕迹时检测准确率大幅下降，无法适配黑盒、恶意对抗场景下的审计需求。","importance":"该工作将数据溯源审计的范式从单条样本的成员检测转向分布层面的指纹匹配，其核心观察——“模型为保留领域效用必须保留训练数据中语义与词汇的联合统计特征”——为对抗场景下的数据溯源提供了可行的思路，同时指出了分布级审计能力与隐私攻击的结构性同构，对研究模型记忆、数据产权、隐私边界等问题有参考价值。","technical_approach":"核心出发点是理性对手面临硬效用约束：无论采用何种混淆手段，只要模型需要保留专有领域的专家级性能，就无法彻底擦除专有数据中语义内容（如领域推理逻辑）与词汇形式（如术语使用偏好）交叠形成的内在统计特征，即内在分布指纹。基于这一观察设计的DPA框架包含两个核心组件：一是联合语义-词汇相似度（JSLS）指标，同时度量语义一致性与词汇偏好匹配度，既避免纯表层词汇指标易被释义规避的问题，也避免纯语义指标无法区分通用领域知识与特定专有数据的问题；二是自指令数据提取（SIDE）算法，在黑盒条件下从模型自身的高似然区域自举采样，不依赖外部提示词，避免提示引入的分布偏差，忠实重构模型的内在输出分布。最终将审计建模为严格的统计假设检验，通过比较提取的模型分布与专有语料分布的指纹匹配度，判定专有数据是否被未经授权使用。分析同时指出，支撑审计能力的高保真分布指纹同样可被用于实施分布级隐私攻击与数据提取，可审计性与隐私泄露之间存在结构性的权衡。","experiments":"在医疗、法律两个专有领域微调任务上的评测显示：JSLS能够在用专有数据微调的模型与用独立同领域数据微调的模型之间形成清晰的判定边界；即使对手采用数据释义、知识蒸馏等混淆手段，DPA仍能以较高的统计置信度正确判定数据使用情况，检测效果显著优于传统成员推理攻击基线；消融实验验证SIDE提取的分布相较外部提示采样的分布更忠实于模型内化的统计特征，提示偏差明显更低。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，重点关注引言对现有审计方法缺陷的分析、“效用约束下的分布指纹留存”核心洞察，以及文末关于审计能力与隐私攻击同构性的讨论。普通读者可快速浏览JSLS与SIDE的具体实现细节；从事模型记忆、数据隐私、数据溯源方向的研究者可进一步关注其分布指纹构造与统计检验的设计细节。预计阅读时间15-25分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Existing data intellectual property auditing methods for LLM fine-tuning have critical gaps: invasive methods such as dataset watermarking and zero-knowledge proofs require proactive intervention during data release or training, and cannot support post-hoc audit of already occurring infringement; In this paper, we describe a practical training recipe for nGPT and evaluate it on modern hybrid Mamba-2--Transformer Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. The recipe introduces Logit Gradient Preconditioning, Logarithmic Learning Rate Decay, GatedAdamW, angular update control, and optional exploration mechanisms. Training it stably and efficiently on modern hybrid Mamba-2–Transformer MoE language models requires a coherent recipe of normalization, optimizer changes, and learning-rate design.","importance":"This is a substantial practical extension of nGPT. On the same hybrid MoE architecture, a 14B-total-parameter nGPT reaches the same validation loss as an unnormalized AdamW-trained model using roughly half the tokens. If the result holds at larger scale and under fuller ablations, it has direct implications for pretraining optimizers, representation normalization, and training efficiency.","technical_approach":"The recipe treats attention and MLP blocks as making angular updates on a hypersphere. The output layer uses a learnable vocabulary-wise logit scale s_z to control distribution sharpness. The authors observe that the mean of s_z grows approximately logarithmically during training and implicitly amplifies gradients flowing into all earlier layers. Logit Gradient Preconditioning keeps s_z in the forward pass but replaces its explicit backward multiplier with a normalized scale; with q=1, it removes the global mean growth while preserving relative vocabulary-wise variation. Other components are logarithmic learning-rate decay, GatedAdamW, Pre-Moment Tangent Projection, Angular Step Cap, Second-Moment Growth Clipping, and optional Post-Moment Exploration Noise. The MoE variant applies corresponding normalization to router and expert matrices; the same rules also apply to dense MLPs after removing MoE-specific modifications.","experiments":"Experiments use a Nemotron-3-style hybrid Mamba-2–Transformer MoE architecture with an industry-grade data blend, up to 14B total parameters. The main result is that nGPT reaches the same validation loss as an unnormalized model of the same architecture trained with AdamW using approximately half as many tokens. A dense 8B nGPT trained alongside a Nemotron-H cycle on 4T tokens motivates LGP: after an initial transient, the mean of s_z grows logarithmically. The authors explicitly note a limited compute budget, so they do not provide exhaustive component-wise ablations or hyperparameter scaling laws; several design choices remain empirical.","reading_guide":"Read carefully. Start with Figure 2 and the LGP derivation: the key point is not merely adding normalization, but identifying a learned logit scale that becomes a hidden global learning-rate multiplier over long training. Then read GatedAdamW, angular update control, and the moment-related modifications as a coupled system for constraining updates on the hypersphere. In the MoE section, focus on where router/expert normalization differs from the dense case. For experiments, compare validation loss against tokens, while keeping in mind the absence of full component ablations: the recipe works as a package, but individual contributions are not isolated.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"Ilya Loshchilov 亲自操刀的 nGPT 训练 recipe，在 14B 混合 MoE 上实现 2x 训练加速，必须立刻在内部小规模复现 LGP 和 LLRD，这可能是下一代基座模型的标配优化器方案。","en":"Ilya Loshchilov's practical recipe for nGPT achieves 2x training speedup on 14B hybrid MoEs; LGP and LLRD should be immediately ablated internally as they might become standard for next-gen foundation models."},"metadata":{"institutions":["NVIDIA"],"authors":["Ilya Loshchilov","Boris Ginsburg"],"notable_authors":["Ilya Loshchilov","Boris Ginsburg"],"tags":["nGPT","Optimizer","Training Recipe","Hybrid MoE","Mamba-2"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01284","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.01284","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.02091","title":"One QK Channel, Many Sources: Guarding Low-Precision Attention Collapse","abstract":"A bfloat16 transformer can train normally for many steps and then collapse abruptly. studies a late-collapse failure mode in bf16 Transformer training: a run can look healthy for many optimization steps and then fail abruptly. The key question is whether each low-precision fault must be fixed at its own source, or whether heterogeneous faults enter a shared failure channel. Using a GPT-2 small collapse on OpenWebText, the authors localize one reproducible fault to the streaming-softmax accumulator in a FlashAttention-2-style tiled online softmax recurrence. They then use this fault as an assay and show that errors originating both inside and outside attention converge to the same QK spectral runaway. Correcting the QK route blocks the collapse while leaving the fault active.","importance":"The main contribution is a clean separation between numerical fault sources and the channel that turns them into training failure. This is directly relevant to low-precision pretraining: loss spikes and sudden collapses are often debugged as local kernel or dtype bugs, but this paper shows that the dangerous part can be a shared QK route rather than the original error site. The practical implication is also clear. For temporally sign-coherent low-precision errors that perturb residual-block parameter updates, acting at the QK locus may be sufficient, whereas making every possible source fp32 is neither targeted nor scalable. Modern reranking recipes use billion-scale cross-encoders, distillation, and increasingly MoE backbones, while the existing Tevatron path through Hugging Face Trainer, DeepSpeed, and PyTorch FSDP1 is unreliable or inefficient for these workloads. The goal is to make dense and MoE reranker training reproducible under an academic-scale budget by adding a Megatron-Core backend to Tevatron.","importance":"This is not a core pretraining-method paper, but it is useful for researchers who train large post-training or retrieval models. The paper makes a practical point: parameter sharding alone is not enough for MoE training, because routed experts need expert parallelism. It also shows that a memory-safe FSDP configuration can still be throughput-poor. The compatibility choices are important: keeping the same data path, evaluation pipeline, and Hugging Face-loadable checkpoint format reduces the cost of switching training backends.","technical_approach":"Tevatron 3.0 adds a Megatron training backend while preserving Tevatron’s existing data pipeline, collator, evaluation interface, and checkpoint conventions. The implementation uses bridge-based HFMegatron weight conversion. For dense rerankers, the backend uses a Megatron-style distributed optimizer with tensor parallelism. For MoE rerankers, it uses expert parallelism so that active experts are routed and parallelized properly rather than replicated and sharded as ordinary parameters. The toolkit supports LoRA, full-parameter tuning, listwise-KL distillation, and contrastive training. It also adds a unified evaluation layer with local ranklist scoring and remote rerank-and-score through HF or vLLM scoring servers. A named LoRA target-group registry is introduced so adapters can be attached correctly to MoE expert layers.","experiments":"The evaluation focuses on trainability and throughput. For an 8B reranker, DDP runs out of memory. FSDP1 configurations fit but have limited throughput, and full_shard does not deliver a meaningful speed advantage over lighter sharding. The recommended Megatron configuration trains the dense 8B reranker about 22% faster while matching FSDP-trained quality. For MoE, the system trains a 30B-A3B reranker with 128 experts using expert parallelism, a setting that the FSDP1 path cannot support efficiently. The end-to-end demonstration sweeps dense 8B versus MoE 30B-A3B, LoRA versus full-parameter tuning, and listwise-KL distillation versus contrastive loss, evaluated on BEIR-15 with three first-stage retrievers. The reported demonstration shows the MoE reranker matching dense-8B quality with less than half the activated parameters and better inference throughput.","reading_guide":"Skim time: about 20–30 minutes. We introduce AOS-R (Adaptive Optimizer Switching, Rule-Based), a lightweight controller that monitors six online gradient-space signals -- gradient noise scale (GNS), Hutchinson curvature trace, loss stagnation, update stability ratio","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"glance","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-03T09:57:50Z","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2608_01997.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"解决单一优化器难以同时适配训练早期噪声梯度和后期平坦极小值泛化的问题。","importance":"贡献类型是训练 recipe / 优化器控制策略。它关注 gradient noise scale、曲率、更新稳定性等在线信号，这些信号对 LLM 训练诊断有可迁移价值；但实验主要是小中型视觉分类设定，不能直接外推到大规模预训练。","technical_approach":"AOS-R 是 rule-based adaptive optimizer switching 控制器，在线监控六个训练状态信号：GNS、Hutchinson curvature trace、loss stagnation、update stability ratio、gradient stability index 和 loss improvement ratio。它在 AdamW、SGD with momentum、Lion 之间切换，并用 state-preserving momentum transfer 和 400-step learning-rate bridge 减少切换时的精度损伤。","experiments":"在 CIFAR-100 / WRN-28x10 上，AOS-R 到达 78% top-1 需要 81 epochs，相比 AdamW 的 109 epochs 少 26%，相比 SGD-M 的 143 epochs 少 43%，相比 Lion 的 96 epochs 少 16%。摘要称在 8 个 model-dataset benchmark 中取得更好的准确率表现，但未给出 LLM 或大规模预训练实验。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses the mismatch between a single optimizer and different phases of training: noisy early optimization versus late-stage generalization in flatter regions.","importance":"It contributes a training-recipe and optimizer-control mechanism. nonlinear transformation they apply to representations. The paper asks whether a forward-pass measurement of each layer's geometric change can determine a better layerwise FFN width allocation under a fixed parameter budget.","importance":"This is an architecture-allocation paper. Its goal is not to add parameters, but to distribute an existing FFN budget according to layer demand. It provides a data-dependent alternative to hand-designed schedules such as Tapered Language Models and suggests that activation geometry can improve loss at fixed parameter and FLOP budgets. The measurements also expose functional differences across depth: raw work tracks residual-norm growth, while normalized work is concentrated in earlier layers.","technical_approach":"Each FFN is treated as a transport map from the post-attention token point cloud to the post-FFN residual point cloud. The induced geometric work is measured with three complementary tools: correspondence-preserving shift, Gromov-Wasserstein distortion, and degree-one persistent homology under raw and scale-normalized metrics. Euclidean, spherical, and hyperbolic geometries are compared; unit normalization is used to reduce sensitivity to the scale variations that LayerNorm or RMSNorm already remove. Geometric work serves as a proxy for approximation difficulty C_l in a layerwise approximation surrogate. Together with a loss-consequence term λ_l and a depth-dependent error-width exponent d_int(l), this yields a continuous fixed-budget optimizer with an exact width allocation. Hardware rounding is handled separately.","experiments":"Across seven pretrained decoder-only language models, raw Euclidean work largely tracks residual-norm growth, whereas scale-normalized work is predominantly front-loaded. Gromov-Wasserstein work shows a more consistent association with perturbation sensitivity. The main end-to-end evaluation is a five-seed 440M comparison at fixed parameter count, where topology/hyperbolic-based allocation reduces mean loss more than a TLM-style cosine taper, uniform width, and an anti-work control. The paper also studies scale sensitivity, local loss additivity, intrinsic dimension, and sensitivity association to assess which geometric signals are suitable for width allocation.","reading_guide":"For a quick read, start with the distinction between a prescribed taper and a measured allocation; that is where the paper differs from TLM. In the theory section, focus on how the fixed-budget optimizer converts geometric work into widths, especially how λ_l and d_int(l) modify a simple proportional rule. In the experiments, prioritize the work profiles across seven models, the contrast between raw and normalized signals, and the five-seed 440M comparison. A useful follow-up question is that geometric work is only a proxy for approximation difficulty; the paper does not directly fit layerwise error-width curves. Calibrating C_l with real perturbation or pruning errors could yield a stronger allocation rule. Existing methods, however, typically connect these states to the reasoning trajectory through decoded tokens, making sequence-level credit assignment indirect and obscuring how latent updates shape subsequent reasoning. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-03T17:55:24Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2608_02585.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作处理 test-time latent reasoning 的信用分配问题。已有 LatentSeek、LTPO、MILR 一类方法在测试时优化样本级连续 latent，但 latent 通常先被解码成 token，再通过离散 token 序列影响后续推理。这样 reward 信号只能间接穿过解码过程回到 latent，既弱化优化信号，也让每个 latent 对后续 reasoning token 和最终答案的作用难以解释。","importance":"对预训练方向的直接价值不在训练 recipe，而在 latent reasoning 与模型内部计算接口。它把可优化 latent 放到 Transformer 中间层 hidden space，而不是放在输出侧 token 接口，这提供了一种更接近模型内部计算图的测试时推理增强方式。对研究 pretrain-posttrain 接口也有启发：如果模型未来要支持 latent thinking 或 internal scratchpad，latent 插入位置、attention 可达性、梯度路径是否直接，会影响推理时优化的稳定性和可解释性。","technical_approach":"GradCuit 选择某个 Transformer 中间层作为 latent 优化空间。对一个 M 层 decoder，在第 l 层后得到 prompt 和已生成 token 的 hidden states，然后把一组可优化 latent states 插入这些表示之间，再经过剩余层和 LM head 预测下一个 token。这样 latent 不是先映射成离散 token，而是直接作为 self-attention 中的节点参与后续层计算。生成 token 对 latent 的影响路径由剩余 Transformer 层中的 更新贡献更大。和 LatentSeek 的主要区别是：LatentSeek 的 latent 作用在输出侧，需要通过 decoded prefix 建立影响；GradCuit 的 latent 作用在中间 hidden space，梯度路径更短，也更贴近 Transformer 自身的计算结构。","experiments":"实验覆盖 5 个 backbone 和 3 个 reasoning dynamics。若只关心预训练启发，可重点思考一个问题：预训练时是否可以显式暴露或正则化这种中间层 latent slot，让模型在训练阶段就学会使用可优化 internal states。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets the credit-assignment problem in test-time latent reasoning. Prior methods optimize instance-specific continuous latents, but the latents usually affect reasoning through decoded tokens. The reward signal therefore reaches the latents only through a discrete generation interface, making optimization indirect and making it hard to tell how a latent variable contributed to later reasoning tokens or the final answer.","importance":"The main relevance to foundation-model research is the interface between latent reasoning and the model’s internal computation, rather than a new pretraining recipe. GradCuit places optimizable latent states inside the Transformer hidden space instead of at the output-token interface. This is a useful design point for future latent-thinking models: where the latent state is inserted, whether tokens can attend to it, and how directly gradients can reach it all affect robustness and interpretability at inference time.","technical_approach":"GradCuit selects an intermediate layer of an M-layer decoder as the latent optimization space. After the first l layers process the prompt and previously generated tokens, a small set of latent states is inserted into the hidden-state sequence. The concatenated sequence is then passed through the remaining Transformer layers and the LM head to predict the next token. Since these latent states participate directly in self-attention, each generated token can attend to the latent positions, and reward-weighted gradients can flow back to the latents through the same Transformer computation path. The method also inspects the Jacobian of token probabilities with respect to latent states to analyze token-latent sensitivity. The key difference from LatentSeek is the interface: LatentSeek lets latents influence generation through decoded prefix tokens, while GradCuit lets them operate as hidden-state nodes inside the attention circuit.","experiments":"The experiments cover five backbones and three reasoning benchmarks, with comparisons against standard CoT and four enhanced reasoning baselines. Averaged over all backbones, benchmarks, and answer formats, GradCuit improves over CoT by 6.6 percentage points. Its overall average accuracy is 64.5%, compared with 62.1% for the strongest competing method. In a robustness study over seven learning-rate settings, GradCuit consistently outperforms LatentSeek and reduces the standard deviation of accuracy from 1.53 to 0.82. A useful ablation replaces policy-gradient updates with a random walk; GradCuit remains competitive with LatentSeek, suggesting that the intermediate hidden-state latent space is easier for the model to use than an output-side latent interface. The interpretability analysis uses Jacobians and highlights the role of reasoning-connector tokens in latent dynamics.","reading_guide":"A 25–35 minute skim is enough. Focus first on the algorithm section: the latent insertion point, the modified autoregressive factorization, and the reward-weighted latent update. Then read the experimental tables for average accuracy, learning-rate sensitivity, and the random-walk ablation. The Jacobian-based interpretability figures are worth checking because they clarify whether the method only improves test-time search or also provides a usable view into latent dynamics. f","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-02T23:01:10Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2608_01534.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究如何让预训练 LoRA、深监督和约束投影把模型行为压到“改进当前候选解”上。对于研究 latent reasoning、any-order correction、非一次性解码范式的人，它提供了一个工程上更容易落地的基线。","technical_approach":"R-Qwen 把多类任务统一表示为三元组 (x, c, y*)：x 是带空位符号的初始状态，c 是可选上下文，例如自然语言线索或示例，y* 是目标解。每一步维护一个候选解 y^(t)，初始候选解来自题目本身。模型输入包含原题、上下文、上一轮候选解、当前 refinement step 和总步数，输出一个新的序列化候选解。输出后会经过任务特定的 projection，保证硬约束不被破坏，例如保留 Sudoku 给定数字、迷宫或网格任务中的固定结构。训练上，论文冻结 Qwen 主体，用 LoRA curriculum、amortized candidate generation 和 early stopping 使训练时间和成本降低超过 50%。实验还系统分析了训练递归深度与推理递归深度的关系：更多测试时递归通常能带来改进，但收益受任务类型、候选解质量和 early stopping 策略影响。可用内容没有列出完整分任务数值，因此重点应放在递归训练 recipe projection 和深监督损失，它们决定了这个方法是“递归修正”而不是普通多轮提示。训练部分重点看 HSW、recursion-depth curriculum、amortized candidate generation、per-instance early stopping。实验部分优先看 recursion depth 的训练/推理 trade-off、各任务的消融，以及与 HRM/TRM 的差异。对预训练研究来说，值得进一步思考的是：这种显式候选解循环能否转化为预训练阶段的 denoising/refinement objective，而不是只在特定符号任务上 LoRA 适配。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies how a pretrained Qwen-based model can solve symbolic and spatial reasoning tasks through repeated candidate refinement rather than a single autoregressive pass. The central issue is that fixed-depth autoregressive generation tends to propagate early mistakes, while tasks such as Sudoku, maze solving, ARC, and crosswords often require correction, constraint checking, and backtracking-like behavior.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper, but it is relevant to test-time compute and the interface between pretrained models and recursive reasoning. Its useful contribution is a simple substrate for recursion: keep the backbone unchanged, serialize the intermediate solution, feed it back into the same model, and train the model as a refinement operator. Compared with latent recursive models such as HRM or TRM, this design keeps the linguistic and visual priors of a pretrained model and avoids introducing a specialized architecture.","technical_approach":"R-Qwen represents each task instance as (x, c, y*), where x is the serialized initial state with empty slots, c is optional context such as clues or demonstrations, and y* is the target solution. The model maintains an explicit candidate y^(t). At each recursion step, a chat-formatted prompt includes the original instance, the context, the current candidate, and the refinement index. The model outputs an improved serialized candidate, which is then passed through a task-specific projection that preserves hard constraints. The Qwen backbone is frozen and adapted with LoRA. Training applies deep supervision at every refinement step, with Hierarchical Supervision Weighting assigning larger weights to earlier, more informative steps. The training recipe also includes a recursion-depth curriculum, amortized candidate generation, and per-instance early stopping to reduce unnecessary refinement.","experiments":"The paper reports results on eight benchmarks spanning Sudoku, maze pathfinding, ARC-AGI-1/2, and CrossWordBench. It compares R-Qwen with recursive models such as HRM and TRM, as well as larger LLMs and VLMs. The reported findings are that explicit recursive refinement improves reliability on exact structured-output tasks, and the proposed efficiency measures reduce training time and cost by more than 50%. The study also examines the trade-off between training-time recursion depth and inference-time recursion depth. Since the available text does not include full per-task numerical tables, the main result to retain is the training and inference recipe rather than any single benchmark number.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. Read the unified serialized formulation first, then the prompt construction and constraint-preserving projection. These parts define the method more clearly than the benchmark framing. The training section is the most useful part: focus on deep supervision, Hierarchical Supervision Weighting, recursion-depth curriculum, amortized candidate generation, and early stopping. In the experiments, look for recursion-depth ablations and comparisons with HRM/TRM. A useful pretraining-facing question is whether this explicit refinement loop can be turned into a broader denoising or correction objective during pretraining, rather than remaining a LoRA adaptation for symbolic puzzles.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得看 §方法和梯度方差实验；若 setup 干净，它会改变“递归只适合小专用模型”的判断。","en":"Read the method and gradient-variance ablation; if the setup is clean, it weakens the prior that recursion only works for small task-specific models."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Omid Nejati Manzari","Guillaume Lajoie","Hassan Rivaz"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["recursive-reasoning","latent-reasoning","Qwen","symbolic-reasoning","deep-supervision"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01534","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.01534","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.02032","title":"DART: Decoded Attention over Recurrent States for Efficient Long-Context Sequence Modeling","abstract":"Modern language models are built primarily from Transformers, recurrent models, and their hybrid architectures. The paper asks whether one can add cross-dimensional recurrent dynamics without paying the cost of a dense state transition.","importance":"The paper is most relevant as an architecture idea for long-context language modeling. Its contribution is incremental rather than a new pretraining recipe. The useful part is the low-rank recurrent coupling inserted into selective SSM dynamics. Mamba gains linear-time processing by simplifying the state transition, but that simplification reduces direct communication among state channels. Motif-Mamba tries to recover part of this expressivity with a compact recurrent pathway while preserving the main efficiency profile.","technical_approach":"The method augments the selective SSM update with a low-rank recurrent pathway. Two adapter matrices project the high-dimensional hidden state into a small latent dynamical subspace and map it back. The coupling can be written as M_I M_J, with rank at most r where r is much smaller than the state dimension n. This avoids a dense n-by-n transition matrix. The paper then constrains the interaction pattern in the low-dimensional subspace using directed three-node network motifs. In the continuous-time formulation, the low-rank term acts as an additional recurrent driving component; after exponential Euler discretization, it becomes part of the token-wise SSM update. Compared with standard Mamba, the model adds explicit cross-state communication. Compared with an unconstrained low-rank correction, it imposes a motif-guided structure on the latent dynamics.","experiments":"The paper evaluates the model on long-sequence extrapolation, standard language modeling benchmarks, motif ablations, and brain-computer-interface decoding. The ablations compare motif choices with different dynamical properties and study their effect on hidden-state evolution. The supplied full-text material does not include concrete table values, parameter counts, rank choices, or data scales, so the gains should not be treated as quantified here. When the value route query is tied to the QK route query, RD-AttnRes exactly recovers Block AttnRes — making it a strict superset extension. 一个结构性优势：时间平移不变性使得预计算状态可以无损注入，这在带位置编码的 Transformer 上根本不成立。Theorem 1 证明了 PRECOG 与 in-context RAG 产生完全相同的状态轨迹，这不是经验近似而是数学保证。这一性质对检索增强、持久记忆、上下文复用等场景有架构层面的启发，也为 SSM vs. Transformer 的比较提供了一个新的维度——不仅是推理效率，更是状态可组合性。论文的边缘部署定位不影响其核心洞察的通用性。","technical_approach":"PRECOG 离线对每个文档 chunk 运行 SSM 语言模型，捕获逐层隐状态；查询时通过轻量 embedding 索引检索最匹配的状态，直接作为模型循环状态的初始条件注入。由于 SSM 递推具有时间平移不变性（Δt 和 B 仅依赖当前 token，A 固定），预计算状态与在查询开头重新摄入该 chunk 达到的状态完全一致。同一机制无法迁移到 Transformer：RoPE 或绝对位置编码使 KV-cache 位置纠缠，预计算 cache 在其他位置失效；即使忽略位置问题，3000-token 上下文的 Transformer KV-cache 存储量约为 SSM 隐状态的 500 倍。论文还提出 SMC（Structured Memory Consolidation），将历史交互状态组织为分层持久记忆，按认知域聚类、两级检索、可调保真度-存储权衡，会话初始化保持 O(1)。","experiments":"在 TENNs-LLM（1.2B 参数，24 层 gated selective SSM，d=2048，N=4096，总隐状态 192KB at FP16）上实例化。领域问答任务上 PRECOG 与 in-context RAG 质量持平（数学保证一致），部署目标上 prefill 延迟从约 27s 降至 <6ms，约 4500 倍加速。存储对比：3000-token chunk 的 SSM 隐状态远小于等效 Transformer KV-cache（约 500 倍差异）。SMC 部分报告了分层记忆的经验行为，但全文未提供大规模通用 benchmark 上的对比，实验主要面向边缘部署场景。","reading_guide":"泛读。重点看 Section 2 的 Theorem 1 及其证明思路——这是全文最有价值的部分，解释了为什么状态注入在 SSM 上是精确的而在 Transformer 上不可行。Introduction 中关于 KV-cache 位置纠缠的讨论也值得读。TENNs-LLM 架构细节和 SMC 部分可快速浏览。预计阅读时间 25-35 分钟。对架构方向研究者，建议思考：这种状态可注入性是否可以推广到混合 SSM-Transformer 架构（如 Jamba），以及预编码状态的检索质量如何评估。","authors_note":"作者团队未在提供内容中明确列出机构信息，涉及 TENNs-LLM 和神经形态边缘处理器部署，相关专利申请中。"},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"RAG on The paper exploits the SSM's fixed-size, position-agnostic recurrent hidden state to pre-encode document corpora as states and inject them directly at query time, reducing prefill from O(L) to O(1).","importance":"The architectural value lies in a structural advantage of SSMs: time-translation invariance enables lossless state injection, which is fundamentally impossible for Transformers with positional encodings. Theorem 1 proves that PRECOG and in-context RAG produce identical state trajectories—a mathematical guarantee, not an empirical approximation. This property has implications for retrieval augmentation, persistent memory, and context reuse, and adds a new dimension to SSM vs. Transformer comparisons: not just inference efficiency but state composability. The edge-deployment framing does not diminish the generality of the core insight.","technical_approach":"$11","experiments":"Instantiated on TENNs-LLM (1.2B parameters, 24-layer gated selective SSM, d=2048, N=4096, total hidden state 192KB at FP16). Indexer for fine-grained token selection and achieves near-full-attention quality on long contexts, but deployment exposes two system bottlenecks. The indexer still scores every prefix token and retains O(L^2) overhead, reaching up to 90% of per-layer latency at 1024K context. Its selected indices are also discontinuous, forcing scattered HBM accesses in the sparse-attention operator and yielding only about 4.5% of peak HBM bandwidth. LongCat Sparse Attention targets both indexing cost and memory-access efficiency.","importance":"The work is directly relevant to long-context pretraining and inference. DSA has been adopted by large-scale models such as DeepSeek-V3.2 and GLM-5, but its indexer cost becomes dominant at very long contexts. LSA combines cross-layer indexing, streaming-aware contiguous layout, and hierarchical retrieval to reduce both training and inference cost. Validation on Meituan LongCat models at 69B, 560B, and 1.6T scales shows that the design is not merely a kernel trick but a sparse-attention recipe usable in foundation-model training.","technical_approach":"$12","experiments":"LSA is evaluated on LongCat-Flash-Lite (69B-A3B) and LongCat-Flash (560B-A27B), matching full attention on general, reasoning, coding, and long-context benchmarks. The improved training efficiency over full MLA enables training LongCat-2.0 (1.6T-A48B) with up to one-million-token context under a limited compute budget. LongCat-Flash-Lite-Sparse extends native context length to 1M and provides higher long-context inference efficiency and stronger agentic capabilities than its dense predecessor. Ablations indicate that the training-aware SI and CLI mechanisms preserve quality, while the plug-and-play HI module adds inference speedup with marginal quality loss.","reading_guide":"Read in depth. The analysis defines and measures sessions, turns, LLM calls, tool calls, context compaction events, model switches, and idle intervals. It focuses on five workload properties: the coupling between LLM calls and tool execution; KV-cache behavior within turns and across turn boundaries; cache invalidation from model switches and compaction; compute amplification caused by tool failures and retries; heterogeneity across user populations; and the alternation between GPU-bound inference and CPU/IO-bound tool phases. 的设计目标从“如何压得更短”改成“如何让 decoder 看到更像原始中间表示的东西”，这比单纯做 prompt 压缩更接近模型内部表征工程。第二，它明确利用中间层 hidden state 作为可压缩、可重建的记忆单元，而不是只在 token 层做选择，这对未来的训练-推理接口设计有启发：如果模型内部表征比 token compression systems. Many soft-compression methods use compact memory tokens both as storage and as the decoder's conditioning prefix. Under high compression, those memory slots can drift away from localized source content, and the decoder is forced to generate from overly compressed representations. Third, sparse reconstruction avoids reconstructing the full cache at every decoding step and keeps only informative positions for attention, improving throughput.","experiments":"The abstract does not provide model sizes or benchmark details, but the key experimental questions are clear: quality degradation at a given compression ratio, prefilling latency, decoding throughput, and robustness across prompt distributions. agent 场景下的在线 KV cache 压缩，而不是普通长上下文静态压缩。难点不在于“怎么压缩一个已知文档”，而在于 agent 的上下文是边交互边增长的：推理轨迹、工具调用、环境反馈会持续追加进上下文，压缩某一轮时，未来会怎么读这段信息还不知道。于是离线场景里常见的 query-aware 压缩信号在这里不再天然可用。论文要回答的是：在在线 agent 循环里，什么样的 proxy query 能以足够低的额外开销指导 KV 压缩；不同压缩家族在这种不完美信号下谁更稳；延迟压缩是否值得。","importance":"这不是 pretrain 论文，但对基座研究员仍有价值，主要在长上下文推理与 serving 接口。它把一个常被离线设定掩盖的问题讲清楚了：agent 的 KV 压缩核心不是压缩算法本身，而是压缩时拿什么 query 代表未来读取需求。这个结论对训练侧也有启发，因为很多长上下文建模、记忆模块、甚至训练时的 context packing，都默认未来读取模式可知或可近似。论文显示在真实 agent 轨迹里，这个假设很脆弱。另一个有用点是，简单的 token eviction 在代理信号不准时反而比更复杂的 attention matching 更稳，说明在线系统里“更强的拟合能力”不一定带来更好的端到端效果，代理信号质量和时机往往更关键。","technical_approach":"作者没有提出全新压缩算法，而是系统比较两类已有 sequence-level compaction 方法在在线 agent 场景下的适配。第一类是 token eviction，简称 TE。它用一组 proxy queries 去计算原始 KV cache 中各位置被关注的程度，然后只保留得分最高的若干位置的原始 K/V。第二类是 attention matching，简称 AM。它先做类似的 key 选择，再进一步拟合 additive attention bias 和重构后的 values，使压缩后 cache 对这些 proxy queries 的 attention 输出更接近完整 cache。\n\n论文的核心工作在于把“proxy query 从哪里来”这个问题系统化。作者比较了几种在线可得的信号来源：1）boundary queries，即当前完成 turn 边界附近的 query；2）repeat-prefill 这类轻量代理，即重复 prefill 或从当前上下文中抽取廉价查询信号；3）deferred future-generation queries，即先不立刻压缩，等 agent 后续生成了若干 token 或下一轮内容后，再用这些真实未来 query 反过来指导对前一轮内容的压缩。这里的关键取舍很明确：立即压缩延迟低，但 query 失真大；延迟压缩信号更贴近真实读取模式，但会多占一段时间的 KV 内存。\n\n从方法判断看，论文最重要的不是 TE/AM 数学细节，而是在线 compaction protocol：按 completed turn 为单位压缩；在 agent loop 内控制额外计算预算；比较不同 proxy source、不同 delay、不同 compaction ratio 的交互。作者把问题限定在 practical online compaction，而不是追求离线最优重构误差，这个设定是合理的，因为 agent serving 的瓶颈就是延迟、显存和吞吐的联合约束。","experiments":"实验在 BrowseComp-Plus 和 WideSearch 两个 agent benchmark 上进行，模型包括 Qwen3.5 和 Gemma-4 的多个尺度。摘要和正文片段给出的主结果比较清楚：立即压缩通常会伤害任务性能，而延迟压缩、使用 agent 自身后续生成得到的 future queries，可以稳定追回大部分性能损失。在 Qwen3.5-27B 和 Gemma-4-31B 上，compaction ratio 设为 0.2 时，延迟压缩能在保留大部分 no-compaction 准确率的同时，把 peak KV footprint 分别降到最多 3.5× 和 2.7×，吞吐分别提升最多 4.2× 和 1.7×。\n\n另一个重要实验结论是 TE 的稳健性。尽管 AM 更复杂，理论上更能拟合完整 attention 输出，但在 proxy 不完美、预算有限、压缩时机受限的在线场景里，TE 依然很有竞争力，甚至更稳。这说明误差来源主要不是“压缩器表达能力不够”，而是“用于指导压缩的 query 代表性不足”。如果全文里有消融，最值得看的是：不同 proxy source 的质量排序；delay 长度与性能/内存的折中；不同 compaction ratio 下 TE 与 AM 的交叉点；以及不同模型规模是否改变这一结论。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction 和 Design Space of Online KV Compaction，把问题设定吃透：在线 agent 压缩和静态 prompt 压缩不是一回事。然后重点看方法部分对 TE、AM 和 proxy query source 的定义，不必陷入公式细节，核心是理解它们各自依赖什么信号。实验部分优先看三类图表：1）立即压缩 vs 延迟压缩；2）不同 proxy source 的效果；3）压缩率、峰值 KV、吞吐、任务成功率之间的 trade-off。预计 25 分钟左右可以抓住主要结论。值得深挖的点有两个：一是能否把“延迟后用真实未来 query 压缩过去 turn”做成训练信号，反过来蒸馏一个更好的在线 proxy predictor；二是 TE 的稳健性是否意味着 agent 记忆压缩更适合做保守选择，而不是高自由度重构。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies online KV-cache compaction for LLM agents, which is a different problem from static long-context compression. In an agent loop, context grows turn by turn through reasoning traces, tool calls, and environment feedback. When a completed turn must be compacted, the future queries that will later read that information are still unknown. This breaks a key assumption behind many offline query-aware compaction methods. The paper asks three practical questions: what proxy queries are available online at low enough cost, how different compaction families behave under imperfect proxy signals, and whether delaying compaction is worth the extra temporary memory.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper, but it is relevant to foundation-model researchers working on long-context inference, memory, and serving-aware model design. The main value is that it isolates the real bottleneck in agent KV compaction: not just the compactor, but the quality and timing of the query signal used to decide what to preserve. That matters beyond serving. Many training-time assumptions around long-context modeling, memory modules, and context packing implicitly assume future read patterns are known or can be approximated. The paper shows that this assumption is fragile in interactive trajectories. Another useful takeaway is that a simpler method, token eviction, can be more robust than a more expressive attention-matching method when the proxy signal is noisy. In online systems, better fitting capacity does not automatically translate into better end-to-end behavior.","technical_approach":"$17","experiments":"$18","reading_guide":"For a skim, start with the problem setup in the introduction and the design-space section. The key point is that online agent compaction is not the same as static prompt compaction. Then read the method definitions of TE, AM, and the proxy-query sources; the formulas matter less than understanding what signal each method depends on. In the experiments, prioritize three comparisons: immediate versus delayed compaction, different proxy sources, and the trade-off among compaction ratio, peak KV memory, throughput, and task performance. In roughly 25 minutes, you should have the main picture. The deeper questions are whether delayed future-query signals can be turned into training targets for a learned online proxy predictor, and whether the robustness of TE implies that agent memory compression should favor conservative selection over high-flexibility reconstruction.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看 proxy query 对比和 TE/AM 失效案例；它不会改预训练方向，但会改变 agent 长轨迹 serving 的默认压缩策略。","en":"Read the proxy-query comparison and TE-vs-AM failure cases; it will not change pretraining, but it should update default serving choices for long agent traces."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Yujian Liu","Jiabao Ji","Li An","Rohit Jain","Gungor Polatkan","Siyu Zhu","Shiyu Chang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["kv-cache","agent-serving","online-compaction","long-context","token-eviction"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00902","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.00902","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.01034","title":"Opt.Gear Technical Report","abstract":"We introduce Opt.Gear, a foundation model designed for efficient on-device deployment, real-tim inference, and strong task capability. still require QK score computation, softmax, and repeated KV-cache traffic, which becomes expensive on mobile NPUs, GPUs, and MCU-class devices with limited SRAM and memory bandwidth.","importance":"The main value is the joint design of architecture, pretraining recipe, and deployment constraints. Instead of only shrinking parameter count, the model replaces many local attention computations with bounded-state convolutional mixing while retaining some GQA layers for global routing. This is relevant for pretraining researchers working on small long-context models, hardware-aware backbones, and edge-native LM design. The claim of training from a curated 0.5T-token subset selected from a 2T-token pool without distillation is also interesting, but it should be judged together with the filtering recipe, evaluation coverage, and ablations.","technical_approach":"The backbone is a hybrid of GQA, ConvKV-Gated Mixer blocks, and dense feed-forward modules. The ConvKV-Gated Mixer applies causal convolution to key/value streams and gates values using query-key interactions. The design removes much of the sequence-length-dependent KV-cache used by local attention and replaces it with a small convolutional state. This differs from sliding-window attention, which still pays for QK matmul and softmax, and from fully recurrent or SSM-style models, which may weaken content-addressable retrieval. Opt.Gear keeps GQA layers to preserve global information flow. The training pipeline is bilingual Korean-English, uses the KORMo tokenizer, and includes pretraining, long-context extension, and supervised fine-tuning.","experiments":"Reported numbers include 1M, 270M, and 1B dense models; 64K context length; 0.5T training tokens selected from a 2T-token candidate corpus; no knowledge distillation; and up to about 4.9× faster prefill and decoding on NPUs compared with similarly sized models. The 1M model is positioned for MCU deployment with W4A32 quantization on ARM Cortex-M7 and a sub-1MB target. The visible evidence emphasizes latency, KV-cache reduction, and deployment compatibility across Transformers, llama.cpp, ExecuTorch, vLLM, Qualcomm Hexagon NPU, and Apple ANE. The quality-side tables and ablations should be checked carefully, especially for long-context retrieval and reasoning.","reading_guide":"Skim in 25–35 minutes. quantization for reasoning LLMs. The goal is to compress models to 1.58-bit weights without expensive quantization-aware training, distillation, or training from scratch, while preserving math, Existing 1.58-bit LLMs are often tied to QAT or full pretraining recipes, which makes low-bit capability expensive to reproduce and hard to scale across architectures. A reliable PTQ method would decouple low-bit deployment from the original pretraining run. It also raises a calibration issue that matters for post-training pipelines: reasoning models should be quantized under distributions that resemble their long CoT trajectories, not only short generic text samples.","technical_approach":"The paper frames ternary quantization around three primitive choices: the scaling factor alpha, the threshold Delta, and the optimization treatment of non-differentiable ternarization. Earlier ternary methods for LMs often use static statistical rules for alpha and Delta, and QAT relies on STE during training. ScaleQ-1.58 moves this problem into a scalable PTQ setting and uses the model’s own reasoning behavior as a calibration signal. The title’s idea, “Attend to Your Own Thoughts,” points to calibration on chain-of-thought traces or reasoning-like internal distributions, so the quantizer sees the activation and sensitivity patterns that appear during multi-step math and code generation. The supplied text does not expose the full algorithm, so the exact parameterization of learned scales, the reconstruction or language-modeling objective, and whether any lightweight backpropagation is used should be verified in the method section.","experiments":"The visible experimental scope covers 1.58-bit ternary PTQ for reasoning LLMs, with math and coding tasks as central evaluations. The introduction notes that existing PTQ methods can already degrade sharply around 3-bit on reasoning workloads, while QAT requires large token budgets, long training, and careful stability tuning.
