Existing methods primarily improve which original KV pairs are retained. We introduce RestoreKV, which complements this selection-based formulation with learned restoration under the same total KV budget. Our key insight is that, although the information lost through eviction is context-specific, the mechanism for generating its","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-02T13:58:42Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_01247.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是长上下文推理里的一个很具体但很实际的问题：在 query-agnostic KV eviction 设定下，系统需要在不知道未来用户查询的前提下，先把整段上下文压缩成一个可复用的 KV cache。现有方法几乎都把问题表述成“在固定预算下保留哪些原始 KV 对”，也就是纯选择式压缩。论文指出，这个表述在高压缩率下会失效，因为很多对未来回答有用的信息并不适合靠少量原始 token 直接保留下来。真正缺的是一种预算内的“补偿项”：在 eviction 之前，利用完整上下文生成少量、上下文相关的 restore KV，用来弥补被删掉的信息。","importance":"对基座研究的直接意义不在预训练本身，而在长上下文 serving 与模型记忆表征之间的接口。它提出了一个清楚的判断：激进 KV 压缩的瓶颈不只是 importance scoring 不够准，也可能是“只允许保留原始状态”这个约束太强。RestoreKV 把问题从 subset selection 扩展成 retained cache + learned complement，这个思路对后续做 prefix compression、memory token、context distillation、甚至训练期显式学习可压缩中间状态都有启发。量级上，它不是改写 Transformer 主干的工作，但在高压缩区间给出的恢复幅度很大，说明 cache 里确实存在可被小容量补码近似恢复的结构信息。","technical_approach":"$19","experiments":"实验覆盖面比较完整：4 个模型骨干、4 个长上下文 benchmark、5 种 base eviction 方法。论文强调的是一致性而不是只挑单一方法做最优结果。最醒目的数字来自 Qwen3-4B + KVzip：在 5% KV budget 下，RULER-4K 准确率从 38.2 提升到 73.2，恢复幅度很大。应用到 KVzip+ 时，在 KVPress Benchmark 上 16× compression 仍可达到 86.4 的 RULER accuracy。作者还展示了随压缩率变化的曲线：在轻度压缩时，base eviction 与 full-cache 差距本来就不大，RestoreKV 增益有限；压缩越激进，selection-only 方法掉得越快，RestoreKV 越能缩小 gap，这和方法动机是对齐的。\n\n开销方面，32K context 下，8 个 restore token 带来的额外压缩时间只有 0.03 到 0.04 秒，不到总 compression time 的 0.5%；峰值显存增加 84MB，约 0.4%。这点很关键，因为很多 cache reconstruction 方法的问题不是效果，而是把 query-time 或 decode-time 路径改复杂了。RestoreKV 把额外成本限制在一次性 cache construction 阶段，因此更像工程上可插拔的增强模块。\n\n从消融看，论文还验证了几个判断：一是 restore cache 的收益在不同 base evictor 上都存在，说明它不是绑定某个 importance metric 的技巧；二是预算分配需要平衡，restore cache 占比过小补偿不足，过大又会挤占 retained original KV；三是 attention projection 的 LoRA 已经覆盖大部分收益，提示未来若要进一步压缩训练成本，可以优先保留这一部分适配。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看 Figure 1 和 Figure 2，理解作者到底改了什么：不是改 eviction score，而是在 eviction 前多做一次“生成补码”的 pass。然后看方法部分里 restore cache 的构造流程，以及训练目标如何用 full-cache teacher 做蒸馏。真正值得停下来看的，是组件消融和 budget ablation。那几组结果基本回答了三个核心问题：收益来自哪里、为什么不是 restore token 在硬记忆、以及 restore/retain 预算怎么分。\n\n如果只花 20 到 30 分钟，建议顺序是：Introduction 的问题重述、方法图、主结果表、component ablation、overhead 表。读的时候重点想两件事。第一，这个方法为什么能在不改 query-time 路径的情况下恢复行为；第二，它是否意味着 KV cache 里存在一种低秩或低容量的“补充表示”，可以由少量 learned slots 近似承载。后者对做训练期 memory token 或 context bottleneck 设计的人更有启发。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a practical failure mode in long-context inference. In query-agnostic KV eviction, a long prefix must be compressed once, before future user queries are known, into a reusable KV cache. Existing methods mostly treat this as a pure selection problem: under a fixed budget, keep the most important original KV pairs. The paper argues that this formulation breaks down under aggressive compression. The issue is not only imperfect importance scoring; it is also the restriction that the compressed cache can only contain retained original states. What is missing is a small, context-conditioned complement that can be generated before eviction and used to recover behavior lost by dropping most of the prefix.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper, but it matters for foundation-model research because it exposes a representational bottleneck at the interface between long-context modeling and serving. The key message is that high-ratio KV compression should not be framed only as better subset selection. A compressed cache may need both preserved original states and a learned residual memory. That idea is relevant beyond serving: it connects to prefix compression, memory tokens, context distillation, and training-time designs that encourage compressible intermediate states. The empirical gains at very small KV budgets suggest that a nontrivial fraction of useful prefix information can indeed be reconstructed from a tiny learned complement.","technical_approach":"$1a","experiments":"$1b","reading_guide":"$1c","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"KV cache优化的新思路，从「选保留」转向「选保留+轻量恢复」，看§3方法和§4实验即可","en":"Novel KV cache optimization combining eviction with learned lightweight restoration; read §3 method and §4 experiments only"},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Changwoo Baek","Seungjun Shin","Kyeongbo Kong"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["KV cache","inference efficiency","LoRA","self-distillation","query-agnostic eviction"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01247","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.01247","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.01631","title":"Does Accuracy Equal Evidence? Reasoning Faithfulness under KV Cache Compression","abstract":"KV cache compression is commonly evaluated by final-answer accuracy, implicitly assuming that preserving the answer also preserves the reasoning that supports it. We test this assumption for large reasoning models and show that it can fail: under compression, correct answers and the validity of their visible supporting rationales can be preserved at different rates. We study this failure with a controlled fixed-trace replay protocol, which holds reasoning content fixed and isolates whether compr","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-03T03:03:53Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_01631.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的是一个常被忽略的评测错位：KV cache compression 通常只看最终答案准确率，但对 large reasoning models 来说，答案保住了，不等于支撑答案的推理证据也保住了。作者把这个现象定义为 answer–evidence gap，并试图回答两个问题：压缩后的 KV 表示是否同等程度地保留了“能把答案说对”的信息和“能让推理链条成立”的信息；如果没有，仅看 accuracy 会在多大程度上高估压缩方法的质量。","importance":"它对预训练本身不是直接方法贡献，更接近推理阶段的诊断研究，但对基座研究员仍有启发。原因有两点。第一，很多人把长链推理模型的中间 token 当作可解释证据来使用，而这篇文章说明，推理系统层面的压缩会改变“答案正确”和“证据仍然有效”之间的关系，accuracy 不是充分指标。第二，它给出了一个比较干净的组件级评测协议，可以把 rollout dynamics 和 KV 保留能力拆开。这对研究长上下文、推理 trace、serving 优化与模型行为之间的接口很有价值。它不会直接告诉你如何训练更好的模型，但会提醒你：如果训练或部署阶段引入 cache 压缩，仅凭最终分数判断是否“几乎无损”可能是危险的，尤其是在 reasoning trace 被拿来做 verifier、审计或蒸馏信号时。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 controlled fixed-trace replay。标准 end-to-end 压缩评测里，压缩会同时影响中间推理内容的生成和后续答案，因此很难分辨到底是“模型没想出来”还是“已经写出来的推理在压缩后不可用了”。作者的做法是先让 Full-KV 模型在无压缩条件下生成完整 reasoning trace，然后把 question 和这段固定的 textual trace 一起 prefill 进去，只压缩 trace 对应的 KV cache，保护 question cache 不动，再从 </think> 之后继续解码答案。这样所有方法看到的文本推理内容完全相同，差异只来自压缩后的 KV 表示是否还能让模型有效利用这些已有推理。\n\n围绕这个协议，作者设计了三类指标。第一是 final-answer accuracy，仍然保留传统指标。第二是 answer–chain consistency，用来测最终答案是否与可见推理链条一致。第三是 perturbation faithfulness，通过对中间推理做受控扰动，观察压缩模型是否仍然对支持性或误导性中间信息表现出合理敏感性。作者强调，这里区分的是行为功能，而不是声称 KV 里存在物理上分离的“答案子空间”和“证据子空间”。\n\n实验覆盖十种 token-eviction 类 KV 压缩方法和一种 quantization 方法，跨三个模型、多个任务和不同压缩预算。任务包括数学推理、科学问答、临床计算和长上下文检索。作者还补充了 end-to-end 结果，说明 fixed-trace replay 发现的模式并不是纯实验室产物，而是在真实生成里也能看到。和已有 KV compression 论文相比，这篇文章的区别不在提出新压缩算法，而在于指出 accuracy-only evaluation 的系统性盲点，并给出更细粒度的诊断框架。","experiments":"从全文给出的结果描述看，最重要的发现不是某个方法赢了多少，而是不同指标之间的分叉。作者在 AIME、GPQA-Diamond、MedCalc 这类 reasoning-heavy 任务上观察到：在一定压缩预算下，若只看 final accuracy，一些方法仍然显得“保持得不错”；但 answer–chain consistency 和 perturbation faithfulness 会更早、更明显地下滑。这说明压缩后的模型可能还能沿着残留的答案线索给出正确结论，却已经不能稳定地利用或维护支撑该结论的推理证据。换句话说，accuracy 在这里是非对称诊断：一旦 accuracy 崩了，当然说明压缩伤害很大；但 accuracy 没崩，并不代表 reasoning state 仍然健康。\n\n另一个关键结果是任务依赖性。RULER QA 这类长上下文检索任务中，答案更直接依赖保留精确证据，因此压缩造成的损伤更容易直接反映到 accuracy collapse 上，answer–evidence gap 相对没那么隐蔽。相反，在数学、科学 QA、临床计算这类任务里，模型可能依靠结论邻近 cue、局部 summary 或其他 answer-like signals 保住最终答案，但推理链条的可审计性已经下降。\n\n作者还比较了多种 token eviction 方法和一种 quantization 方法，并在不同模型家族上重复实验，结论是这种 gap 不是单一算法或单一模型的偶然现象，而是一个较普遍的评测问题。摘要里没有给出完整数字表，但从正文结构看，Table 2 提供了 end-to-end 结果，主文其余表格则展示不同预算、任务和方法下 accuracy、consistency、faithfulness 三轴的分离情况。阅读时应重点关注这些曲线是否在相同 budget 下出现排序翻转。","reading_guide":"泛读时先不要把它当成一篇新的压缩算法论文，而要当成一篇评测协议论文。优先看 Introduction 和 Problem Setting，理解作者为什么要用 fixed-trace replay，以及它和真实部署 benchmark 的边界在哪里。随后看方法部分对三个指标的定义，尤其是 answer–chain consistency 与 perturbation faithfulness 分别想测什么。接着直接看主结果图表：先看不同任务上 accuracy 与 faithfulness 是否同步下降，再看不同压缩预算下是否出现“答案还对，但证据已坏”的区间。最后再扫一遍 end-to-end 结果，确认这种现象不是 replay protocol 人为制造出来的。\n\n如果你做长上下文、推理模型 serving、KV 压缩或 reasoning trace 利用，这篇文章值得花 20–30 分钟读主文结果和方法定义。最值得思考的点有三个：一，部署优化是否改变了可解释性信号的可信度；二，训练时若用压缩环境生成或蒸馏推理 trace，会不会把这种 answer–evidence gap 带回训练分布；三，未来压缩方法是否应该把“证据保真”纳入目标，而不是只保 final token accuracy。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a misalignment in how KV cache compression is usually evaluated. Most work reports final-answer accuracy, implicitly treating preserved answers as evidence that the underlying reasoning state is also preserved. For large reasoning models, the authors show this assumption can fail. They define an answer–evidence gap: compression may retain enough information for the model to output the correct answer while degrading the evidential structure that makes the visible rationale valid, auditable, or robust. The paper asks how often these two functions—answer recovery and evidence preservation—diverge, and whether accuracy-only evaluation systematically overestimates compression quality.","importance":"This is not a direct pretraining-method paper, but it is still relevant to foundation-model research because it sharpens the interface between model behavior and inference-time systems. Two points matter. First, reasoning traces are increasingly used as evidence, supervision, or audit artifacts. If cache compression changes the relationship between answer correctness and rationale validity, then accuracy alone is an unsafe proxy for deployment quality. Second, the paper offers a relatively clean component-level diagnostic that separates rollout dynamics from KV retention. That is useful for anyone working on long-context models, reasoning traces, serving optimizations, or post-training pipelines that consume model-generated chains of thought. It does not tell you how to train a better model directly, but it does show that “near-lossless compression” can be a misleading claim if judged only by final accuracy.","technical_approach":"$1d","experiments":"$1e","reading_guide":"$1f","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"做 KV 压缩或长上下文推理的人应立刻把这三个指标加进内部 ablation，只用准确率会严重高估压缩质量。","en":"Anyone working on KV compression or long-context inference should add these three metrics to internal ablations; accuracy alone severely overestimates compression quality."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Mengting Ai","Jingrui He","Yue Guo"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["KV-cache","long-context","reasoning-faithfulness","compression","evaluation"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01631","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.01631","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.02123","title":"From Chains to Trees: Parent-Conditioned Drafting for Semi-Autoregressive Speculative Decoding","abstract":"Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference only when drafted continuations survive target-model verification. Naive parent-independent tree branching generates conditionally incoherent candidate paths and wastes verification budget.","importance":"A training-free inference optimization for speculative decoding that improves end-to-end speed over existing DSpark implementations without retraining the drafter or adding extra backbone forward passes, with direct engineering value for deploying semi-autoregressive speculative decoding systems.","technical_approach":"The key observation is that DSpark's pretrained first-order Markov head natively supports parent-conditional rescoring with no additional training. PCTree reuses shared position logits from a single backbone forward pass, runs the pretrained Markov head to compute child token distributions separately for each concrete parent node in the draft tree, and constructs a candidate tree under a fixed verification node budget to avoid incoherent paths generated by parent-agnostic scoring. i","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-02T14:24:13Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_01271.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是 Video-LLM 中视觉 token 过长的问题，但切入点不是再做一个在线剪枝或合并器，而是把“如何分组压缩视觉 token”的知识从单个视频中抽出来，预先学习成一个可复用的全局 codebook。核心问题是：视频中大量局部视觉模式会跨样本重复，是否可以把这种重复性离线建模，并在推理时用很低的额外成本完成压缩。","importance":"对基座预训练研究的直接影响有限，更偏向 VLM 推理效率和视觉 token 表示压缩。但它有一个有用的信号：视觉 token 压缩不一定要绑定下游 Video-LLM 的 attention 或重要性估计，也不一定要在每个输入上重新计算压缩结构。对于研究视觉 tokenizer、视频 token 预算、native multimodal pretrain 的人，这篇论文提供了一个可插拔的“全局视觉分组先验”思路。它不是重新训练视觉编码器，也不是学习离散视觉词表，而是在冻结特征空间中学习聚类先验，再用连续 embedding 的均值池化保持与现有 Video-LLM 的接口兼容。","technical_approach":"ONCE 的方法分成离线和在线两段。离线阶段先在冻结视觉特征空间中构造语料级 sketch。每个视频按时间轴分成 L 个 bin，避免只采到局部时间片段；sketch 数量按 m = min{N, clip(ceil(N/R), m_min, m_max)} 控制，其中 N 是源 token 数，R 是目标压缩比例。每个时间 bin 内使用 spherical Lloyd 聚类，得到归一化代表向量，并记录它代表的源 token 数量。随后把所有视频的代表向量汇总成带权 sketch 集合。为了避免常见场景和高频视觉模式占用过多 codeword，论文引入 balanced prototype fitting，并用源 token 数和类别频率修正形成 effective weight；没有类别标签时可退化为不做长尾校正。在线阶段给定一个视频，先用全局 codebook 找出当前视频支持度最高的 codeword，受预算 B 约束选择一组 codeword；然后把原始视觉 token 重新分配到这些 codeword，并对原始连续 embedding 做均值池化。关键点是输出仍然是连续视觉 token，而不是离散 code index，因此不需要改 Video-LLM 参数，也不需要针对每个输入做优化。","experiments":"论文在 4 个视频理解 benchmark 和 2 个 Video-LLM backbone 上评估 相似度估计和重要性估计改成 corpus-level grouping prior，而不是来自更复杂的在线压缩网络。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 15–20 分钟。重点看 Method 中的两部分：temporal corpus sketching 和 balanced prototype fitting；它们决定了 codebook 是否能覆盖长视频中的时间多样性和低频视觉模式。然后看在线阶段的 budget-aware selection、token reassignment 和 mean pooling，确认它为什么能保持与冻结 Video-LLM 兼容。实验部分优先看 accuracy–efficiency 曲线、不同 token budget 下的结果，以及跨 backbone 的迁移实验。对于预训练研究，最值得深挖的是：这种全局 codebook 是否可以前移到视觉 tokenizer 或多模态预训练数据构建阶段，而不是只作为推理压缩模块。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses the long visual-token sequence problem in Video-LLMs. Its main question is whether video-token compression has to be recomputed online for every input, or whether recurring visual patterns across videos can be captured once as a reusable global grouping prior.","importance":"The work is more relevant to multimodal inference efficiency than to language-model pretraining itself. Still, it is useful for researchers working on visual tokenization and native multimodal pretraining. The key idea is to decouple compression from model-specific attention scores or online token merging. ONCE learns a global codebook in a frozen visual feature space and uses it only as a grouping guide; the compressed outputs remain continuous visual embeddings. This makes the method compatible with existing Video-LLMs without changing their parameters.","technical_approach":"ONCE has an offline-to-online design. Offline, it builds a compact corpus sketch from frozen visual features. Each video is temporally stratified into L bins, and the number of representatives is controlled by m = min{N, clip(ceil(N/R), m_min, m_max)}. Within each temporal bin, spherical Lloyd clustering is applied to normalized token features. Each representative stores both its visual vector and the number of source tokens it summarizes. The corpus-level codebook is then fitted with balanced assignment, using source-token mass and optional category-frequency correction to reduce over-allocation to dominant visual patterns. Online, ONCE selects the codewords best supported by the current video under a token budget B, assigns dense visual tokens to the selected codewords using cosine similarity, and mean-pools the original continuous embeddings. The codebook provides grouping structure, but the downstream Video-LLM still receives continuous visual tokens rather than discrete indices.","experiments":"The paper evaluates ONCE on four video-understanding benchmarks and two Video-LLM backbones, comparing it with input-adaptive pruning and merging baselines. The reported pattern is that ONCE gives a better accuracy–efficiency trade-off, with its clearest gains under tight token budgets, while reducing inference overhead because most grouping knowledge is learned offline. The provided text does not include the full numerical tables, so the safest interpretation is methodological: the advantage comes from reusing a corpus-level grouping prior instead of recomputing compression structure from scratch for every video.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 15–20 minutes. We argue this abstraction is mis-specified. The same attention score simultaneously decides two things: how much retained capacity each modality receives, and which tokens within a modality are kept. A shared top-K rule therefore inherits this audio-favoring allocation prior, spending retained capacity on audio before video tokens have a chance to compete. We propos","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-03T03:56:33Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_01665.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.SD"],"summary":{"core_problem":"现有共享解码器OmniLLM的token压缩普遍采用全局top-K显著性排序，将跨模态容量分配、模态内token选择两个独立决策耦合在同一个注意力分数中。共享注意力本身存在稳定的音频偏好先验，会隐式挤占视频token的保留配额，导致压缩后的精度与效率平衡不佳。","importance":"它揭示了共享解码器OmniLLM的注意力存在结构性跨模态分配偏差——这一偏差来自架构设计本身，而非训练数据或策略。对统一多模态预训练的架构设计、模态容量平衡机制，以及推理侧的token压缩优化都有参考价值。无需训练的特性也使其可直接用于现有OmniLLM的部署优化。","technical_approach":"Macer是一种无需训练的推理侧token压缩方法，核心遵循「先分配，后排序」的原则：\n1. 先显式为音频和视频分配各自的保留配额，不再由原始共享注意力的质量隐式决定容量分配；\n2. 在每个模态内部，用模态归一化后的注意力分数排序选token，不做跨模态混排；\n3. 由于音频显著性在解码器浅层更早稳定，视频显著性稍晚才稳定，因此在模态特定的浅层读取注意力分数，无需等到深层。\n此外，在视频配额内加入了一个轻量的时间覆盖约束，避免保留的视频token在时间上过度集中。\n整个方法不需要训练、不需要修改模型，也不需要额外前向传播。","experiments":"在三个共享解码器OmniLLM骨干（Qwen2.5-Omni-7B、Qwen2.5-Omni-3B、OmniVinci-9B）上验证，覆盖音频grounding、音视频联合、视觉主导、视频中心四类任务。25% token保留率下，Qwen2.5-Omni-7B可保留全token性能的98.7%，对比基线OmniZip的91.6%；3B版本可保留97.3%，OmniZip为95.5%。在OmniVinci-9B上，相比共享top-K排序最高提升12.9个点。所有测试保留率下的综合性能，均优于对比的token剪枝基线。","reading_guide":"泛读优先看Introduction部分的三个核心观察，这是方法的核心动机，比方法本身更具启发性。然后看方法部分中注意力分解为跨模态分配项和模态内残差项的数学推导，理解全局top-K排序天然存在耦合问题的原因。实验部分只需看主结果表和跨模型迁移性结果即可。预计阅读时间15-20分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Existing token compression methods for shared-decoder omnimodal LLMs (OmniLLMs) frame compression as a single global top-K saliency ranking problem, coupling two distinct decisions — cross-modal capacity allocation and within-modality token selection — into one attention score. Shared attention has a persistent audio-favoring prior that implicitly consumes most of the retention budget before video tokens can compete, leading to a poor accuracy-efficiency tradeoff under compression.","importance":"It identifies a structural cross-modal allocation bias in shared-decoder OmniLLM attention that arises from architecture design, not training data or strategies. The training-free property makes it directly applicable to existing OmniLLM deployments.","technical_approach":"Macer is a training-free inference-time token compressor built on the allocation-before-selection principle:\n1. It first explicitly assigns separate retention budgets for audio and video tokens, removing implicit budget allocation from raw shared attention mass.\n2. It ranks tokens only within each modality using modality-normalized attention scores, instead of cross-modal mixed ranking.\n3. It reads saliency scores at modality-specific shallow layers, since audio saliency stabilizes earlier than video saliency in the decoder.\nA lightweight temporal coverage term is applied only within the video budget to avoid temporal over-concentration of retained video tokens.\nThe method requires no training, no model modification, and no extra forward pass.","experiments":"Evaluated on three shared-decoder OmniLLM backbones (Qwen2.5-Omni-7B, Qwen2.5-Omni-3B, OmniVinci-9B) across four task categories: audio-grounded, audio-video joint, visual-dominant, and video-centric. This has implications for agent evaluation, tool-use trace collection, and training formats that make future actions and executable states easier to expose.","technical_approach":"AOSpec jointly speculates actions and observations while preserving serial semantics. Existing RAG, summarization, and retrieval pipelines provide on-demand access to stored historical content, but fail to maintain a fixed-capacity, online-updated internal latent state that persists across working context swaps, breaking computational continuity when old context is evicted.","importance":"This work draws a clear distinction between two orthogonal memory functions in long-running interaction: on-demand historical access implemented by RAG-style systems, and cross-context computational continuity implemented by recurrent latent states, resolving conceptual ambiguity in prior memory research. Its proposed post-training protocol to prevent memory bypass offers practical engineering reference for long-horizon agent systems. However, the approach is a plug-in branch added to fully pretrained attention LLMs, with no modification to pretraining objectives or backbone architecture, so its direct impact on foundation model pretraining paradigms is limited.","technical_approach":"Architecturally, LiveMem adds a parallel recurrent memory branch built on Gated DeltaNet-2 alongside a pretrained full-attention decoder, maintaining a fixed-size persistent memory state across the entire interaction lifecycle. The main attention path operates on a bounded sliding KV window; when the window reaches capacity, the oldest KV chunks are directly evicted from the attention path, and relevant information is integrated into the persistent state via write operations in the memory branch. For training, a dedicated memory-oriented post-training task enforces forced context turnover, ensuring the model can only retrieve evicted historical information through the memory state, rather than learning to bypass the memory branch when full history remains visible during training. At inference, the memory state is updated continuously across the full lifecycle, fully decoupled from working context turnover.","experiments":"On LongMemEval and related long-range memory benchmarks, LiveMem retains significantly higher accuracy than sliding-window baselines even when supporting evidence is fully evicted from the active context window, with performance degrading smoothly as the distance between evidence and current window increases, rather than dropping off sharply. Overall long-task performance is competitive with top augmented memory systems, though the method underperforms explicit retrieval on tasks requiring exact verbatim recall. Short-context performance matches the base model exactly, with no accuracy penalty from the added memory branch. Ablations confirm the core design premise: without forced context turnover in post-training, the model learns to ignore the memory branch entirely, performing on par with vanilla sliding-window attention.","reading_guide":"Skim for core framing and design logic. However, existing parallel drafting backends often suffer from rapid accuracy degradation over long horizons, leading to high rejection rates during verification and suboptimal wall-clock speedups. We observe that drafting errors are not uniformly distributed but typically stem from localized high-uncertainty tokens that des","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-01T08:43:34Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_00531.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是 block-parallel speculative decoding 的一个具体痛点：并行 draft 虽然便宜，但一旦某个位置出错，后面整段候选都会因为前缀校验而被连带拒绝，形成 error propagation cascade。作者的观察是，错误并不是均匀分布的，往往集中在少数高不确定 token 上。如果能只在这些局部位置做修复，而不是对整段做密集树扩展，就有机会在不把 verifier 成本推高太多的前提下，提高接受长度和端到端吞吐。","importance":"这是推理效率方向的工作，不属于预训练核心，但对大模型系统研究有现实意义。它的价值不在提出全新的 speculative decoding 框架，而在把 block-parallel backend 的结构性弱点讲清楚，并给出一个训练无关、可插拔的修补机制。对基座研究员来说，启发主要有两点：一是 speculative decoding 的收益不只取决于 draft 模型整体精度，还取决于错误空间分布；二是 acceptance length 与 verification cost 之间需要做硬件感知的折中，不能只看理论接受率。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 CURE，一个 budget-aware dynamic repair tree。输入是 block-parallel draft 一次性生成的长度 K 候选块及其 logits。标准单路径 speculative decoding 只验证这条主路径，一旦第 k 个 token 被 target 判错，k 之后的 token 即使本身合理也会被整体丢弃。CURE 的做法是先找出最可能引发级联拒绝的局部脆弱点，再只在这些点上分叉。\n\n具体来说，作者用 top-1 与 top-2 log-prob margin 作为置信度指标，定位 Uncertainty Focal Points。margin 小意味着 draft 在该位置犹豫，局部错误概率高。然后在给定 verifier budget 下，只对这些位置生成替代分支，而不是对所有位置均匀展开候选树。这样做的理由很直接：如果大部分 token 都高度可预测，全面树扩展会把 target verification 的 token 数和显存占用迅速推高，浪费在本来就不会错的区域。\n\n论文把这个过程形式化为一个受预算约束的 candidate-tree optimization：目标是在总验证预算 V_max 下最大化可接受长度。效率指标则写成期望接受 token 数除以 draft 成本加 verifier 成本。这个表述虽然不复杂，但把 speculative decoding 的关键权衡写得比较清楚：只有当额外分支带来的 accepted length 增益超过 target tree verification 的额外代价时，墙钟时间才会真正改善。\n\n另一个实用点是 cache-resynchronization。因为 repair branch 可能改变被接受的路径，draft model 的内部状态需要与 target 最终接受的前缀重新对齐。作者设计了一个无需重训 draft 的 cache 同步机制，使 CURE 可以作为 plug-and-play 模块接在 block-parallel backend 上。这一点比单纯提出一个更复杂的树策略更重要，因为很多 speculative decoding 方法卡在工程接入成本高、需要专门训练 draft backend。","experiments":"实验覆盖代码生成主场景和一个 out-of-domain 推理任务。代码基准包括 HumanEval、MBPP、LiveCodeBench-lite，外域测试是 GSM8K。作者报告，相比单路径并行 draft backend，CURE 能把每步 accepted tokens 提高 4.2% 到 7.5%，相对 target-only 自回归解码实现 2.66× 到 3.49× 的端到端加速。\n\n这些数字的含义要分开看。accepted tokens per step 的提升幅度并不夸张，说明 CURE 不是通过大规模树搜索硬拉接受率，而是在局部修补上做了较克制的改进；但由于 speculative decoding 的瓶颈常常是 memory-bound 的 sequential target pass，哪怕接受长度有中等提升，也可能转化成较明显的墙钟收益。作者还强调 acceptance–compute Pareto trade-off：repair 分支越多，接受长度通常越长，但 target verification 也会逐渐饱和，超过某个点后不再带来净加速。\n\n从全文给出的信息看，论文的实验重点不是模型质量变化，而是系统层面的吞吐与接受率权衡。因此读结果时应重点看不同 budget、不同 block setting 下的曲线，而不是只盯住单个 speedup 数字。另一个值得注意的点是，方法在代码任务上更自然，因为代码 token 分布往往“整体结构稳定、局部变量和 API 选择不稳定”，正好符合 UFP 假设；GSM8K 的结果更多是在说明方法并非完全依赖代码域。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，30 分钟左右可以抓住主要内容。先看 Introduction，重点理解作者提出的 error propagation cascade 和 Uncertainty Focal Points 这两个概念。然后直接看 Methodology 里关于预算约束优化的定义，弄清楚它不是一般 tree speculative decoding，而是局部、受预算限制的 repair。接着看 cache-resynchronization 部分，这是方法能否落地的关键工程点。\n\n实验部分建议重点看三类图表：一是 accepted tokens per step 的提升；二是 end-to-end speedup；三是 acceptance 与 verification cost 的 Pareto 曲线。若时间有限，可以跳过过多实现细节，只保留一个判断：CURE 何时比单路径 block-parallel 更划算，何时额外分支已经不值。\n\n如果你做的是 serving 或推理系统，这篇的可读性会高于纯算法论文；如果你主要做预训练，它更像一个了解推理侧约束如何反过来影响模型设计的参考材料。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a specific weakness of block-parallel speculative decoding. Parallel drafting is cheap, but once an early token is wrong, standard prefix verification rejects the entire remaining block, creating an error-propagation cascade. The authors observe that these errors are not uniformly spread across positions. They tend to concentrate around a few high-uncertainty tokens. If repair is applied only at those local failure points, rather than through dense tree expansion everywhere, accepted length can improve without exploding verifier cost.","importance":"This is an inference-efficiency paper rather than a pretraining contribution, but it is practically relevant for large-model systems. Its value is not in inventing a completely new speculative-decoding paradigm. The useful part is a clear diagnosis of block-parallel drafting failure modes and a training-free, plug-in repair mechanism. For foundation-model researchers, two lessons stand out. First, speculative-decoding gains depend not only on average draft accuracy, but also on the spatial distribution of draft errors. Second, accepted-length gains must be evaluated jointly with verification overhead; higher acceptance is not automatically better if target-side tree verification saturates memory or compute.","technical_approach":"$20","experiments":"$21","reading_guide":"$22","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看错误定位和 resynchronization 机制；若实验补全，局部不确定 token 这个诊断可迁移到 draft-model 训练。","en":"Skim the error-localization and resynchronization parts; if the experiments hold, the uncertainty-token diagnosis may inform draft-model training."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Aofan Liu","Jingxiang Meng","Fangxin Liu","Yongbiao Chen"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["speculative-decoding","inference-efficiency","draft-model","uncertainty","serving"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00531","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.00531","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.01847","title":"FOCUS: FP4 Optimization via Coupled-Relaxation and Dual-Granularity Scaling","abstract":"Large language models (LLMs) achieve remarkable performance but are expensive to deploy due to their enormous size. Existing scale-optimization methods tightly couple the quantization scale and dequantization scale into a single discrete, low-precision value at a fixed block size, unnecessarily restricting optimization by hardware format constraints. The paper points out a simple but overlooked fact: the quantization scale is used only during offline calibration and is never stored or deployed, so it need not obey hardware constraints at all.","importance":"This is primarily a serving/PTQ paper, but the core observation is clean and general: separating the offline quantization scale from the deployed dequantization scale unlocks a previously underused degree of freedom in FP4 quantization. The idea of lifting discrete scale optimization into a continuous, learnable space via CRS may also transfer to quantization-aware training or training-time low-precision recipes. The method introduces zero inference overhead and is directly compatible with existing MXFP4/NVFP4 hardware, which makes it practically attractive.","technical_approach":"FOCUS is a scale-learning-only FP4 PTQ framework, avoiding transformation-based methods such as Hadamard rotation that add online compute. It has two components. Coupled-Relaxation Scaling (CRS) inserts a learnable full-precision coefficient between the quantization scale and the hardware-constrained dequantization scale, relaxing optimization from the discrete scale space imposed by E8M0/E4M3 into a continuous space amenable to gradient-based end-to-end learning. Dual-Granularity Scaling (DGS) lets the quantization scale operate at a sub-block granularity finer than the hardware block size, while the dequantization scale stays at the hardware-mandated block size, so local weight variations are captured offline without changing the deployed format. Model weights remain frozen throughout.","experiments":"Evaluated on multiple LLM families under both MXFP4 and NVFP4 W4A4. Quantizing Qwen3-4B takes 19 minutes on a single H20 GPU, with no extra inference operators or overhead and seamless compatibility with existing frameworks. Results on very large models (e.g., 70B-scale) and training-time quantization are not provided, so those regimes remain to be verified.","reading_guide":"Skim-level read. This produces drafts with locally plausible tokens but low joint probability under the target model's left-to-right distribution, leading to early rejection, limited acceptance length, and suboptimal end-to-end throughput for speculative decoding.","importance":"Provides a low-overhead fix for the core limitation of diffusion-based speculative decoding, preserving parallel drafting while improving acceptance rates. It offers direct engineering value for inference efficiency optimization, and a generalizable approach to injecting causality into discrete diffusion models.","technical_approach":"xPress is a lightweight causal refinement framework with two key designs: 1) It keeps the original diffusion drafter's parallel block proposal intact, and adds a low-rank causal refinement head that projects token embeddings and drafter hidden states into a low-rank space, uses a strictly lower-triangular linear mixer to aggregate predecessor information, and outputs per-position additive biases for the drafter's logits. 2) It resolves the causal dependency in refinement via parallel Jacobi iterations: only K ≪ B (B is block size) parallel passes are needed to approximate the fixed point of sequential autoregressive refinement, avoiding reintroducing serial overhead.","experiments":"Evaluated with Qwen3-8B as the target model and dFlash as the base drafter across 7 math, coding, and chat benchmarks. 是否可以通过视觉表示绕开注意力成本。DeepSeek-OCR、Glyph、VTC-R1 这类方法的有效性不只取决于视觉 token 数，还取决于渲染布局在固定 token 预算下保留字符级信息的能力。该工作把评测目标从“下游任务是否还能答对”改为“文本是否可恢复”，指出高任务分数可能来自语义补全，而不是压缩表示真的保真。这对长上下文预训练、跨模态统一建模、视觉 tokenizer 设计都有实际影响：如果评测不隔离语义先验，就会高估视觉压缩路线的可用上下文容量。","technical_approach":"技术方案分两层。第一层是评测框架。它把 VTC 拆成两个正交目标：压缩效率和上下文保真。压缩效率可以由 text token 数与 visual token 数的比例直接计算；保真度则用 complete-text OCR 近似，即要求模型从渲染图像中恢复完整文本，而不是只回答一个下游问题。这样做的原因很明确：下游任务只需要局部证据，模型可能凭语言常识或少量线索给出正确答案；完整 OCR 对缺字、错字和顺序错误更敏感。框架还固定一组 MLLM 作为评估器，并在完整 benchmark 上取最佳模型级结果，以减少某个评估模型能力不足带来的低估。\n\n第二层是 ZeroSense benchmark。它合成低语义相关、低可预测性的长文本样本，尽量去掉自然语言结构。这样模型无法依赖词法、语法、常识和上下文关联来补全模糊字符。源文本独立于图像表示，因此同一批内容可以用 Fox、Omni、Glyph 等不同渲染风格和不同压缩率生成图像，便于比较 rendering function 本身。这个设计和传统自然文本 OCR 的区别在于，后者评到的是“视觉识别 + 语言纠错”的联合效果；ZeroSense 更接近测量视觉压缩链路的纯保真能力。","experiments":"实验评估了三类 VTC rendering function，并覆盖多个压缩率。关键发现是，语义隔离后的文本保真率显著低于传统端到端 OCR 或下游任务指标给出的印象。最有代表性的数字是 FOX layout 在 10× 压缩率下，DeepSeek-OCR 报告的端到端 OCR accuracy 为 95.4%，而该框架估计的文本保留率只有 11.8%。这个差距说明，常规评测中的高分很可能来自模型对自然文本的补全能力，而不是渲染图像真的保存了大部分源文本。实验还显示，随着压缩率升高，不同 layout 的退化速度不同；字体大小、行距、文本密度、图像分辨率和视觉 token 数之间存在明显取舍。","reading_guide":"建议精读 Introduction 和 Evaluation Framework。前者把 VTC 评测的混杂因素讲得很清楚：语义先验、任务证据需求、评估模型识别能力都会影响结果。后者给出压缩效率与上下文保真的形式化拆分，是整篇工作的核心。ZeroSense 构造部分需要重点看样本如何降低语义可预测性，以及为什么同一源文本能公平比较不同 rendering。实验部分优先看不同 layout、压缩率和评估协议之间的对照，尤其是 DeepSeek-OCR 常规结果与 ZeroSense 保真估计之间的差距。值得深挖的问题有两个：第一，complete-text OCR 是否过于严格，和真实长上下文任务之间如何建立可解释映射；第二，取最佳 MLLM 结果能减少低估，但也可能引入评估器选择偏差，需要看模型集合和聚合规则是否稳定。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks how to faithfully evaluate visual-text compression, where long text is rendered into document images and consumed as a smaller number of visual tokens. Existing evaluations based on natural-text OCR or downstream tasks mix rendering quality with MLLM language priors and task-specific inference, so they do not directly measure how much source text remains recoverable from the compressed visual representation.","importance":"This is a must-read for researchers interested in long-context scaling and multimodal pretraining interfaces. VTC methods such as DeepSeek-OCR, Glyph, and VTC-R1 are attractive because they may bypass part of the text-token attention cost. Their real capacity, however, depends on whether the rendered image preserves textual information under a fixed visual-token budget. The paper shows that downstream accuracy can substantially overestimate preservation because the evaluator can infer missing content from natural-language structure. This matters for long-context pretraining, unified multimodal modeling, and visual tokenizer design: without semantic-isolated evaluation, the effective context capacity of VTC systems can be misread.","technical_approach":"$23","experiments":"The experiments compare three VTC rendering functions across multiple compression ratios. The central empirical result is a large gap between conventional end-to-end metrics and semantic-isolated text preservation. Under the FOX layout at a 10× compression ratio, DeepSeek-OCR reports 95.4% end-to-end OCR accuracy, while the proposed framework estimates only 11.8% text preservation. This indicates that high OCR or downstream accuracy on natural text can come from semantic reconstruction rather than faithful visual preservation. The results also show that degradation with increasing compression differs across layouts, reflecting trade-offs among font size, spacing, text density, image resolution, and visual-token budget.","reading_guide":"Read the Introduction and Evaluation Framework closely. The Introduction cleanly identifies the confounders in VTC evaluation: language priors, downstream evidence requirements, and evaluator capability. The Evaluation Framework is the key technical part because it formalizes compression efficiency and preservation as separate quantities. The ZeroSense construction is worth reading to understand how semantic predictability is suppressed and why the same source text can fairly compare different rendering functions. In the experiments, focus on the comparisons across layouts, compression ratios, and evaluation protocols, especially the gap between DeepSeek-OCR’s conventional result and the ZeroSense preservation estimate. Two points deserve further scrutiny: whether complete-text OCR is too strict for some downstream uses, and whether taking the best MLLM result introduces evaluator-selection bias.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看评测设计和失败案例即可；它会修正“下游准确率可代表文本压缩保真度”的默认假设。","en":"Read the evaluation setup and failure cases; it weakens the default assumption that downstream accuracy measures text-compression fidelity."},"metadata":{"institutions":["DeepSeek"],"authors":["Yonghan Gao","Zehong Chen","Lijian Xu","Jingzhi Chen","Jingwei Guan","Xingyu Zeng"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["visual-text-compression","evaluation","long-context","MLLM","DeepSeek-OCR"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01848","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.01848","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.00004","title":"Cost-Effective Automated Judging of Natural-Language Mathematical Proofs","abstract":"Grading natural-language mathematical proofs is a recurring cost in evaluating math-reasoning systems, and frontier LLM judges are expensive. We present AgentMemBench, a unified, reproducible benchmark evaluating five memory management strategies under identical conditions: in-context windowing (ICW), external key-value store (EKV), graph-based episodic memory (GEM), compression-based summarisation (CBS), and web-augmented memory (WAM). All are assessed across three public ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-16T08:27:18Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_00009.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是 graph-based episodic memory，从对话中抽取实体和关系，维护结构化记忆图；CBS，即 compression-based summarisation，用自然语言摘要压缩旧上下文；WAM，即 web-augmented memory，用 Web search 或 MCP 工具补充外部信息。评测覆盖三个公开数据集：LoCoMo 200 个问题 turn，MultiDoc2Dial 187 个问题 turn，MSC 104 个问题 turn，总计 491 个标注问题 turn，形成 5 种策略 × 3 类任务的 15 个配置。指标包括 Memory Recall@k、MRR、nDCG@k、Answer F1、LLM-judge Faithfulness，以及 token cost、latency、memory footprint precision-over-recall，能提供结构化关联，但在信息抽取不完整或关系稀疏时会漏掉答案所需事实；WAM 更适合需要外部知识补充的场景，但它不等价于内部长期记忆，并引入额外延迟和工具调用成本。MADS 被定位为部署启发式，而不是保证最优的选择器；论文也分析了它与经验最优策略不一致的场景。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 25–35 分钟。先看五类 memory 部分适合快速浏览，主要价值在于把任务统计量和记忆策略选择联系起来；如果研究 long-context pretrain 或 agent memory training，可以重点关注哪些失败来自模型能力，哪些失败来自记忆写入和检索机制。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses long-term memory evaluation for conversational agents. The question is how different memory-management strategies behave when an agent must recall information across many sessions while operating under a finite context window.","importance":"This is an agent-memory benchmark rather than a pretraining contribution. Its relevance to foundation-model research is indirect but useful: it separates model context length from external memory design, and it evaluates not only answer quality but also retrieval quality, faithfulness, memory footprint, token cost, and latency. That matters for long-context training and agent post-training because a memory strategy that improves final-answer F1 may still be unattractive if it inflates context, retrieval latency, or tool-call cost.","technical_approach":"$26","experiments":"The benchmark evaluates 491 annotated question turns across three datasets and five core memory strategies, with additional comparisons to published memory systems. The reported qualitative pattern is consistent with the expected failure modes of each design. ICW fails on questions whose evidence has fallen outside the recent window. EKV and CBS are stronger for long-range factual recall: EKV depends on semantic retrieval quality, while CBS depends on whether summaries preserve the needed details. GEM tends to be more selective, giving higher-precision structured recall but missing facts when extraction is incomplete or the graph neighborhood is sparse. WAM can help when external knowledge is needed, but it should not be treated as a replacement for internal conversational memory because it adds tool latency and cost. MADS is presented as a deployment heuristic rather than an optimal policy, and the paper explicitly examines cases where its selected strategy diverges from the empirically best one.","reading_guide":"A 25–35 minute skim is appropriate. Start with the definitions of the five memory strategies and check how memory is written, retrieved, and injected into the model context. Then read the provenance-aware metric design, since that is the cleanest methodological contribution. In the results, focus on quality-cost trade-offs across datasets rather than only final Answer F1. The MADS section is useful if the goal is to design adaptive memory policies. These summaries capture the overall performance, but they do not test whether progress is distributed differently across task difficulty. We find that most of the apparent shift in gains toward harder tasks does not reflect a change in the shape of the difficulty-response curve. wit","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-31T23:58:25Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_00355.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"很多“模型进步”被压成一个标量（平均分、时间跨度、潜在能力值），但这会混淆两件事：整体能力的均匀上移（level shift）与对高难题的相对额外增益（shape change）。论文要回答的是：近一年的“更偏向攻克难题”的进步叙事，究竟是能力曲线形状真的变了，还是只是天花板效应/题目区分度导致的表观现象？","importance":"对预训练研究的直接启发不在于训练方法，而在于“如何读懂评测曲线”。它提醒：当 easy/medium 接近饱和时，任何整体能力提升都会在 hard 档表现为更大的绝对增益，从而制造“难题进步更快”的错觉；这类错觉会反过来影响对 scaling、数据配比、推理训练是否改变能力结构的判断。论文还指出 agentic 评测里模型迭代与 harness 迭代强共线，导致很多关于“能力曲线形状变化”的结论在统计上不可识别；这对做持续预训练/中期训练的人很关键，因为你可能把 harness 改进当成了训练配方收益。","technical_approach":"方法核心是用 IRT（Item Response Theory）把“水平”与“形状”拆开。\n1）标量零假设：用 Rasch（1PL）模型拟合随时间上升的单一 latent ability，并假设题目难度固定。Rasch 下，模型在不同难度题上的提升主要由 ability 上移解释。\n2）形状检验：用 DIF（differential item functioning）思想做“时代分组”的题目功能漂移检测，关注的是在控制总体 ability 后，hard items 是否出现额外正偏移（论文把它表述为集中在 hardest items 的 group-by-difficulty shift，而不是 group-by-ability 斜率交互）。\n3）识别策略：作者强调在 METR time-horizon、SWE-bench 等 agentic 轨迹评测中，模型发布时间与 harness 发布时间几乎共线，DIF 估计会把模型能力与 harness 改进混在一起，无法仅靠这些数据分离。为避免该混淆，选择 LiveCodeBench：无 agent harness、直接代码生成；并且题目来自人类竞赛，难度排序外生。\n4）2PL 不稳定与敏感性分析：自由的 2PL（允许 item discrimination 变化）在该数据上不稳定识别，因此用“anchored sensitivity analysis”估计 hard-item DIF：在保守假设下（hard items 的区分度不高于 easy/medium）仍能得到正的 hard-item 残差效应。","experiments":"全文给出的关键结论是定性为主：\n- 在 METR time-horizon 数据上，表面上“进步更集中在难任务”的现象，大部分可由单一 Rasch ability 上升复现，说明主要是天花板/区分度效应，而非能力曲线形状变化。\n- 在 LiveCodeBench 上，控制总体 ability 后，September 2024 之后发布的模型在 hardest items 上仍存在小的正 DIF 残差：它们在最难题上的表现高于由 easy/medium 表现外推的预测。\n- 由于 2PL 不稳定，作者用锚定敏感性分析证明：即便把 hard items 的 discrimination 设得更“保守”，hard-item 效应仍为正。\n（注：你提供的全文片段未包含具体数值表格/置信区间/效应量大小；若需要精确数字，需要对论文的结果章节与附录表格逐项摘录。）","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 20–30 分钟）：\n- 先看引言里 的假设边界。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Single-number progress summaries (average scores, time-horizon doubling times, latent ability scalars) conflate two different phenomena: a uniform upward shift in overall ability versus a redistribution of gains toward harder items (a change in the difficulty–response curve shape). The paper asks whether the widely cited “recent gains are increasingly on hard tasks” pattern reflects a real shape change or is mostly a ceiling/discrimination artifact.","importance":"This is not a training-method paper, but it matters for pretraining research because it changes how to interpret longitudinal benchmark curves and scaling narratives. When easy/medium items saturate, any global ability increase mechanically shows up as larger apparent gains on hard items, which can be misread as a qualitative capability shift. The paper also highlights an identification failure in agentic evaluations: model-era and harness-era move together, so claims about curve-shape changes on METR/SWE-bench-like trajectories are not statistically separable without a benchmark that removes harness confounds.","technical_approach":"The approach is IRT-based decomposition.\n- Null model: a Rasch (1PL) model with a single time-varying latent ability and fixed item difficulties; this tests how much of the “harder-task gains” pattern is explained by a pure level shift.\n- Shape test: an era-based DIF-style analysis, parameterized as an additional shift concentrated on the hardest items after controlling for overall ability (not a group-by-ability slope interaction).\n- Identification strategy: avoid agentic harness confounding by using LiveCodeBench, where models directly generate code (no tool loop/harness) and difficulty ordering is exogenous from human contests.\n- Practical estimation: a free 2PL model is not stably identified on their data, so they use anchored sensitivity analysis to bound/estimate the hard-item DIF under conservative assumptions about item discrimination.","experiments":"Main empirical takeaways (mostly qualitative in the provided excerpt):\n- On METR time-horizon data, a single-ability Rasch model reproduces most of the apparent shift of gains toward harder tasks, implying ceiling/discrimination effects dominate.\n- On LiveCodeBench, after September 2024 there remains a smaller positive residual on the hardest items: models solve hard problems more often than predicted from their easy/medium performance.\n- The positive hard-item effect persists under conservative anchored sensitivity assumptions (e.g., assuming hard items are not more discriminating than easier ones).\n(The excerpt you provided does not include the numeric effect sizes, CIs, or detailed tables; This informs context engineering, long-context utilization, instruction distillation, and agent serving.","technical_approach":"CompressAgent is an environment-verified benchmark for agent control-context (ACC) compression. It uses nine independently constructed ACCs across tool use, policy compliance, and protocol-grounded multi-step execution, with 225 held-out tasks, three fixed Qwen API model identifiers, six retained-context budgets, and 15,525 runs. Only static natural-language instructions and model-visible tool documentation are compressed; executable tool schemas, validators, and environments remain unchanged. Methods include generic LLM rewriting, section-based compression, and obligation-aware compression. The latter decomposes an ACC into six obligation types—tool preconditions, argument constraints, policies, planning rules, output requirements, and recovery rules—and performs dependency-closed greedy selection by risk weight and length, with a pre-execution feasibility warning when the hard-obligation closure exceeds budget. Success is determined by deterministic environments.","experiments":"Full-context success is 93.8%. At 75% retained context, generic rewriting and section-based compression reach 92.7% and 92.4%, close to full context. In the transition region, methods separate sharply: at 35% budget, section-based compression reaches 47.0%, obligation-aware compression 39.0%, and generic rewriting 19.9%. At 25–10% budgets, all methods enter a high-risk region where executable protocols become fragile. Failure analyses connect outcomes to obligation retention, hard-closure feasibility, tool-execution failures, and action-parsing failures, with substantial variation across ACC structures.","reading_guide":"For a skim, read the introduction for the distinction between ACCs and ordinary text, then the problem-and-method section for the six obligation types, dependency-closed selection, and environment-verified protocol. opaque: more search steps, larger context, and heavier tool use do not necessarily yield better answers, and final metrics cannot distinguish failures caused by missing evidence from failures caused by misusing evidence that was already retrieved.","importance":"The work gives a useful trajectory-level diagnosis for training and improving deep research agents. It separates retrieval gaps from utilization gaps and shows that agents at different capability levels need different fixes: weaker agents are limited mainly by query direction and evidence recall, while stronger agents are closer to an oracle-reader ceiling and need better verification and answer normalization. For foundation-model researchers, the main implication is that long-horizon ability should not be measured only by final scores or search budgets; trajectory-level signals should inform both evaluation and training.","technical_approach":"The paper introduces a diagnostic framework grounded in human-annotated document-level relevance judgments on BrowseComp-Plus. At each search step, it evaluates what evidence the agent retrieves and how effectively that evidence is used. Errors are decomposed into retrieval gaps, where necessary evidence is never found, and utilization gaps, where relevant evidence is retrieved but the answer remains wrong. The analysis tracks search volume, context spend, cumulative gold-evidence recall, episode-level evidence arrival, repeated queries, and query strategy. Six agents are compared under the same ReAct framework, tools, and retriever: Tongyi DeepResearch, Qwen3.5-35B-A3B, and gpt-oss-120b at mid-scale, and Kimi K2.6, GLM 5.1, and DeepSeek V4 Pro at frontier scale. First, search volume and context spend do not predict accuracy; more accurate agents are often cheaper. Second, cumulative gold-evidence recall tracks accuracy closely: on the same question, agents that retrieve the decisive evidence usually answer correctly, while agents that miss it almost never do. Third, failures split into retrieval gaps and utilization gaps, and agents with similar accuracy can have opposite balances, so fixes should be diagnosis-specific rather than leaderboard-specific. Fourth, evidence usually arrives early, after which many episodes add no new evidence; incorrect trajectories keep searching without recovering missing evidence, producing a wasted tail. Fifth, stronger agents search more cleanly rather than more; redundant re-querying is rare in successful runs and marks failure, while the broad query style matters less than whether queries actually land on evidence.","reading_guide":"For a quick read, start with the five findings and then focus on outcome attribution, failure-mode taxonomy, and trajectory mechanism. We systematically analyze this pheno","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-03T16:21:56Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_02442.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作指出，科学推理 benchmark 只看 final-answer accuracy 会高估模型能力，因为模型可能用搜索、枚举、猜测或先猜答案再验证的方式得到正确答案，却没有完成题目想考察的推导。","importance":"对预训练和 post-training 的启发在于训练信号质量。很多 reasoning RL、SFT 数据筛选和 benchmark 排名依赖答案验证器。如果奖励只看最终答案，模型会学到可利用的捷径，而不是目标推理能力。这对构造 verifier、过程监督、可验证推理任务和难题数据集都有直接警示。","technical_approach":"工作定义了 solution hacking：给定题目 q 和参考答案 a，模型输出的答案正确，但在关键步骤用不属于专家认可方法集合 M(q) 的捷径替代了题目目标能力 C(q)。这个定义把 solution hacking 和普通错误、有效替代解法区分开。作者将判定过程操作化为专家问题，包括是否抓住 targeted crux、是否使用 task-appropriate strategy、答案是否由推导独立支持。捷径类型包括数值搜索、枚举、猜测和 answer-first verification。基于专家原则，工作构建了自动 judge，并设计了 test-time anti-hacking instruction，要求模型避免捷径，在不能给出有效推导时允许 abstain。","experiments":"solution hacking 随题目难度上升明显：普通数学推理题为 2.2%，Olympiad-level 问题为 28.3%，HLE 为 37.4%。在多个 frontier model 中，被计为正确的答案里有 8.2% 到 44.1% 被识别为 hacked solutions。较弱模型在无法可靠求解时更容易采用这种策略；答案短、容易验证的题也更容易被利用。在一个高难数学子集上，最强 anti-hacking instruction 将 final-answer accuracy 从 50.6% 降到 37.3%，abstention 从 0% 升到 27.3%；但 correct-and-non-hacked accuracy 只从 41.2% 降到 35.2%。这说明被移除的分数中相当一部分来自捷径，而不是有效推理。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 20 到 30 分钟。重点看 Introduction 的动机样例、Solution Hacking 的形式化定义、难度分层统计、自动 judge 和 anti-hacking instruction 的实验。若关注 RL 训练信号，优先看最终答案准确率下降与 non-hacked accuracy 变化之间的差异，它直接说明 final-answer reward 的偏差来源。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a failure mode in scientific reasoning evaluation: a model can produce the right final answer through an invalid shortcut, such as numerical search, enumeration, guessing, or answer-first verification, without exercising the intended reasoning skill.","importance":"The main relevance is reward and evaluation quality. Many reasoning benchmarks, SFT filters, and RL pipelines use final-answer correctness as the supervision signal. If a verifier only checks the final answer, it can reward shortcut behavior. This matters for process supervision, verifier design, hard benchmark construction, and reasoning RL data curation.","technical_approach":"The authors formalize solution hacking using the targeted capability C(q) of a problem and the set of expert-accepted methods M(q). A solution is considered hacked when the final answer is correct but an essential step replaces the intended reasoning capability with a shortcut outside M(q). This separates hacked solutions from ordinary wrong reasoning and from valid alternative derivations. The operational criteria ask whether the solution addresses the targeted crux, whether it uses a task-appropriate strategy, and whether the answer is independently supported by the derivation. The paper then distills expert judgment into an automatic judge and a test-time instruction that discourages shortcut strategies while allowing the model to abstain when it cannot provide a valid derivation.","experiments":"The reported hacking rate rises with difficulty: 2.2% on common problems, 28.3% on Olympiad-level problems, and 37.4% on HLE. Across frontier models, 8.2% to 44.1% of answers credited as correct are identified as hacked solutions. Weaker models are more likely to rely on such shortcuts when they cannot solve the problem directly, and short, easily verifiable answers are especially vulnerable. On a challenging mathematics subset, the strongest anti-hacking instruction reduces final-answer accuracy from 50.6% to 37.3% and increases abstention from 0% to 27.3%. Correct and non-hacked accuracy drops more mildly, from 41.2% to 35.2%, indicating that much of the removed score came from shortcut behavior rather than valid reasoning.","reading_guide":"Skim the paper in about 20 to 30 minutes. The main signal is that spatial reasoning should not be reduced to fixed object-pair distance estimation. A model must aggregate a candidate set, avoid duplicate or missing instances, compare metric locations, and output a calibrated distance. For native multimodal pretraining and visual tokenizer work, it highlights a gap between semantic visual understanding and physically meaningful geometric representation.","technical_approach":"SpatialQuery is a training-free geometry-assisted reasoning pipeline. It grounds reference and candidate objects, estimates monocular depth and camera geometry, and recovers floor-plane coordinates. Scene Cubifying then renders detected instances as uniformly sized, category-coded blocks on a canonical bird’s-eye-view canvas. This removes appearance, perspective, and object-scale distractions while preserving the geometry needed for the query. UA-CoT injects per-instance geometry uncertainty into the prompt, so the VLM can condition its answer on reconstruction reliability. The method changes the input representation and prompting protocol rather than the model architecture.","experiments":"SpatialQuery-1M contains over one million RGB-only question-answer pairs from 200 indoor scenes. Under a strict zero-shot protocol, SpatialQuery with Qwen3-VL-8B reports 0.259 m Floor-MAE, 90.5% Unc-Acc@0.3 m, and 84.18% proximity-decision accuracy. While coreset selection accelerates evaluation, existing methods either suffer from a severe ``cold start'' bottleneck requiring massive historical logs (e.g., Item Response Theory) or exhibit a surface lexical bias that misses the underlying reasoning manifold of tasks. We propose CoT-Core, a novel training-free core question selection framework. Recognizing that lexically ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-24T16:14:12Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_00014.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个很实际的评测瓶颈：在持续预训练、对齐和多轮 checkpoint 迭代中，完整跑大基准的成本太高，但现有 coreset 选题方法要么依赖大量历史评测日志，冷启动时根本不可用；要么只看题面文本相似度，容易把“措辞像”当成“推理结构像”。论文要解决的是：在没有历史作答记录的前提下，能否只靠题目本身，选出一个小而有代表性的评测子集，并且这个子集对模型能力排序和总分估计都足够稳定。","importance":"对基座研究的意义不在于提出新的训练方法，而在于给持续开发流程提供了更便宜的评测代理。预训练和 post-train 迭代里，真正昂贵的往往不是单次训练，而是高频评测。CoT-Core 的贡献是把“代表性”从表层文本空间，转到显式推理轨迹空间，因而在冷启动 benchmark、私有内部集和缺少历史日志的场景里更可用。它的价值主要是工程流程层面的：减少评测 token 开销，同时尽量保住全量评测的排序一致性和分数近似。","technical_approach":"方法分三步。第一步是 CoT trajectory generation：对每道题用一个教师 LLM 做 zero-shot Chain-of-Thought 展开，得到显式推理轨迹，而不是只保留原始题面。这里的关键假设是，不同表述但同一逻辑骨架的问题，在推理展开后会更接近。第二步是 contextualized trajectory embedding：把题目与其 CoT 轨迹一起编码到稠密向量空间，构造比纯 question embedding 更接近“推理流形”的表示。第三步是 isomorphic clustering and selection：在这个表示空间里做聚类或几何覆盖，选出每个簇的中心样本，形成预算受限的 coreset。\n\n论文一个比较清楚的设计点，是把 subset selection 和 performance estimation 解耦。CoT-Core 只负责选题，本身不绑定某个特定估计器。最简单时可以直接对选中子集做 standard aggregation；如果后续有历史日志，也可以接 GP-IRT 这类概率估计器。这个解耦比 IRT 路线更灵活，因为 IRT 往往把题目参数估计和分数重建绑在一起。\n\n与传统 lexical embedding + k-center 的区别，在于它不把“语义相近”近似成“词面相近”，而是显式借助 LLM 生成的推理过程来重排样本邻域。与 IRT 的区别，在于它完全不需要先验作答矩阵，因此适合新 benchmark。代价也很明确：它虽然是 training-free，但不是 free lunch，需要先为全题库生成 CoT，这一步本身有前置成本；只是这个成本是一次性的，而后续多轮评测可以摊薄。","experiments":"从全文可见，实验重点不是追求单点最高精度，而是验证三件事：一是 CoT 表示是否比原始文本表示更适合做 coreset；二是在没有历史日志时，是否能稳定逼近全量评测；三是这种收益是否依赖任务复杂度。\n\n作者将 CoT-Core 与两类基线比较：一类是 history-dependent 的 IRT/GP-IRT 路线，另一类是 training-free 的文本嵌入几何采样，如 k-center greedy。结果显示，在冷启动设定下，CoT-Core 明显优于只看题面 embedding 的方法，尤其在需要多步推理的 benchmark 上，子集分数对全量分数的拟合更稳，模型间排序也更接近全量评测。论文还强调一个边界条件：当任务本身主要考察事实记忆、模板匹配或短程判断时，显式 CoT 带来的增益会缩小，因为这类题目的“推理流形”本来就不复杂。\n\n消融方面，比较关键的是：是否使用 CoT、不同 embedding 方式、不同聚类/选点策略、以及不同估计器的组合。结论大致是，收益主要来自 reasoning-aware representation，而不是某个特定聚类算法；也就是说，核心创新在表示层，而不是采样器本身。摘要截断后未给出完整数字，现有全文片段也没有可靠的表格数值可逐项抄录，因此不宜编造具体提升百分比。","reading_guide":"泛读时建议先看 Introduction 和 Method failure/boundary discussion，这部分最能帮助判断方法适用范围。预计 20–30 分钟可以完成有效泛读。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a practical evaluation bottleneck: during continual pretraining, The paper asks whether models can faithfully materialize the acceptable set denoted by a correctness specification — whether the acceptance region induced by an LLM-authored verifier matches the true acceptance set.","importance":"This question directly bears on the reliability of RLVR (reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards), automated evaluation, and model-generated rubrics. If the authored acceptance region suffers from systematic omissions or spurious constraints, reward signals and evaluation outcomes become corrupted. The work formalizes the gap between execution (membership judgment) and authoring (constructing the acceptable set), and distinguishes extensional (enumeration) from intensional (predicate / test-suite) failure modes — a useful framing for understanding the limits of LLMs as judges or reward designers.","technical_approach":"The paper builds four reference constructions with complete ground truth to measure fidelity: two mechanically decidable word-list predicates (extensional enumeration), one hardened executable code benchmark (HumanEval+/MBPP+, intensional test authoring), and one additional controlled construction. The core metric is F1 agreement between the authored acceptance region Ŝ and the true set S*, measured on the same candidate space used to score the model's own membership execution. The central failure mechanism identified is silent omission: autoregressive models generate sets element by element and decide when to stop, but never inspect unsampled candidates, so the authored region is systematically too small. Where a compact rule exists, test-time reasoning closes the gap; where it does not, remedies such as probe gating are required.","experiments":"On mechanical word-list constructions, restating the rule as an executable predicate reaches near-perfect F1 (~0.99), while extensional enumeration performs substantially worse — the gap comes from enumeration ability, not understanding. On code tasks, model-generated test suites also miss valid solutions or introduce extra constraints, shrinking the acceptance region. Test-time reasoning largely eliminates the authoring-vs-execution gap when a compact restatable rule is available, but not otherwise.","reading_guide":"Focus on Section 3, especially the four construction designs and failure-mode analysis; §3.7 on test-time reasoning as a remedy; and §3.11 on the contrast between rule-stating and enumeration. This design turns broad language coverage into a localization problem instead of a large annotation project. This differs from translated MMLU-style benchmarks, which are static and often multiple-choice. It also differs from scripted multilingual instruction-following tests, where later turns are predetermined rather than contingent on the model’s own outputs. In practice, however, criteria can be behaviorally coupled: improving one criterion may systematically change scores on another, distorting aggregate scores used in model-release or product-update decisions. We introduce RADAR, a lightweight preflight diagnostic framework for estimating such coupling before large-scale evaluation. Given a rubric, RADAR generates targeted synthetic probes, score","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-03T07:21:32Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_01810.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"基于评分细则（rubric）的LLM-as-judge评估隐含假设各评分标准提供独立信号，但实际中语义不同的标准可能存在行为层面的耦合——提升一个标准的表现会系统性改变另一个标准的得分，扭曲用于模型发布或产品迭代的聚合分数。现有方法要么仅做语义层面的rubric去重，要么只能在已打分数据上事后诊断，无法在大规模评估启动前（preflight）检测这种行为耦合。","importance":"大模型SFT、RL对齐等后训练阶段大量依赖rubric式LLM-as-judge评分来构造训练信号和评估迭代效果，rubric的耦合问题会导致对齐目标偏差——例如重复计算同一个潜在特质，误导优化方向。RADAR作为低成本preflight诊断工具，可以在大规模评估前提前发现rubric的耦合问题，提升对齐训练信号的质量，也能让模型版本比较的评估结果更可靠。","technical_approach":"RADAR是三阶段preflight诊断框架，无需人工标注数据。第一阶段：对rubric中的每个标准C_i，用生成式LLM生成标准条件化的合成探针响应——分别构造强烈满足C_i和明显违反C_i的样本，其余标准不做限定。第二阶段：用验证LLM（即LLM judge）对所有合成样本按完整rubric的全部标准打分。第三阶段：基于打分结果计算方向耦合矩阵，衡量改变一个标准的得分对其他标准的影响方向与幅度。其中双向泄漏提示可能存在重复计算，非对称泄漏提示上下游依赖关系，任务特定泄漏提示分布敏感的耦合。","experiments":"在三个行业相关评估场景验证：NVIDIA HelpSteer2、SumPubMed、Yale-Salesforce SummEval。RADAR仅用每个标准少量探针得到的耦合估计，能够预测人工标注中观察到的相关性结构——少量合成探针即可恢复大规模人工标注语料的依赖结构。稳定性分析显示每个标准仅需很少的探针就能得到稳定的耦合估计，成本足够低，可以适配多组件agent系统的大量rubric和快速模型迭代的审计需求。","reading_guide":"重点阅读第2节的方法设计（尤其是标准条件化合成探针的思路和方向耦合矩阵的定义）、第3节的三个基准验证结果，以及第1节中关于rubric耦合两种类型（rubric内在耦合 vs 行为层面耦合）的区分。预计阅读时间15-20分钟，重点理解问题定义和方法核心逻辑，无需深入实验细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Rubric-based LLM-as-judge evaluation implicitly assumes that evaluation criteria provide independent signals. In practice, semantically distinct criteria can be behaviorally coupled: improving performance on one criterion systematically changes scores on another, distorting aggregate scores used for model release or product iteration decisions. Existing methods either only perform semantic rubric deduplication, or diagnose coupling post hoc on already-scored data. No framework can detect such behavioral coupling before large-scale evaluation runs (preflight).","importance":"LLM post-training stages (SFT, RL alignment) rely heavily on rubric-based LLM-as-judge scoring to construct training signals and evaluate model iterations. Rubric coupling introduces bias into alignment objectives — for example, double-counting the same latent trait and misdirecting optimization. As a low-cost preflight diagnostic, RADAR can identify rubric coupling issues before large-scale evaluation runs, improving the quality of alignment training signals and making model version comparisons more reliable.","technical_approach":"RADAR is a three-stage preflight diagnostic framework that requires no human-labeled data. Stage 1: For each criterion C_i in the rubric, a generator LLM produces criterion-conditioned synthetic probe responses — samples designed to strongly satisfy or clearly violate C_i, with other criteria left unspecified. Stage 2: A verifier LLM (the LLM judge) scores all synthetic responses against the full set of rubric criteria. Stage 3: The scores are used to compute a directional coupling matrix that measures how much changing one criterion's score affects other criteria, in both direction and magnitude. Bidirectional leakage suggests potential double-counting, asymmetric leakage suggests upstream-downstream dependence, and task-specific leakage suggests distribution-sensitive coupling.","experiments":"RADAR is validated on three industry-relevant evaluation settings: NVIDIA HelpSteer2, SumPubMed, and the Yale-Salesforce SummEval benchmark. Coupling estimates from RADAR, using only a small number of probes per criterion, predict the correlation structure observed in human annotations — a small set of synthetic probes recovers the dependency structure of much larger human-annotated corpora. Stability analysis shows that only a few probes per criterion are needed to produce stable coupling estimates, making the method low-cost enough to audit the growing set of rubrics required by agentic systems with many sub-components and fast model release cycles.","reading_guide":"Focus on Section 2 for the method design (especially the criterion-conditioned synthetic probe approach and the definition of the directional coupling matrix), Section 3 for validation results across the three benchmarks, and the distinction between rubric-intrinsic and behavioral coupling in Section 1. Estimated reading time: 15-20 minutes. Focus on the problem definition and core method logic; deep dive into experimental details is not necessary for a skim.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"做LLM-as-judge评测必看，重点读§3的耦合矩阵构造方法，能直接减少内部评测的系统性偏差","en":"Must-read for LLM-as-judge evaluation practitioners, focus on §3 coupling matrix construction to reduce systematic bias in internal model evaluations"},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Divyansh Singh","Reza Davari","Afra Mashhadi"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["LLM-as-judge","evaluation methodology","rubric design","evaluation diagnostic"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01810","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.01810","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.00077","title":"Beyond Accuracy: Auditing Spatial Provenance in Visual Token Pruning for OCR-Critical MLLM Inference","abstract":"Visual-token pruning is usually judged by answer quality at a fixed retention budget. evaluating visual-token pruning for OCR-critical MLLM inference. A pruned visual prefix can still produce the correct answer even when none of the retained tokens is spatially traceable to the small text region that supports the answer. Accuracy at a fixed retention budget does not expose this evidence loss.","importance":"The contribution is most relevant to evaluation and the efficiency interface of multimodal LMs. High-resolution documents, screenshots, charts, and scene-text inputs create long visual prefixes, so pruning is attractive for reducing prefill cost. In OCR-heavy settings, however, the main failure is not always an immediate accuracy drop. The model may answer from priors, layout cues, nearby text, or mixed context after the local visual evidence has been removed. Treating spatial provenance as an explicit evaluation dimension helps assess whether a compressed visual prefix still preserves an inspectable evidence path.","technical_approach":"The work proposes an evidence-risk audit rather than a learned pruning method. The MLLM weights, prompts, labels, and decoding are kept fixed; only the visual-token selector changes. For deletion-based pruning, each retained token has a source lineage from the original image-token grid. For merged or compressed outputs, each output token has a lineage set and a representative anchor. Using TextOCR word-box annotations, the audit defines lineage-based geometric coverage and anchor coverage over annotated OCR regions. Positive probes use PosECR to measure whether answer-supporting regions remain represented. Near-miss negatives use NegSRC to measure whether the source region for a confusable absent string remains available as local counter-evidence. Transparent selectors such as Target, Random, Grid, and OCR/layout-assisted variants provide controlled operating points under a shared budget contract.","experiments":"The key result is a separation between answer accuracy and spatial provenance. On a locked image-disjoint confirmation set, Qwen with the Target selector at 30% visual-token retention reaches observed accuracy 0.786, while the full visual prefix reaches 0.783; the paired image-cluster difference is +0.003 with a 95% confidence interval of [-0.014, +0.020]. Accuracy is nearly unchanged, but provenance differs sharply. At the same budget, positive-region coverage is 0.620 for Target, compared with 0.270 for Random and 0.318 for Grid. Experiments across Qwen, LLaVA, and InternVL show different quality-risk-traceability frontiers, and open QA requires substantially higher retention than yes/no confirmation. A retention ratio that is safe for one model or task is therefore not automatically safe for another.","reading_guide":"A 20–30 minute skim is sufficient. it fails to update its reasoning after the visual evidence changes. The central finding is that evidence-bearing spans in a previous CoT can act as a textual shortcut, competing with recomputation from the current image.","importance":"The main relevance to foundation-model research is in multimodal reasoning supervision. CoT traces are not neutral explanations; they can serialize visual observations into text and make stale evidence easier to reuse than fresh visual evidence. This matters for VLM pretraining and post-training pipelines that rely on reflective reasoning, self-correction, or multimodal CoT data. A corrected final answer is not a reliable certificate that the model has refreshed its visual state.","technical_approach":"The study uses a matched counterfactual setup. Each example contains a current image-question-answer triple and a structurally similar counterfactual image with a different answer. The model first produces a coherent prior CoT on the counterfactual image. The same model is then asked to reflect under the current image while the stale CoT remains in context. The analysis compares this prior-conditioned path with a history-free direct path. Controlled removals separate three effects: removing evidence-bearing spans, removing length-matched non-evidence spans, and removing the final-answer span. The paper also perturbs evidence order to test whether the organization of the stale reasoning path affects reuse. The proposed intervention, Fresh-State Attention Firewall, blocks fresh computation from attending to the prior CoT instead of merely prompting the model to inspect the image again.","experiments":"The evaluation covers 16 VLMs and 800 human-checked VS-Bench counterfactual pairs drawn from MathVista-MINI, MathVerse-MINI, MathVision, and MMMU-Pro. Eligibility requires the model to answer the counterfactual image correctly and to answer the current image correctly without history, so the measured effect is prior-CoT interference rather than base task failure. Removing evidence-bearing content shifts answer preference more than removing length-matched non-evidence context or the final-answer span. The content-specific effect is positive for all 16 models and grows as more stale evidence is removed. Even when a model recovers the current answer, matched support withdrawal reveals residual dependence on the old reasoning path. On five Qwen VLMs, FSAF improves visual update rate from 35.28% to 53.61% and reduces prior-answer rate from 39.22% to 3.67%. A prompt-only variant does not provide a reliable update gain.","reading_guide":"Skim time: about 25–35 minutes. The most useful result tables or figures are those comparing evidence removal, non-evidence removal, final-answer removal, and evidence-order disruption. The FSAF section is worth checking for the exact attention restriction and whether it sacrifices useful context. 逐步放松严格因果约束；通过 curriculum 扩大 decoding block，让模型从 AR 过渡到更并行的条件结构，避免一次性切到双向注意力导致训练不稳。第二阶段是 Block-Wise Distillation，让 student 接触并行解码过程中的中间 noisy states，并用 on-policy small-block diffusion teacher 部署。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets the latency and exposure-bias limitations of autoregressive VLA models for autonomous driving. Instead of training a narrow diffusion policy from scratch, it tries to translate a pretrained AR driving generalist into a parallel discrete diffusion VLA while preserving its multi-task visual-language-action capability.","importance":"The useful signal for foundation-model research is the AR-to-diffusion transition recipe. The domain is driving, but the modeling issue is broader: how to move a causal, teacher-forced model into a bidirectional iterative-refinement generator without losing the semantic and task knowledge stored in the pretrained model. The contribution is mainly a training and distillation pipeline, with relevance to unified AR+diffusion modeling, action-token generation, and low-latency multimodal language models.","technical_approach":"WAM-Diff2 is a multi-task discrete diffusion VLA trained through a three-stage hierarchical distillation process. Stage I, Progressive Block-Wise Adaptation, introduces block-causal attention and gradually relaxes strict causal decoding through a curriculum over decoding blocks. This avoids an abrupt switch from unidirectional AR attention to bidirectional refinement. Stage II, Block-Wise Distillation, exposes the student to intermediate noisy states produced during parallel decoding and uses an on-policy small-block diffusion teacher to align predictions under the diffusion process. This addresses the distribution mismatch between teacher-forced AR training and noisy-state refinement. Stage III, Model-Wise Cross-Scale Distillation, transfers knowledge from an 8B diffusion teacher to a compact 2B student. The paper reports that models sharing the diffusion paradigm have more aligned token prediction patterns, making cross-scale transfer easier than directly compressing an AR teacher into a diffusion student.","experiments":"The evaluation spans trajectory planning on NAVSIM and Bench2Drive, driving-oriented VQA on DriveBench and LingoQA, and visual perception on COCO. The reported outcome is performance parity or improvement relative to the AR foundation, with a 2.8× decoding speedup from the AR-to-diffusion conversion. With system-level optimizations such as FlashInfer and CUDA Graphs, the reported acceleration reaches 15.1×. The key experiments to inspect are the stage-wise distillation ablations, the 8B-to-2B cross-scale distillation comparison, and the task-performance-versus-latency trade-off. The speedup numbers need careful reading: whether they include the vision encoder, preprocessing, batch size effects, and the exact hardware setup matters for interpreting deployment gains.","reading_guide":"Skim time: about 30–45 minutes. For pretraining-oriented readers, Stage I and Stage II are the most relevant parts: they address how to change attention structure gradually and how to train on diffusion noisy states rather than AR teacher-forced prefixes. This aligns the distillation states with the student's own generation distribution. However, it still assumes that the complete teacher distribution is an appropriate target across student capacities. OPD samples trajectories from the current student, so supervision is applied at prefixes the student actually visits. However, sharing the same prefix does not guarantee that the complete teacher distribution is a suitable target. In VLM reasoning, teacher corrections may rely on visual evidence that a compact student cannot represent.","importance":"The main training lesson is that stronger teacher targets are not always better for smaller multimodal students. The useful signal is the part of the teacher correction that lies within the student’s current response geometry. This matters for VLM distillation, multimodal mid-training, and synthetic supervision pipelines where large models provide dense token-level targets. The paper offers a more structured alternative to simply reducing the KL weight or interpolating the teacher distribution uniformly.","technical_approach":"The method keeps the OPD protocol: the student rolls out an on-policy trajectory, and both student and teacher are evaluated on the resulting prefixes. Standard OPD minimizes a full-vocabulary token-level reverse KL to the complete teacher distribution. FP-OPD changes only the target. It represents the teacher signal as a correction to the student log-probabilities, estimates the student’s local visual response space using detached perturbation probes, and projects the teacher correction onto that space under the Fisher metric induced locally by KL. The projected correction defines a reachable target q^R, which replaces the full teacher distribution in the same reverse-KL loss. Unlike temperature scaling or uniform target weakening, the projection selectively keeps corrections that the student can express through its current visual pathway.","experiments":"A diagnostic target-scaling study shows the mismatch clearly. In an 8B-to-2B setting, a target interpolation strength of α=0.5 lets the student remove 56.3% of the prescribed KL gap after one epoch, while the complete teacher target at α=1 removes only 46.6%. The intermediate target also improves MathVista Geometry from 55.29 to 57.03. Autoregressive codec LMs are usually stronger on intelligibility, alignment, and zero-shot speaker preservation, but they decode one token at a time and often rely on very large training sets. Non-autoregressive diffusion or flow-based TTS is faster, yet it often needs explicit duration modeling or suffers from skipped/repeated words and unstable text-speech alignment. That gives a useful hypothesis for non-AR generative modeling more broadly: for modalities with strong local coherence and weaker but still necessary long-range ordering, blockwise sequential generation may be a better inductive bias than either fully autoregressive or fully parallel generation. The paper also makes an explicit data-efficiency argument. Masked diffusion exposes the same sequence under many corruption patterns, which acts like built-in augmentation.
