Although the experiments target image generation rather than text LMs, the setup is close to sequence-level post-training for discrete generative models: a frozen tokenizer and decoder, an AR Transformer, and a distribution-level loss applied after likelihood pretraining. It is a relevant reference point for thinking about exposure bias, rollout-based training, and non-token-level objectives in tokenized multimodal generation.","technical_approach":"The method post-trains a pretrained discrete AR generator using only a Fréchet Distance loss in a frozen representation space. The key design is a dual-pass procedure. The first pass performs gradient-free generation under the model’s native inference configuration and records detached rollout contexts. The second pass replays generation on these self-generated contexts and applies a probability-level straight-through estimator: the forward pass keeps hard argmax decoding, while the backward pass propagates gradients through temperature-scaled probabilities. The tokenizer, decoder, and feature extractors are frozen; only the AR generator is updated. This differs from simply adding an image-level loss under teacher forcing, because the optimization is tied to contexts sampled from the model’s own inference policy.","experiments":"The method is evaluated on class-conditional ImageNet 256×256 with LlamaGen, TiTok, VAR, and GigaTok. The best reported FID improves from 2.42 to 1.43 without changing parameter count or inference steps. and","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-03T17:04:43Z","category_id":"multimodal_gen","cover_url":"/covers/2608_02502.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"普通Muon优化器直接应用于Diffusion Transformer（DiT）时会出现后期收敛失效、最终生成质量无法稳定优于AdamW的问题，根因是DiT中为算力效率拼接的功能异构权重会被普通Muon的整体正交化操作引入子空间耦合，扭曲参数更新方向。","importance":"指出了基于线性最小化预言机（Linear Minimization Oracle，LMO）的优化器（如Muon系列）与大模型训练中常见的权重融合工程优化之间的兼容性问题——这类问题此前在LLM场景未被充分暴露，因为LLM融合层的梯度统计差异通常小于DiT。提出的CMuon仅需在优化器中做少量代码修改，无明显额外计算开销，为DiT及其他生成式基座预训练的优化器选型与适配提供了可落地的工程参考，也提示优化器设计需要结合架构的参数组织方式调整，而非仅关注算法本身的收敛性质。","technical_approach":"提出分块Muon（CMuon）：在执行牛顿-舒尔茨（Newton-Schulz）动量正交化步骤前，将DiT中为算力效率拼接的融合张量（如QKV融合投影、AdaLN融合投影）按原始功能子模块拆分，对每个子块的动量矩阵独立做正交化，从根源上避免功能不相交子空间的干扰。同时针对矩阵缩放因子、保留AdamW的层选择（如嵌入层、最终投影层）、学习率缩放策略三类关键超参做了系统消融，给出最优配置建议。","experiments":"在675M参数DiT模型上验证，采用VA-VAE潜空间，训练集为ImageNet Transformers (DiTs) leads to suboptimal late-stage convergence and no stable quality gains over AdamW, with the root cause being that standard DiT implementations concatenate functionally distinct weights from AdaLN, QKV, and FFN layers into unified tensors for computational efficiency, and vanilla Muon performs collective momentum orthogonalization on the full fused matrix, inducing unintended coupling between functionally disjoint subspaces that distorts parameter update directions.","importance":"The work identifies a critical compatibility issue between Linear Minimization Oracle (LMO)-based optimizers (Muon family) and common engineering optimizations (weight fusion) in generative model training, a problem not fully exposed in LLM scenarios where gradient statistics across fused layers are typically more homogeneous. The proposed CMuon requires minimal code changes and negligible computational overhead, providing practical engineering guidance for adapting Muon-style optimizers to DiT and other generative foundation model pre-training pipelines, and highlights that optimizer design needs to account for architectural parameter organization rather than only algorithmic convergence properties.","technical_approach":"Chunked Muon (CMuon) splits fused weight tensors (e.g., fused QKV projections, fused AdaLN projections) back into their original functional sub-components before performing Newton-Schulz orthogonalization on each chunk's momentum independently, eliminating cross-subspace interference at its source. The work also conducts systematic ablation on three key hyperparameter categories: matrix scaling factors, layer selection for retaining AdamW (e.g., embeddings, final projection layers), and learning rate scaling strategies, with optimal configuration recommendations provided.","experiments":"Validation is conducted on a 675M-parameter DiT with VA-VAE latent space, trained on ImageNet 256×256 at batch size 1024 for 200 epochs. CMuon delivers consistent 2× training speedup over AdamW throughout the entire training process with no late-stage plateau observed in vanilla Muon, reaching a final FID of 1.18 without auxiliary acceleration techniques such as Representation Alignment (REPA). Ablations confirm consistent improvements over vanilla Muon across various hyperparameter configurations, with gains in both convergence speed and final generation quality.","reading_guide":"Skim the root cause analysis of vanilla Muon's late-stage convergence failure on DiTs in the introduction first, then review the formalization of subspace interference and Algorithm 1 (CMuon pseudocode) in the methodology section, and focus on hyperparameter ablation conclusions and optimal configuration suggestions in experiments. While Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is widely adopted, it suffers from sparse reward signals and loses gradients entirely when all responses within a group receive identical rewards. On-policy distillation (OPD) offers a natural remedy by providing dense, token-level supervision from a teacher model. However, naively combining GRPO with OPD leads to deg","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-01T17:29:14Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_00782.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"RSTG 解决的是 RLVR 中 GRPO 学习信号稀疏的问题，尤其是 group 内样本全错时的 negative zero-variance prompts。GRPO 在这种情况下没有相对优势，梯度会消失。直接加入 on-policy distillation 虽然能提供 token-level dense signal，但会带来 teacher-boundedness、样本不适配、token advantage 大量为负和局部前缀误差传播等问题，实际效果可能差于标准 GRPO。","importance":"这项工作对 post-train 与 pretrain 接口有明确参考价值。它说明 dense teacher signal 不能无差别加入 RL，而应该只在 RL 无法给出有效梯度的位置补信号。这个思想对 RL 数据效率、verifier-based training、reasoning model 的失败样本挖掘都有启发。贡献量级属于方法级改进，不是新训练范式，但抓住了 GRPO+distillation 中一个常见且实际的问题：什么时候应该蒸馏，蒸馏哪些 token，以及如何避免过快贴近 teacher。","technical_approach":"RSTG 先把 GRPO、OPD 和 SFT 放到统一 policy-gradient 视角下分析。GRPO 用 group reward 的均值和方差估计 advantage；当组内 reward 相同，advantage 退化。OPD 在学生自己采样的轨迹上最小化 student 到 teacher 的 reverse KL，可写成 token-level advantage，其中 advantage 与 teacher-student log-prob gap 相关。问题是多数学生生成 token 在 teacher 下概率低，负 advantage 过多，会压制探索。RSTG 的处理有三部分。第一，sample selection：只在 student 失败但 teacher 能成功的样本上施加 OPD，重点覆盖 negative zero-variance prompts，并按 teacher mastery 加权。文中指出，仅使用全量数据的 3.63% 做这类 OPD，效果反而优于对所有样本蒸馏。第二，token selection：只更新学生高熵 token 或 teacher-student divergence 较大的 token，减少噪声，也减缓过早收敛到 teacher。第三，auxiliary SFT：在 negative zero-variance prompts 上使用 teacher 预生成的正确轨迹做 SFT，补充全局正向轨迹信号，缓解 OPD 只沿学生错误前缀给局部梯度的问题。","experiments":"实验覆盖 Qwen2.5 和 Qwen3 系列，在数学和代码 benchmark 上验证。相对于 naive GRPO+OPD，RSTG 在数学任务上提升 4.02%，在代码任务上提升 3.05%。文中还报告了几个训练动态层面的现象：选择性 OPD 能缓解 advantage asymmetry；token selection 会减缓向 teacher 的过快收敛；辅助 SFT 可以减少负样本组完全无梯度的问题；RSTG 还避免了 response length 的突然膨胀。由于给出的全文片段未包含完整实验表格，无法确认具体模型尺寸、每个 benchmark 的绝对分数和训练 token 数。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 25–30 分钟。先看 weighting、token-level mask 的定义。实验部分优先看 Qwen2.5/Qwen3 上的主结果、OPD 数据比例 3.63% 的消融、response length 曲线和 advantage distribution。若在做 RLVR recipe，这项工作最值得复用的是“只在 RL 失效处补 teacher signal”的训练调度，而不是简单叠加蒸馏 loss。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"RSTG addresses the loss of learning signal in RLVR with GRPO, especially on negative zero-variance prompts where every sampled response in the group is wrong. In that case, group-relative advantages vanish. On-policy distillation can provide dense token-level supervision from a teacher, but a naive GRPO+OPD objective often performs worse because distillation is not useful for every sample, it can make the student converge too quickly to the teacher, and its token-level advantages are often negative on student-generated trajectories.","importance":"The work is useful for the interface between RL post-training and pretraining-style learning signals. It argues that dense teacher supervision should not be applied uniformly; it should be used where RL has no useful gradient. This is relevant to RL data efficiency, verifier-based reasoning training, and failure-driven data construction. The contribution is a targeted recipe rather than a new training paradigm, but it addresses a practical failure mode in GRPO plus distillation: when to distill, which tokens to update, and how to avoid collapsing exploration toward the teacher too early.","technical_approach":"$32","experiments":"The experiments cover Qwen2.5 and Qwen3 model families on mathematical and code benchmarks. The counterintuitive result is that seeding new problems from near-successes—problems the model can mostly get right—works better than seeding from the hardest failures.","technical_approach":"Question-begets-Question (QbQ) has a teacher model transform existing problems into diverse variants that probe the same underlying skills, reducing the diversity collapse seen in static augmentation. Training uses GRPO with only problem statements and final answers, without teacher step-by-step reasoning traces, which models the no-oracle-reasoning setting and limits distillation. The self-evolving curriculum runs for 20 rounds: evaluate the current checkpoint on the previous round’s problems with pass@16, sort them into mastered, mostly correct, sometimes correct, and never correct bands; seed new variants from the mostly-correct problems; then train with GRPO. Because each round’s seeds come from the previous round’s generated problems rather than a fixed source set, the curriculum continuously tracks the model’s evolving ability.","experiments":"Qwen2.5-Math-7B starts at 5.6% pass@1 on AIME 2025/2026. We propose Instruction-Conditioned Exploration (ICE), which supplements tas","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-03T11:47:52Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_02087.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"RL post-training 中 LLM 的探索问题：token 级随机扰动会脱离文本流形，而高概率采样又导致行为多样性不足，RL 训练本身还会使策略分布变尖锐而非拓宽。如何在训练时系统性地扩大行为覆盖，并将这些探索到的行为迁移到测试时的无条件策略上。","importance":"对理解 RL post-training 中的探索-利用权衡有参考价值，尤其是 instruction-conditioned rollout 作为一种比 token 级噪声更结构化的探索手段。但实验结果显示方法仅在 1.7B 规模有效，4B 规模无增益，适用范围有限，属于增量性工作。","technical_approach":"ICE 在训练时从一组 N 个差异化行为指令中采样，附加到任务 prompt 上，驱动 instruction-conditioned 的 teacher 策略产生多样化 rollout。Asymmetric-RL/SD 定义两个共享参数的策略：teacher 接收 DAPO（GRPO 变体）的 RL 更新，student 仅接收 forward-KL 自蒸馏更新，将 teacher 经过正确性过滤的 rollout 知识迁移到无条件策略。测试时直接使用 student（不加指令），无需额外推理开销。与已有 prompt augmentation 方法的区别在于指令是观点性、策略性的行为引导，而非格式说明。","experiments":"在 Qwen3 1.7B/4K 上，ICE+Asymmetric-RL/SD 相比 DAPO baseline 在 5 个数学推理 benchmark 上 macro-mean pass@1 相对提升 5.0%，5/5 全胜，95% bootstrap CI 不含零。1.7B/8K 长上下文设置下增益缩小。4B/4K 设置下无提升。ICE+RL alone（无自蒸馏）的增益更小且不确定性更大，说明自蒸馏组件对效果有贡献。","reading_guide":"重点看 §3（ICE 形式化定义和与 support composition vs expansion 的区分）和 §4（Asymmetric-RL/SD 的非对称更新机制）。Figure 1 的流程图能快速理解 teacher-student 共享参数结构。实验部分关注多规模对比表，注意 4B 无增益这一负面结果。预计泛读 15-20 分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Exploration during RL post-training of LLMs: token-level random perturbation falls off the text manifold, high-probability sampling yields insufficient behavioral diversity, and RL itself sharpens rather than broadens the policy distribution. Reusing each rollout batch for additional learner updates amortizes this cost, but later updates become increasingly off-policy as the learner departs from the behavior policy. At a token position, exact off-policy correction must account for both the current action and the probability of reaching its prefix. The cumulative importance ratio provides this correction, but its produc","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-02T18:02:03Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_01418.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"LLM 强化学习中，为了摊销自回归 rollout 的高昂采样成本，通常会对同一批轨迹做多轮（多 epoch）策略更新；但随着更新轮次增加，当前策略逐渐偏离采样时的行为策略（policy lag / off-policy 漂移），标准 PPO/GRPO 使用的 token 级局部重要性比只是一种近端近似，无法纠正前缀访问分布的变化，而精确的状态-动作密度比（累积乘积形式）动态范围又会随前缀长度和漂移程度急剧膨胀，导致训练不稳定。","importance":"提出了一种在因果前缀校正与权重尺度可控之间取得折中方案的 off-policy 目标 PNPO，对需要复用 rollout、提升采样效率的 LLM RL 训练（尤其是推理模型的大规模 GRPO/PPO 训练）有直接参考价值。其核心洞察——自回归设定下前缀分布的变化是可计算的，但精确累积比尺度不可控——也为理解 policy lag 下的 off-policy 校正提供了更清晰的分解视角。","technical_approach":"PNPO 在位置 t 使用前缀似然比的几何均值作为权重：w_t^PN = (∏_{k=1}^t r_k)^{1/t}，其中 r_k = π_θ(y_k|x,y_{<k}) / π_β(y_k|x,y_{<k}) 是 token 级局部比。与精确累积比 C_t = ∏_{k=1}^t r_k 相比，1/t 次方同时压缩了当前 token 和历史前缀的比，牺牲了精确的状态-动作密度校正，换取权重尺度的可控性。与 GSPO 使用的全序列共享长度归一化统计量相比，PNPO 的权重随位置变化且不依赖未来后缀，保持了因果性，仅在终止位置数值上与全响应几何均值一致。完整目标还结合了位置相关的接受门控和响应级平均。","experiments":"在长上下文数学推理任务上构造了两种受控 off-policy  regime：每批 rollout 做 1 个 epoch 和 4 个 epoch 的策略更新，在相同 minibatch 更新预算（2400 次更新）下对比。1 epoch 时 PNPO 并不稳定优于 GSPO；4 epoch 时 PNPO 在三个独立选择的 benchmark 上均取得最高 Avg@32，三个峰值的未加权均值为 50.24，比 GSPO 高 3.00 个百分点。在匹配的 2400 次更新预算下，4 epoch PNPO 经过 150 批 rollout 达到最终 macro Avg@32 49.66，与 1 epoch 下 600 批 rollout 达到的 49.56 相当，即 rollout 采样量减少到 1/4 仍能达到相近性能。","reading_guide":"重点阅读第 3 节 PNPO 目标的完整定义，以及图 1 对四种权重粒度（token 局部比、累积比、序列共享统计量、前缀归一化）的位置依赖 log 权重尺度对比。第 4 节的 1 epoch vs 4 epoch 对比实验是核心结果，关注其如何通过匹配更新预算来分离 rollout 效率和更新次数的混淆。附录中的权重分布可视化有助于理解为什么几何均值能有效压缩动态范围。预计阅读时间 30-40 分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In LLM reinforcement learning, autoregressive rollout generation is expensive, so reusing a collected batch for multiple learner updates (multi-epoch PPO/GRPO) is a standard way to amortize sampling cost. However, as the learner drifts away from the behavior policy across updates (policy lag / off-policy drift), the token-level local importance ratio used by PPO/GRPO is only a proximal approximation and does not correct for changes in prefix visitation distribution. The exact state-action density ratio (cumulative product form) has a dynamic range that grows with prefix length and drift magnitude, causing training instability.","importance":"PNPO proposes an off-policy objective that trades off exact causal-prefix correction for controllable weight scale. It is directly relevant to LLM RL training that reuses rollouts to improve sample efficiency, especially large-scale reasoning model training with GRPO/PPO. The core insight—that prefix distribution changes are computable under autoregressive generation but the exact cumulative ratio has intractable scale—also provides a clearer decomposition for understanding off-policy correction under policy lag.","technical_approach":"At position t, PNPO uses the geometric mean of likelihood ratios along the prefix as the weight: w_t^PN = (∏_{k=1}^t r_k)^{1/t}, where r_k = π_θ(y_k|x,y_{<k}) / π_β(y_k|x,y_{<k}) is the token-level local ratio. Compared to the exact cumulative ratio C_t = ∏_{k=1}^t r_k, the 1/t power tempers both the current token ratio and all preceding ratios, sacrificing exact state-action density correction for scale control. Unlike GSPO's sequence-shared length-normalized statistic, PNPO's weight is position-dependent and excludes future suffix likelihood changes, preserving causality; it only numerically coincides with the full-response geometric mean at the terminal position. The full objective combines this weight with a position-dependent acceptance gate and response-level averaging.","experiments":"Two controlled off-policy regimes are constructed on long-context mathematical reasoning: 1 epoch and 4 epochs of policy updates per rollout batch, compared under a matched minibatch-update budget of 2,400 updates. With 1 epoch, PNPO does not consistently outperform GSPO. With 4 epochs, PNPO attains the highest Avg@32 on each of three independently selected benchmarks; the unweighted mean of the three benchmark peaks is 50.24, 3.00 percentage points above GSPO. Under the matched 2,400-update budget, 4-epoch PNPO reaches a final macro Avg@32 of 49.66 after 150 rollout batches, comparable to the 49.56 reached after 600 batches with 1 epoch—meaning roughly 4x reduction in rollout sampling for similar performance.","reading_guide":"Focus on Section 3 for the full PNPO objective specification, and Figure 1 comparing position-dependent log-weight scales across four weighting granularities (token-local, cumulative, sequence-shared, prefix-normalized). Section 4's 1-epoch vs 4-epoch comparison is the core result; pay attention to how the matched update budget disentangles rollout efficiency from update count. Appendix visualizations of weight distributions help understand why the geometric mean compresses dynamic range effectively. However, a complete solution ties reusable mathematical relations to a specific final answer, reasoning order, wording, and instance-specific arithmetic details. As a result, the teacher's token-level targets may depend on information the student cannot recover from question-only input at inference time, creating a training-deployment information asymmetry that reduces transfer effectiveness.","importance":"The paper explicitly frames the design of privileged information (\"what should the teacher see?\") in OPSD as a transfer problem. Drawing on classical problem-space theory, it replaces the full solution trace with structured problem-solving structure as the teacher context, introducing a new design dimension for privileged information in self-distillation. It is methodologically relevant for post-training of reasoning models, especially lines that rely on self-distillation to improve reasoning capabilities.","technical_approach":"PS-OPSD (Problem-Space-Guided OPSD) first uses an offline extractor to transform each verified reference solution into Problem-Space Guidance, which records the Initial State, Goal Conditions, Constraints, and a Selected State-Transition Path. Each transition specifies an Operator, its Preconditions, the induced Transformation, and the Resulting State. Within the OPSD training loop, only the privileged teacher's context is modified—from the complete solution to the structured guidance—while the student continues to generate question-only on-policy trajectories and the token-level distillation objective remains unchanged. At inference, the student reasons from the question alone, with no access to the reference solution, guidance, privileged teacher, or extractor.","experiments":"Evaluated on Qwen3 at three scales (1.7B, 4B, 8B) across three competition-level mathematical reasoning benchmarks. Offers direct methodological reference for RL pretrain and latent reasoning paradigm development.","technical_approach":"Proposes Latent Thought Credit (LTC), a hierarchical credit assignment framework. For each prompt, LTC samples K latent thoughts, freezes the post-thought context of each thought, then samples M discrete answers from that fixed context. The mean reward across these M answers estimates the expected utility of the corresponding latent thought. Two levels of advantages are constructed: thought-level advantages (comparing estimated rewards of different thoughts for the same prompt) optimize the latent thought generation phase, while answer-level advantages (group-relative normalization across all answers for the same prompt) optimize the answer generation phase. An advantage-weighted thought matching objective is added to guide the current policy to reproduce high-credit latent thoughts. Latent thoughts are sampled via Gumbel-Softmax relaxation as continuous embedding mixtures rather than discrete tokens.","experiments":"Effectiveness is validated on reasoning and STEM benchmarks. Ablation studies show multi-answer estimation stabilizes thought-level credit assignment, and fixed-context diagnostics confirm that averaging rewards across multiple answers from a frozen post-thought context reduces estimation variance. In multi-turn settings, models receive a verifier score after each turn and iteratively refine their outputs. Although such scores provide dense feedback, they do not directly provide dense credit: a score measures the quality of the current output, while credit should measure how the current turn changes the refinement trajectory. We propose TCPO, a turn-level credit assignment method for verifier","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-03T04:01:36Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_01667.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"验证器引导的多轮RL中，每轮都能获取验证器分数，但分数衡量的是当前输出的质量，而非当前轮次对整个优化轨迹的边际贡献（即信用）。现有方法要么将最终结果广播给所有轮次，要么用原始分数或相邻轮差作为奖励，无法正确为延迟贡献轮次、正确状态保持轮次、或为后续成功铺路的非改进轮次分配信用，限制了多轮RL的训练效率和最终效果。","importance":"它将多轮推理RL的核心挑战重新定义为「分数到信用的转换」，而非奖励密度不足，这一框架对推理增强类预训练（如自我修正范式）、多轮Agent的RL训练都有直接参考价值。方法不需要额外人工标注或学习型critic，实现成本低，容易集成到现有GRPO风格的训练流程中。","technical_approach":"TCPO是轮次级别的信用分配方法，兼容GRPO风格的策略优化，不需要修改基础多轮rollout流程，也不需要额外标签或学习型critic。它通过三种参考比较，将验证器分数轨迹转换为轮次优势：\n1. 回溯信用：将当前轮分数与之前的最好成绩对比，捕捉即时进步、成功保持或倒退；\n2. 后见延迟信用：针对成功前的非改进轮次，用其后续能达到的最好分数，与同prompt同轮次的其他合格轨迹对比，识别那些暂时无改进但为后续成功铺路的轮次；\n3. 固定历史反事实估计：针对高惊讶度的合格轮次，在相同历史下采样其他输出，用验证器对比原输出的效果，解决高不确定轮次的信用模糊问题。\n此外采用固定预算训练协议：即使提前达到成功标准，也继续rollout到最大轮数，以观测成功后的状态保持或倒退行为。得到的轮次优势广播给对应轮的所有token，用GRPO裁剪目标优化。","experiments":"在不同模型规模、任务域、验证器类型上均有效。数学和代码基准上，Qwen3-4B和DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B的best-turn Pass@8均达到最优或并列最优，同时通常减少成功所需轮数。对比MT-GRPO，Qwen3-4B在MATH-500、AIME、LiveCodeBench、HumanEval上分别提升+4.4、+2.2、+1.6、+0.6个点，DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B上提升幅度更大。性能优于或持平GVPO。在AppWorld多轮工具使用Agent基准上，TGC和SGC设置下的Dev和Test-N均有提升，TGC下的Test-C也有提升。额外训练成本较低。","reading_guide":"泛读优先看Introduction部分提出的「分数到信用转换」问题定义，这是核心概念贡献。然后看方法部分三种信用分配机制的设计直觉和适用场景，无需深究公式细节。实验部分看跨模型跨任务的主结果，以及与MT-GRPO、GVPO的对比即可。预计阅读时间20分钟左右。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In verifier-guided multi-turn reinforcement learning for LLMs, per-turn verifier scores measure current output quality, not the marginal contribution (credit) of that turn to the overall refinement trajectory. First, it collects paired successful and failed trajectories for each query, narrowing the root cause search space to divergence points between the two trajectories instead of probing every step with expensive counterfactual replay. Second, it decomposes trajectories into atomic reasoning steps and builds a dependency hypergraph, where hyperedges capture high-order logical dependencies across multiple steps (rather than pairwise connections in standard graphs), enabling back-tracing of upstream root causes along non-linear dependency paths. When the model rarely solves the task, the verifier returns few positive rewards, and group-based policy optimization receives little useful signal. Better diffusion likelihood estimators alone do not fix this shortage of successful rollouts.","importance":"The work is relevant to the post-training interface of non-autoregressive language models. For dLLMs, RL is hard not only because exact sequence likelihood is intractable under denoising, but also because the model may not explore successful solutions early in training. Using a stronger external policy as a source of high-reward trajectories is a natural idea, but the paper shows that naive mixing is fragile. Length mismatch and reward processing are training signals, not implementation details. The same issue is likely to appear in masked, any-order, and other non-AR language-modeling regimes.","technical_approach":"ERILS mixes on-policy rollouts from the target dLLM with external rollouts generated by a stronger fixed policy, instantiated as an autoregressive LLM. It adds two mechanisms. Rollout Length Control adjusts external completions so that their lengths are closer to the generation length used by the target dLLM, while trying to preserve verifier-assessed correctness. This matters because external answers are often much longer; directly training on them gives less benefit than their reward would suggest. Source-Specific Reward Processing avoids normalizing on-policy and external rewards with a single group mean. Since the two sources have different reward distributions, joint processing can create unstable advantages and lead to collapse. ERILS processes the sources separately before using them in the policy update.","experiments":"The evaluation covers Sudoku, Countdown, and MATH500 under zero-shot settings with stochastic multi-sample metrics. On Sudoku, all ERILS best-of-k completion accuracies exceed 90%, while the strongest reproduced on-policy baseline reaches only 40.3% at best-of-4. On Countdown, ERILS reaches 87.5% Pass@4, compared with 76.6% for SPG. The supplied text also reports gains on MATH500, but does not include the full number. The ablations are the important part: uncontrolled external rollouts give limited gains despite high verifier rewards, length control improves their usefulness, and joint reward processing across sources destabilizes optimization.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes about 30–40 minutes. To mitigate this limitation, ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-02T15:41:57Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_01321.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的是 search-augmented agent 在多步检索场景下的 credit assignment 过于稀疏。标准 GRPO 只根据最终答案给 trajectory 一个 outcome reward，中间每一步 search 是否带来了新证据、是否是必要决策，都没有被单独评估。结果就是训练容易不稳定，策略会出现冗余搜索、无效迭代，甚至 collapse。论文的核心问题因此不是“怎么做更强的搜索 agent”，而是“怎么给多步搜索过程分配更细、更准的训练信号”。","importance":"它对预训练本身的直接关系较弱，更接近 post-train / agent RL 的过程监督问题。但如果关心 pretrain 与 post-train 的接口，这篇工作有一个明确启发：当模型需要通过外部工具逐步收集证据时，终局奖励过粗，训练信号质量会成为主要瓶颈。BiCAA 的价值在于把搜索步骤的好坏拆成两个维度：即时是否提升可解性，以及事后看是否对成功路径关键。这种双向 credit assignment 思路，对工具使用、检索增强推理、甚至长链 CoT 的过程奖励设计都有参考意义。","technical_approach":"方法的核心是构造 bidirectional process reward。作者认为单一过程奖励不够，因为只看 forward gain 会鼓励局部有帮助但整体冗余的搜索，只看 hindsight criticality 又缺少即时反馈，无法判断某一步到底带来了多少新信息。因此 BiCAA 同时建模两个信号。\n\n第一部分是 forward solvability gain。对一个问题及其 ground-truth answer，作者把每一步检索看成增量证据收集。对于第 t 步，比较模型在看到前缀信息 o_{\\le t} 与 o_{\\le t-1} 时，对正确答案 a 的条件对数似然变化。这个变化量衡量该步搜索是否真的提高了模型对正确答案的信心。它本质上是“这一步新拿到的信息，是否让答案更可解”。\n\n第二部分是 hindsight success criticality。这个分支从完整轨迹回看，评估某一步是否是通向正确最终答案的关键动作，而不是偶然带来一点局部增益的冗余步骤。摘要和正文给出的描述表明，作者会根据最终成功轨迹中的因果必要性或关键性，对各步赋予 retrospective credit。虽然给出的全文片段没有完整公式，但方法意图很清楚：如果某一步既提高了局部 solvability，又在回看时被证明是成功路径上的关键节点，那么它应得到更高奖励；如果只是带来轻微局部提升，但对最终成功并不关键，则应被下调。\n\n在这两个信号之上，BiCAA 设计了一个 modulation rule，把 hindsight criticality 用来调制 forward gain，形成更有区分度的 process reward。最后再把 process reward 与 trajectory-level outcome reward 一起做 unified advantage estimation，用于 RL 更新。这个设计的取舍很明确：不放弃终局监督，因为最终答案仍是全局目标；但也不再让中间步骤完全靠终局 reward 反传。与 vanilla GRPO 相比，BiCAA 的主要创新不在优化器，而在 reward decomposition 和 credit shaping。","experiments":"从给出的全文片段看，作者的实验重点是两类指标：一类是最终 answer quality，另一类是搜索行为质量，比如冗余搜索是否减少、搜索路径是否更高效。论文声称 BiCAA 在多步搜索任务上能降低 redundant search behavior，并取得有竞争力的答案质量。结合方法设计，最该关注的不是单一 benchmark 分数，而是过程层面的变化：平均搜索步数是否下降、无效检索比例是否下降、训练曲线是否更稳定、相比 vanilla GRPO 是否更少出现 policy collapse。\n\n由于当前提供的正文片段没有完整实验表和具体数字，能确认的结论是：作者把 dense process reward 与 outcome reward 联合使用，而不是只替换终局奖励；实验结论支持这种联合 credit assignment 优于纯 outcome-only 训练。若阅读全文，建议重点核对三类消融：只用 forward gain、只用 hindsight criticality、两者融合后的差异；以及 modulation rule 和 unified advantage estimation 各自带来的增益。如果这些消融充分，才能说明提升来自双向 credit assignment，而不是简单加了更多 reward shaping。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合泛读。先看 Introduction，确认作者到底在批评 vanilla GRPO 的什么缺陷。然后直接看方法部分里 forward solvability gain 和 hindsight success criticality 的定义，弄清楚每个信号在奖励里扮演什么角色。最后看实验中的行为分析，而不只是最终正确率。\n\n如果只有 15 到 25 分钟，建议看：任务定义、两个 reward 分支的公式、reward fusion / advantage estimation、主结果表、搜索效率分析。阅读时重点问三个问题。第一，forward gain 的计算是否依赖 ground-truth This raises a natura","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-03T06:10:33Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_01743.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"RL 后训练（RLHF/RLVR）会让策略偏离 base model，造成已有能力遗忘。标准 KL 正则把整个响应分布都锚定到 reference policy，既保留正确模式也保留错误模式，过度限制探索；完全去掉 KL 又会导致漂移和遗忘。问题是能否只在需要保留的地方施加约束。","importance":"对 pretrain-posttrain 接口有直接启发：post-train 阶段如何不破坏 pretrain 已获得的能力。CoKL 提出的'按正确性条件化约束'思路，把 KL 从全分布收缩到 verified-correct 响应的条件分布，是对 GRPO 等 RLVR 训练 recipe 的一个具体改进点。理论分析也澄清了 forward/reverse KL 在 imperfect reference 下会耦合'总正确率'与'正确响应内部相对概率'，这一点对设计 RL 正则有参考价值。","technical_approach":"CoKL 用 forward KL 约束 reference policy 与当前策略在'已验证正确响应集合'上的条件分布，即只锚定正确响应之间的相对概率分配，而不约束正确响应的总概率质量，也不约束错误响应。这样总正确率可以自由上升，错误模式可以被重塑，正确模式内部的多样性被保留。作者推导了 GRPO 下的有限组（finite-group）实际训练目标，并给出 population-level 分析：CoKL 解耦了 P(correct) 与 P(·|correct)，而 full-policy forward/reverse KL 会让两者耦合，在 reference 不完美时产生严格的最优正确率上界（correctness gap）。","experiments":"两类实验：一是受控多解仿真环境，用于观察分布层面的行为，验证 CoKL 确实只保留正确模式间的相对结构、不压制总正确率提升；二是跨多个模型规模的 continual post-training 实验，在持续 RL 任务序列上衡量 retention-adaptation 权衡。论文给出的是趋势性结论（CoKL 在保留旧能力的同时不损害新任务学习），未在截取内容中披露具体参数量和数字。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，建议重点看 Method 中条件 KL 的定义和 finite-group 目标推导，以及 population-level 分析里关于 forward/reverse KL 产生 correctness gap 的论证。仿真部分用于验证直觉，可快速浏览。预计 20–30 分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"RL post-training (RLHF/RLVR) drifts the policy away from the base model and erases already-acquired capabilities. transfor","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-31T22:26:45Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_00326.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作关心的不是“LLM会不会做单步代数变换”，而是更难的一层：当外部符号工具本身是精确的，模型能否在一个可验证但难搜索的多步任务里，学会规划、试错、回退、组装最终证明。作者选了 weighted sum-of-squares certificate search 作为载体，因为候选证书一旦写出来可以被严格校验，但找到它需要决定项如何重组、何时因式分解、何时改写、何时停止。问题的难点不在工具执行，而在全局搜索策略与中间表示选择。","importance":"对预训练本身的直接贡献不大，它更接近 post-training + tool-use 的受控研究；但对基座研究仍有两点启发。第一，它提供了一个“终局可精确验证、过程需要策略协调”的干净测试床，比很多开放式 agent 任务更适合分析训练信号质量。第二，它说明仅靠通用推理 backbone 加工具接口远远不够，若任务的瓶颈在搜索与表示切换，就需要专门的课程数据和 verifier-grounded optimization。量级上，这不是会改变预训练范式的工作，但对研究‘可验证环境中的工具协调能力如何形成’有参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法分三层。第一层是任务构造。作者没有只训练最终 SOS 任务，而是设计了九任务课程：四个局部代数操作任务，四个更偏结构选择的任务，再加一个复合的 weighted-SOS 搜索任务。这样做的原因很明确：如果直接在复合任务上学，模型拿到的监督太稀疏，很难学到哪些中间表示更有利于后续证书拼装；先用局部与结构任务提供密集监督，可以把搜索所需的代数先验拆开教。\n\n第二层是监督微调。基于 Phi-4-reasoning-plus，作者用 assistant-token QLoRA SFT 训练直接解题轨迹和模拟的符号执行轨迹。这里一个值得注意的设计是：SFT 语料里包含“像 Python 一样的已求值符号片段”，但不使用原生 function-call message 格式。也就是说，模型先学的是工具使用的语义与轨迹模式，而不是 API 协议本身。部署时再接入四个 SymPy-backed 原生函数。这个设计在方法上比较克制：它试图把“学会何时调用、如何解释结果”与“记住某个接口格式”分开。\n\n第三层是 verifier-grounded GRPO。作者继续用任务特定的符号奖励做 GRPO，尤其在 weighted-SOS 上，终局候选必须满足严格的结构约束：能被解析成有限和式、权重是精确正有理数、每一项是多项式平方，并且展开后与目标多项式逐项系数一致。这个奖励设计的关键不是模糊偏好，而是把正确性尽量锚定在可判定信号上。与一般数学 RL 不同，这里 reward hacking 空间相对小，因为终局验收是 exact verification。\n\n从全文看，真正的创新不在某个新算法，而在一个完整闭环：课程式代数监督 + 模拟轨迹 + 原生符号工具 + 精确验证奖励。它和常见 tool-use agent 工作的区别在于，作者把任务限制在一个可完全验证的代数域内，因此能更清楚地区分“工具本身正确”和“策略层面失败”。论文也很谨慎，没有把结果解释成对一般 agent 的普遍结论。","experiments":"实验规模和结论都比较清楚。训练数据是 135 万条合成样本，覆盖 8 个支撑任务和 weighted-SOS；测试集是单独生成的 9 万道题。完整系统在九个多项式任务上的 macro-average 为 91.75%。在最关键的 weighted-SOS 任务上，Full system 的 verified success 是 78.96%，而同一 backbone、同一工具接口但未完成整套后训练的 Base+Tools 为 44.73%。这个差值说明，工具接入本身不是主要增益来源，真正起作用的是代数课程和 verifier-grounded post-training 的组合。\n\n作者还比较了 Base、SFT、Base+Tools、Full。虽然没有给出所有想要的因果拆分，例如缺少 SFT+Tools without GRPO、以及 GRPO without tools 这两组，因此不能严格分离每个组件的独立贡献，但现有对比已经足够说明“只给工具”效果有限，“完整训练链路”才显著改善复合搜索任务。\n\n从结果解读上，有两个细节值得记。第一，八个支撑任务在训练和评测中都不使用原生工具调用，它们主要承担代数监督作用，因此高分不能直接等价为 tool execution 能力强，而更像是中间表示与局部操作能力被学到了。第二，weighted-SOS 的失败并不表示多项式非 SOS，因为测试样本是按可证实例构造的；失败只表示搜索没找到证书。这让错误分析更干净：问题是搜索策略不足，而不是任务本身无解。\n\n论文还提到终局失败类别分析，但从给出的内容看，重点应是区分解析失败、结构不合法、系数不匹配、搜索耗尽或过早停止等几类。这类分类对后续改进比单一成功率更有用，因为它能告诉你瓶颈是在表示、调用、还是终止判定。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合泛读，不建议投入太多时间抠每个代数细节。优先看 Introduction 和 Verified Weighted-SOS Search，先把任务边界弄清楚：什么是 exact verification，什么是 search difficulty。然后看 Figure 1 和 Figure 2，理解训练边界与部署边界是如何分开的，以及为什么局部正确的符号操作不等于全局证书搜索成功。接着看任务表和结果表，重点关注 Base、SFT、Base+Tools、Full 的差异。\n\n如果只花 20 到 30 分钟，建议抓三件事：一是九任务课程如何拆分；二是 SFT 轨迹与原生 tool call 的关系；三是作者如何定义 terminal verification。若想再深一点，可以看 failure categories，判断模型主要卡在“不会搜”还是“搜到了但不会规范表达”。\n\n不必把它当成数学证明系统论文来读，重点是它提供了一个较干净的 agent/post-train 研究范式：在可验证环境里研究工具协调，而不是在开放任务里混杂太多不可控因素。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not about whether an LLM can execute isolated algebraic manipulations. It targets a harder layer: given exact symbolic tools, can the model coordinate them in a multi-step search problem where final answers are easy to verify but hard to discover. The chosen setting is weighted sum-of-squares certificate search. A candidate certificate can be checked exactly by expansion, yet finding one requires strategic regrouping, representation changes, branch revision, and proper stopping. The bottleneck is global search control rather than local symbolic correctness.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense. Second, it shows that a general reasoning backbone plus tool access is insufficient when the task is dominated by search and representation choice; targeted curriculum data and verifier-grounded optimization are needed. During inference, it iteratively evaluates completion of each dimension for partial solutions, and computes residual cognitive demand as the core controller state instead of traditional discrete action sets. Combined with risk levels derived from self-written validation tests, uncertainty estimation, and reasoning health checks, the demand gap drives early stopping, action planning, and adaptive verification target generation (e.g., edge case checking, inverse verification). third, the direction-aware Taylor score-based FFN neuron attribution method is useful for mechanism analysis and capability localization of pretrained models.","technical_approach":"Distributed Safety Alignment (DSA) is a framework that eliminates single-point dependency of safety capabilities via redundant encoding. It intervenes at the input of down-projection layers in language-side feed-forward networks (FFNs), treating each feature coordinate as an individual neuron. It uses direction-aware first-order Taylor scores, computed from refusal loss gradients and neuron activation states, to precisely locate neurons that contribute most to safety refusal behavior. During adversarial redundant alignment training, it applies deterministic masking to the highest-scoring critical neurons (simulating targeted white-box attacks) and stochastic dropout to the remaining neurons (preventing safety capabilities from re-concentrating on a new minimal subset), forcing the model to redundantly encode refusal behavior across a broader set of compensatory neurons. Joint optimization on harmful refusal data and benign utility data preserves general capabilities, with no inference-time overhead.","experiments":"The provided full text fragment does not include the complete experimental section. ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-03T06:00:44Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_01735.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) 中，teacher 使用参考解、工具输出等推理时不可见的 privileged information 来加强监督，但 student 在推理时拿不到这些信息，产生 privilege illusion：模型表现得好像答案就在旁边，从而做出无依据断言、推理链漂移。现有方法要么直接蒸馏特权 teacher 的全部信号，要么做选择性过滤/重加权，但都没有从根本上解决 teacher 与 student 信息状态不对称的问题。","importance":"论文把 privilege illusion 的机制讲清楚了：问题不是信号太多，而是信息条件不对等导致的“纠缠蒸馏”。这对当前 R1 范式下大量 on-policy 蒸馏、拒绝采样后再蒸馏的实践有直接参考价值。尤其值得注意的是观察到普通 OPSD 的收益随模型增大而消失，但 DAPD 的增益在 1.7B 到 32B 上保持稳定，说明信息不对称是缩放到更大模型时蒸馏退化的一个真实原因，而不只是小模型训练不足带来的噪声。","technical_approach":"DAPD 由两部分组成。第一部分是 Dual-Path Anchoring（DPA）：引入一个 self-conditioned 的桥接分布，让 student 在训练时同时构造两条对齐路径——无特权路径对齐“没有参考时的 reference 行为与 rollout 行为”，特权路径对齐“都能看到参考时的二者行为”。关键是两条路径都在 matched information 下做对齐，避免把 teacher 因特权信息产生的行为错误蒸馏给无特权条件下的 student。第二部分是 Dual-Source Anchoring（DSA）：reference 与 rollout 互为 teacher/student 双向蒸馏，既用参考解保证正确性，又用当前策略 rollout 保证指导信号在 student 可达区域内，避免 reference 偏离 student 当前策略分布导致蒸馏信号失真。","experiments":"在 Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B/14B/32B 上测试，覆盖数学推理（MAA、HMMT）、代码、指令跟随共 6 个 分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) for LLM post-training, the teacher is often strengthened with privileged information such as reference completions or tool outputs that the student cannot access at inference time. This information asymmetry causes a privilege illusion: the student acts as if privileged context were still available, producing unsupported claims and degraded reasoning. Existing approaches either distill the privileged teacher directly or selectively filter/reweight its signal, but neither resolves the structural mismatch between teacher and student information states.","importance":"The paper gives a clean mechanistic diagnosis: privilege illusion arises not from too much supervision, but from entangled distillation under mismatched conditioning. This is directly relevant to current R1-style pipelines that rely heavily on on-policy sampling, rejection sampling, and teacher-guided distillation. A practically important finding is that vanilla OPSD gains vanish as model size grows, while DAPD gains remain stable from 1.7B to 32B, suggesting that information asymmetry is a genuine scaling-related failure mode rather than a small-model artifact.","technical_approach":"DAPD combines two anchoring mechanisms. Dual-Path Anchoring (DPA) introduces a self-conditioned bridge distribution and constructs two alignment paths under matched information: an unconditioned path aligns reference and rollout behavior when neither sees privileged information, and a privileged path aligns them when both do. This preserves dense OPSD supervision while avoiding the leak of privilege-dependent behavior into the student's inference-time policy. Dual-Source Anchoring (DSA) then applies the same alignment in both directions between reference and rollout, so references provide reliable correctness guidance and on-policy rollouts provide student-reachable guidance within the current policy support.","experiments":"Evaluated on Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B/14B/32B across six benchmarks covering math reasoning (MAA, HMMT), coding, and instruction following. Ablations confirm that matching information access via the self-conditioned bridge alone substantially reduces unsupported assertions, and that bidirectional source anchoring is more robust than one-directional reference-to-rollout distillation.","reading_guide":"Skim-level read. Both are speech tasks evaluated in the same shared embedding space, so the usual intuition—more speech data should improve speech understanding broadly—fails here. The counterfactual audit protocol provides a rigorous way to validate sparse attention schemes beyond top-line benchmark scores. The observed tradeoff between signal concentration and integration loss also informs the design of sparsity-aware pretraining targets.","technical_approach":"First, the authors use Block Sparse Flash Attention (BSFA) route replay across four model architectures to establish that sparse routing causally alters content influence on outputs. They then design a dense-calibrated counterfactual audit protocol: three matched probe blocks (Gold with correct answer, Poison with target wrong answer, Benign filler) are placed in a single prompt, with 6 permutations to eliminate position effects. They record the selector's natural route, then measure each probe's effect on answer margin under forced routing. The primary metric Δ subtracts the dense attention content effect (measured on the same kernel path with no pruning) from the sparse content effect: positive Δ means sparse attention amplifies the probe's influence, negative Δ means dense attention does. Each episode is stored in an external memory, scored, and retrieved for similar future tasks to shape later behavior. Viewed through a reward lens, the stored score is a proxy reward for an implicit, non-parametric policy. Each retrieved episode then becomes a policy-improvement step whose reliability hinges on how that score is produced. In deployment, ground-truth labels are unavailable, so the stored re","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-29T12:20:14Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2608_00017.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CE"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究的是 memory-based self-improving agent 里的一个失真源：agent 不更新参数，而是把过往 episode 写入外部记忆库，并用一个分数决定哪些记忆会被未来检索和复用。问题在于，部署环境通常没有真实标签，这个分数只能由 LLM 自评或近似 judge 给出。论文指出，这会产生系统性的 reward inflation：错误 episode 可能被高分写入，并在后续被反复检索，形成作者称为 Echo Gap 的正反馈回路。核心问题不是一般性的 judge 噪声，而是“被高估的错误经验会持续进入策略循环并放大”。","importance":"它和预训练不是直接同一层问题，但和 pretrain↔post-train 接口、reward 信号质量、无标签自改进都有直接关系。对基座研究员更重要的启发是：只要学习信号来自模型自身评估，而不是外部可校验反馈，就要警惕 reward hacking 从一次性偏差变成持久性记忆污染。这个结论对 memory agent、RLAIF、self-training、合成偏好数据都有参考意义。论文的贡献主要在诊断和机制刻画，而不是提出一个通用强方法。","technical_approach":"技术上分三层。第一层是现象定义。作者把 memory retrieval 看成一种隐式、非参数化 policy，memory score 则对应 proxy reward。这样一来，写入高分记忆并在相似任务上复用，就等价于在一个外部记忆空间里做 policy improvement。Echo Gap 指的是：自评奖励与真实成功之间存在系统偏差，而且这种偏差与未来检索频率相关，因此会被动态放大。\n\n第二层是理论诊断。论文提出 Error-Independence Assumption, EIA，用来刻画“更强的 verifier 为什么不一定能纠偏”。如果 verifier 的误差与原始 self-grade 的偏差高度相关，那么即便 verifier 平均更准，也无法对具体 memory 做有效去膨胀。作者把可恢复收益写成依赖两个量的函数：一是 verifier score 与真实效用的一致性，二是 verifier error 与原始 bias 的去相关程度。这里的关键判断很实用：纠偏不只需要更高能力，还需要不同错误模式。\n\n第三层是缓解方法。作者反对 label-blind 的全局 calibration，因为全局映射只能重标定分数分布，无法识别“哪条记忆被高估”。他们主张 per-memory demotion，即对每条 memory 用额外、尽量去相关的信号做个体化降权。文中还给出一种 answer-free de-inflation 方案，并在 Memento 风格的 retrieve–inject–write agent 上验证。这里“answer-free”强调的是不依赖真实标签，而是利用与原始自评不同的信息通道来修正写回分数。","experiments":"实验分成两部分。第一部分看 memory bank 本身的分数失真。作者在多个模型家族和 live factual banks 上观察到，错误 episode 会被系统性高估，而且这种高估与被复用频率相关，不是简单的随机噪声。更强的 re-grader 往往整体更接近真实标签，但其错误仍可能和原始 self-grade 偏差同向，因此无法稳定修正 bank 排序。\n\n第二部分看端到端行为后果。作者在 BIRD text-to-SQL 上搭建了一个较忠实的 Memento-style memory loop：agent 检索相似历史、注入上下文、完成当前任务、再把新 episode 写回记忆库，且写回时不访问真实标签。评测则用 benchmark 官方 execution accuracy。结果是，自评写回会逐步污染 memory bank，而提出的 de-inflation 方法在多随机种子下都优于 self-graded baseline，说明 Echo Gap 不只是评分层面的统计现象，而会转化为真实行为退化。\n\n从摘要和正文片段能确认的重点，是现象稳定存在、理论条件与实证一致、以及 mitigation 在真实 memory loop 中有效。现有片段没有完整表格和精确数值，无法可靠给出提升点数或误差幅度，因此不应虚构具体数字。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓住作者的建模视角：memory score 不是普通 metadata，而是代理奖励。理解这一点后，再看 Echo Gap 的定义和 Theorem 1，重点不是公式本身，而是“为什么错误高分会在检索闭环里乘法式放大”。\n\n然后看 EIA 那一节。这里是全文最有信息量的部分，因为它解释了为什么“换一个更强 judge”常常不够。实验部分建议优先看两类图表：1）memory score 与真实正确性的偏差分布；2）端到端 BIRD agent 在不同写回策略下的 execution accuracy 变化。若只做泛读，30 分钟左右足够；若关心 RLAIF、self-training 或 memory agent 的训练信号问题，EIA 和 de-inflation 细节值得细看。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a failure mode in memory-based self-improving LLM agents. These agents do not update model weights; instead, they store past episodes in external memory, assign each episode a utility score, and retrieve high-scoring similar episodes for future tasks. In deployment, ground-truth labels are usually unavailable, so the stored utility is produced by self-grading or an LLM judge. The core problem is that incorrect episodes can receive inflated rewards, get written into memory, and then be preferentially reused, creating a persistent feedback loop. The paper calls this phenomenon the Echo Gap.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense, but it is highly relevant to the quality of learning signals in post-training and self-improvement pipelines. The main lesson for foundation-model research is that once model-generated evaluation is used as a reward proxy, reward hacking can become persistent memory corruption rather than a one-off grading error. That matters for memory agents, RLAIF, self-training, synthetic preference generation, and any setup where future behavior is shaped by model-produced scores without external verification.","technical_approach":"$43","experiments":"$44","reading_guide":"Begin with the framing: memory scores are treated as proxy rewards, not just metadata. That lens makes the rest of the paper much easier to follow. Then read the Echo Gap definition and the theorem around multiplicative amplification in retrieval loops. The important point is not the formalism itself, but why inflated mistakes become self-reinforcing.\n\nNext, focus on the EIA section. This is the conceptual center of the paper because it explains why replacing self-grading with a stronger LLM judge often fails. In the experiments, prioritize the plots that compare stored utility against true correctness and the end-to-end BIRD results under different write-back strategies. A useful skim should take around 30 minutes. If you work on RLAIF, self-training, or memory-based agents, the EIA analysis and the de-inflation mechanism deserve a closer read.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得看失败案例和 judge 相关性分析；它提醒 memory self-improvement 不是免费 RL，奖励噪声会被检索放大。","en":"Read the failure cases and judge-correlation analysis; it treats memory self-improvement as non-free RL where reward noise is amplified by retrieval."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Mohammad Asadolahi","Amir Amini","Samira Talebi","Amirfarhad Farhadi","Azadeh Zamanifar"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["self-improving-agents","memory","reward-modeling","LLM-as-judge","failure-analysis"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00017","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.00017","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.02412","title":"Why Large Language Models Fail at Tabular Prediction","abstract":"Large language models (LLMs) have become the default tool for a remarkable range of tasks, yet they have had conspicuously little success at one of the most common machine learning workloads: predictive analytics over tabular data. The setup deliberately removes tools, code execution, retrieval, multi-turn scaffolding, custom system prompts, and fine-tuning. The object of study is the model’s single-pass behavior on a prompt containing the full training table and the test features.","importance":"The main value for pretraining research is diagnostic. The failure is not well explained by common surface hypotheses such as CSV serialization, numeric tokenization, test batching, or class overlap. The evidence points instead to a high-dimensional in-context learning limitation. This matters for structured-data pretraining: adding more table-shaped text or changing the textual format may not be enough if the model lacks a robust mechanism for fitting supervised predictors over many numeric features in context.","technical_approach":"The authors test five hypotheses with controlled interventions: noisy or non-linearly separable data, loss of column structure from linearized CSV, numeric value tokenization, the number of test queries in one prompt, and feature dimensionality. They also include a memorization probe to reduce contamination artifacts. The most important design is a random linear projection sweep over benchmark datasets, which changes dimensionality while controlling the information content. For behavioral modeling, they compare LLM predictions against classical learners and measure prediction agreement, not only task accuracy.","experiments":"The paper reports that the LLM can perform meaningful in-context classification on narrow tables, especially in two dimensions, but collapses as feature dimensionality grows. In a sweep over 31 benchmark datasets and nine methods, the LLM is the only method whose accuracy systematically decreases as features are added under random projections. In two-dimensional tasks, its decision boundaries are closely reproduced by local distance-based methods: Gaussian processes with short length scales and low-k nearest neighbors reach about 91–92% grid agreement. In higher dimensions, none of 252 configured classical baselines matches the model well; the best prediction agreement is 64.8%. Adding a tuned dimension-dependent label-noise model improves agreement by at most 0.64 percentage points. The model’s natural-language explanations often do not match its actual predictions.","reading_guide":"Skim time: about 30–45 minutes. Read the contamination probe first, then the hypothesis tests for serialization, numeric representation, and test-batch size. The behavioral modeling section is also useful: the contrast between near-local behavior in 2D and the failure to find a high-dimensional surrogate is the strongest empirical signal. The mechanism behind this gap, the causal role of late-layer representations in disambiguation outcomes, and the layer-wise dependency patterns across model scales remain uncharacterized.","importance":"The decoupling between representational geometry and predictive behavior in decoder-only Transformers is clarified, correcting the common practice of using late-layer cosine similarity as a direct measure of semantic encoding depth. 是否合理。最后看 calibration ensembling 的消融，判断它是在修复真实失配，还是只是在保守化 prediction set。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The work studies a failure mode of conformal prediction for LLM pipelines: the data distribution may stay fixed, while the inference configuration changes the nonconformity scores. Prompt templates, sampling temperature, and quantization can all alter the score assigned to the same labeled example. Since conformal coverage depends on exchangeability of the scores, not just exchangeability of the raw examples, these configuration changes can invalidate the nominal guarantee.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it is relevant to LLM evaluation and deployment-time calibration. In practice, an LLM system is a combination of weights, prompts, decoding rules, and serving precision. A calibration threshold obtained under one configuration should not be assumed valid under another. The useful lesson for foundation-model research is that post-training and inference recipes can shift score distributions enough to break statistical guarantees used by uncertainty, verification, or safety layers.","technical_approach":"The paper formalizes configuration shift as a change in the score map s(x, y), while keeping the example distribution fixed. It then runs a systematic empirical sweep over prompt templates, decoding temperature, and weight quantization, across 9 LLMs, 4 datasets, and 4 nonconformity scores. On the theory side, it derives coverage lower bounds where the discrepancy between calibration-score and test-score distributions appears explicitly. It also gives label-free variants based only on model predictions, enabling diagnostics without test labels. The proposed mitigations follow from this analysis: threshold correction using the discrepancy term and calibration ensembling that upweights configurations prone to undercoverage.","experiments":"The main empirical finding is that configuration shift primarily harms validity rather than efficiency. Empirical coverage can fall below the target level, while prediction-set sizes remain close to the i.i.d. baseline in runs that remain valid. More calibration data does not necessarily help; it can make the bias more apparent because sampling noise shrinks and the score-distribution mismatch dominates. The study covers three practical shift axes—prompting, temperature, and quantization—and shows that the derived diagnostics and corrections are actionable rather than only descriptive.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes about 25–35 minutes. Start with the definition of configuration shift and how it differs from covariate or domain shift. Then inspect the experimental setup: the three configuration axes, the four nonconformity scores, and the separation between coverage and set size. The theoretical section is worth reading for the form of the discrepancy term in the coverage bound; the full proof can be skipped unless CP theory is the focus. End with the threshold-correction and calibration-ensemble results, especially the ablations showing when they recover coverage and when they simply make sets more conservative.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"配置漂移影响保形预测覆盖率的实证结论对部署校准有参考，但与预训练方法论关系弱；看摘要和主表即可，无需深读。","en":"Empirical finding that configuration shift erodes CP coverage is useful for deployment calibration but weakly connected to pretraining methodology. 不只是参数量占比较高的逐点非线性模块，也会显著改变 residual direction 的运动轨迹。对于研究 rank collapse、表示聚集、FFN memory、层内并行近似的人，这篇值得泛读。它不提供新的预训练 recipe，也不直接改善 scaling 或数据效率。","technical_approach":"作者把 residual state 写成 x_i = r_i locality。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies residual-stream direction dynamics in Transformers. Its main claim is that attention-only particle dynamics cannot adequately explain the realized block update; the FFN should be modeled as a local steering field acting on each token state.","importance":"The work is more relevant to mechanistic analysis than to pretraining practice. It does not propose a new optimizer, data recipe, or scaling method. Its value is to make the FFN an explicit dynamical term: FFNs are not just position-wise nonlinear capacity or editable memory tables; their tangential component can steer residual directions in a way that attention alone misses. This is useful for readers studying representation collapse, residual-stream geometry, FFN memories, or parallel approximations of Transformer blocks.","technical_approach":"The formulation decomposes each residual state as x_i = r_i u_i, where u_i lies on the unit sphere and r_i is the residual norm. Directional motion is studied on the sphere. Attention is modeled as a non-local aggregation field depending on other tokens, while the FFN is modeled as a local field depending only on the current token state. Only the tangential projection of a field changes the residual direction; the radial component changes the norm and therefore the angular speed. This leads to an aggregation-steering ODE on a product of spheres. The paper then defines critical residual directions, residual attractors, tangent stability, FFN-induced spectral shifts, and the attention–FFN commutator. It also introduces a sequential-to-parallel defect that measures when a finite attention–FFN block can be approximated by a parallel additive map. The technical difference from prior attention-only dynamics is the explicit inclusion of the FFN as a first-class steering term.","experiments":"The empirical analysis uses checkpoints from GPT-2, Pythia, Mistral, and Llama families. The main findings are: the attention branch alone has a substantial angular-alignment deficit relative to the actual block update; the tangential FFN component is necessary for the observed motion in residual-direction space, while the radial component cannot substitute for it; FFN steering helps maintain diversity under aggregation pressure; and the sequential-to-parallel defect can guide practical block parallelization approximations. The appendix adds diagnostics on FFN gate calibration, prefix/Volterra sensitivity, local spectrum geometry, OV non-symmetry, and editing locality. The provided text does not include exact numerical tables, so the results should be read as qualitative and diagnostic evidence rather than an engineering recipe.","reading_guide":"A 20–30 minute skim is sufficient. Read the problem setting first to understand the x_i = r_i u_i decomposition, tangent projection, and the FFN steering field. In the theory part, focus on critical residual directions, tangent stability, and the attention–FFN commutator; the proofs can be skipped on first pass. In the experiments, prioritize the attention-only angular-deficit diagnostic and the tangential/radial FFN ablation, since they directly support the central claim. Readers interested in inference or training acceleration can also inspect the sequential-to-parallel defect section. Readers interested in model editing may want to sample the appendix material on editing locality.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"读 abstract 和干预实验即可；它把 FFN 从“MLP 黑盒”拉回 residual 几何，结论比纯 attention 动力学更贴近 Llama。","en":"Read the abstract and intervention results; it gives a cleaner residual-geometry role for FFNs than attention-only dynamics, especially for Llama-like models."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Timur Mudarisov","Mikhail Burtsev","Radu State"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["mechanistic-interpretability","residual-dynamics","ffn","transformer-theory"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02071","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.02071","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.01833","title":"Tunneling the Loss Landscape: Bypassing Memorization with Monte Carlo Parameter Swapping","abstract":"Grokking is a striking phenomenon in neural network training, where a model can undergo a prolonged period of pure memorization before abrupt generalization. red","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-03T07:44:57Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2608_01833.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cond-mat.dis-nn","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作用玻璃态动力学的工具分析 grokking：模型已经记住训练集但长期不能泛化，是否是因为参数空间进入了动力学迟滞，而不是因为泛化解在能量或熵意义上不可达。","importance":"对预训练研究的价值在训练动态和泛化机制，而不是直接给出 LLM 训练配方。它把 grokking 从“训练损失、权重范数、测试精度”的宏观描述推进到参数轨迹动力学：参数是否还在移动、不同 replica 是否会分叉、单条轨迹是否变成狭窄通道。这对理解大模型训练中的 plateau、迟发泛化、弱梯度区间和 optimizer-induced mobility 有借鉴意义。需要注意，它仍主要是 grokking testbed 上的机制研究，不能直接外推到大规模语言模型预训练。","technical_approach":"方法由两部分组成。第一部分是动力学诊断框架，包含三个量：Parameter Mobility 衡量训练中相邻 step 的参数移动幅度；Replica Correlation 衡量从同一状态分叉出的独立训练轨迹之间的相似度，用于观察历史依赖和 aging；Trajectory Fractal Dimension 衡量单条优化轨迹的形状，区分持续沿通道前进和高弯曲度搜索。作者用这些量检验 AdamW 训练中的 grokking 是否符合 glass-like kinetic arrest。第二部分是 SAM-Swap，即 State-Aware Monte Carlo Parameter Swapping。它借鉴玻璃体系中的 swap Monte Carlo，用非标准梯度步的参数交换来增加参数空间 mobility，目标是在不依赖长时间普通训练的情况下更快离开记忆解附近的迟滞区域。它和调 learning rate、weight decay 越慢，这是 aging 的典型动力学信号；Trajectory Fractal Dimension 在 memorization 后降到接近 1，表示轨迹变得接近一维、通道化，而不是在参数空间中充分探索。这些结果支持 grokking 是 kinetic arrest，而不是宏观能量壁垒或熵壁垒。SAM-Swap 的实验目标是缩短从记忆到泛化的等待时间，验证增加参数 mobility 是否能绕过长期 plateau。可见内容没有给出完整模型规模、数据集规模和提升幅度，精确数字需要看实验表格和算法章节。","reading_guide":"泛读 30–40 分钟。先看 Introduction 中对 glass relaxation、volume hypothesis 和 kinetic arrest 条件和对 baseline optimizer 的控制变量。若只关心 LLM 预训练，可跳过大部分物理类背景，把注意力放在 mobility collapse 与 optimizer 动态上。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether grokking is caused by a glass-like kinetic arrest in parameter space: the model memorizes quickly, gradients become weak, and the trajectory moves slowly toward generalizing solutions that are not macroscopically inaccessible.","importance":"The contribution is mainly about training dynamics and generalization mechanisms. It does not provide a direct large-language-model pretraining recipe, but it offers a useful lens for studying long plateaus, delayed generalization, weak-gradient regimes, and optimizer-dependent mobility. The central point is that training loss and weight norm are not enough to describe the transition; the geometry and mobility of the parameter trajectory also matter. Any implication for LLM-scale training should be treated cautiously because the evidence is from grokking-style controlled settings.","technical_approach":"The diagnostic framework combines three measurements. Parameter Mobility tracks stepwise movement in parameter space. Replica Correlation measures how independently evolved trajectories, branched from the same state, remain similar or decorrelate over time. Trajectory Fractal Dimension characterizes whether a path is channel-like and persistent or tortuous and exploratory. Applied to AdamW training, these quantities are used to test the computational-glass interpretation of grokking. The intervention is SAM-Swap, State-Aware Monte Carlo Parameter Swapping. Inspired by swap Monte Carlo in glassy systems, it introduces non-gradient parameter swaps to improve mobility and accelerate equilibration toward generalizing states. Unlike simply changing learning rate, weight decay, or gradient noise, the mechanism targets accessibility between parameter configurations.","experiments":"The main empirical signals are consistent with kinetic arrest. After memorization, Parameter Mobility collapses even though test accuracy remains poor. Replica Correlation shows two-step relaxation and aging: trajectories decorrelate more slowly when branched after longer waiting times. Trajectory Fractal Dimension drops to approximately 1 after memorization, indicating a narrow, persistent optimization channel rather than broad exploration. SAM-Swap is then used as a causal probe: if delayed generalization comes from low mobility, parameter-swapping moves should reduce the waiting time before generalization. The available text does not include complete numerical tables, model sizes, or absolute improvement values, so the most reliable takeaway is the qualitative dynamical pattern.","reading_guide":"Skim in 30–40 minutes. Read the setup that separates entropy or energy barriers from kinetic arrest. Then inspect the definitions of Parameter Mobility, Replica Correlation, and Fractal Dimension, since these are the reusable parts of the paper. In the experiments, focus on plots aligning PM, RC, FD, train accuracy, and test accuracy across the grokking transition. Existing methods improve memory accessibility, yet action-relevant information may still fail to guide the current decision because it is poorly formed, organized, prioritized, or presented. We call this post-access failure the Memory-Action Gap. We propose MemArbiter, a function-aware memory arbitration framework that addresses the memory-management-induced component of this gap","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-03T12:10:52Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2608_02113.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"现有长时序LLM智能体记忆模块主要解决信息存储和检索的可访问性问题，但存在未被明确定义的失效模式：与当前决策相关的信息已经被记忆系统获取，却因组织方式、优先级排序、呈现形式不合理，无法有效引导行动选择，该问题被定义为「记忆-行动间隙」，与已有的遗忘、检索失败两类问题有明确区别。","importance":"该工作提出的功能分层记忆、动态仲裁调度思路，对长上下文建模、智能体导向的预训练记忆结构设计有参考价值，尤其是不同类型记忆的差异化激活、粒度控制逻辑，可启发预训练阶段的结构化注意力、分层记忆等方向的设计。","technical_approach":"MemArbiter是一套功能感知的动态记忆仲裁框架：首先将交互历史拆解为原子记忆项，按决策功能划分为5个独立记忆库——目标、任务状态、约束、情景经历、参考事实；每个记忆项支持两种呈现粒度：信息完整的焦点形式、信息精简的外围形式；每一步决策时，先计算记忆库层面的当前任务需求度、记忆项层面的决策相关度，再通过时序呈现门控对每个记忆项执行提升、降级、保留、隐藏四类操作，最终在固定的每步token预算下，将选中的焦点和外围记忆组装为结构化prompt送入模型生成行动。","experiments":"基于ReAct智能体在ALFWorld具身任务基准上评测，严格对齐所有对比方法的每步记忆token预算：500token预算下成功率达82.8%，750token预算下达92.5%，分别比最强基线高20.9和25.4个百分点；消融实验验证功能分库、双粒度呈现、时序门控三个核心组件均有独立贡献；MemArbiter同时提升了行动失败后的单步恢复率，显著减少了重复失败行动、状态-行动循环的问题，实验结果在开源权重模型和闭源模型上保持一致。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，重点关注「记忆-行动间隙」的定义、五个功能记忆库的设计逻辑、动态优先级调度机制，可进一步思考这类功能拆分思路是否可下沉到预训练层面的注意力偏置、结构化记忆架构设计中，预计阅读时间20分钟。","authors_note":"作者所属机构未在提供的公开内容中明确标注。"},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Existing memory modules for long-horizon LLM agents primarily address information storage and retrieval accessibility, but suffer from an undocumented failure mode: action-relevant information is already accessible to the memory pipeline, yet fails to guide action selection due to poor organization, prioritization, or presentation. This failure mode is formalized as the 'Memory-Action Gap', distinct from well-documented issues of forgetting and retrieval failure.","importance":"The proposed function-tiered memory organization and dynamic arbitration mechanism offers useful reference for long-context modeling and memory design for agent-centric pretraining, particularly the logic of differentiated activation and granularity control for different memory types, which can inspire structured attention and hierarchical memory designs at the pretraining stage.","technical_approach":"MemArbiter is a function-aware dynamic memory arbitration framework. It first decomposes interaction history into atomic memory items, which are organized into five independent functional memory banks: Goal, Task State, Constraint, Episodic Trace, and Reference Fact. Each item supports two rendering granularities: a fully detailed focal form, and a compact ambient form. At each decision step, the framework computes bank-level demand for the current task context and item-level relevance to the pending decision, then applies one of four operations to each item via a temporal presentation gate: promote, demote, retain, or hide. Selected focal and ambient memories are assembled into a structured prompt under a fixed per-step token budget for action generation.","experiments":"Implemented on a ReAct agent and evaluated on the ALFWorld embodied task benchmark, with strictly matched per-step memory token budgets across all methods. MemArbiter achieves 82.8% success rate under a 500-token budget and 92.5% under a 750-token budget, outperforming the strongest baseline by 20.9 and 25.4 percentage points respectively. Ablation studies confirm independent contributions from all three core components: functional bank separation, dual-granularity representation, and temporal gating. MemArbiter also delivers higher one-step recovery rates after action failures, substantially reduces repeated failed actions and state-action loops, with consistent results across both open-weight and proprietary models.","reading_guide":"Skim recommended. The latter yields unstable gains, and has not turned failure-conditioned, lifecycle-wide editing of an existing executable runtime into a learnable capability driven by realized task utility.","importance":"It proposes a complementary layer of continual improvement orthogonal to model-weight training: training a dedicated harness engineer model via online RL to edit the runtime system from failures, while keeping the target agent frozen. For pretraining and post-training research, it highlights that agent capability is not solely determined by the base model; runtime mechanisms are also trainable and scalable optimization targets. It also demonstrates a practical instantiation of GRPO over code-patch generation with grounded execution feedback, and shows that the target model and the harness engineer can co-evolve.","technical_approach":"Harness-R1 formulates harness editing as a batch-failure-conditioned online RL problem, where the reward signal comes from the realized task-performance change after reruns. The target agent stays frozen; a separate 9B-parameter harness engineer is trained. The policy is first initialized via cold-start supervised fine-tuning, then optimized online with GRPO. Conditioned on batches of target-agent failures, the engineer generates constrained executable runtime patches. After validation, patches are applied to the target agent, and the same batch of tasks is rerun. The resulting performance delta is used as reward to update only the engineer. Editable surfaces span interdependent lifecycle stages: context construction, tool mediation, state management, action validation, control and recovery logic; constraints are enforced to avoid breaking interfaces or introducing unrelated changes.","experiments":"Evaluated on three interactive benchmarks: WebShop, ALFWorld, and DBBench. Th","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-02T14:49:24Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2608_01285.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"长程 Agent 记忆访问存在固定计算深度的问题：轻量检索可能丢失跨步骤、因果或时间依赖，而对每次查询都执行完整深度记忆推理又会带来不必要的延迟和推理成本。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接启发有限，更偏向 Agent 记忆系统与推理时计算分配。但其“先共享检索前缀，再由证据充分性决定是否继续计算”的思路，与自适应计算、条件化推理、测试时计算预算分配有相通之处，可作为思考模型何时停止深入推理的工程参考。","technical_approach":"Router-Mem 将记忆查询组织为渐进式执行。首先执行一个共享的低成本检索前缀，得到初始证据上下文；随后由轻量 sufficiency router 判断当前证据是否足以回答查询。路由器使用证据级监督和 rationale distillation 训练，推理时输出单 token 决策。若证据充分，直接生成答案并跳过后续计算；若不充分，则复用已有检索命中作为定位信号，扩展相关记忆块并调用更深层的分析与证据聚合。快速路径和慢速路径通过共享前缀耦合，避免重新检索。","experiments":"在 AMA-Bench 和 BEAM 上，Router-Mem 分别取得 55.17% 和 38.77% 的分数，同时相对完整记忆处理流程将平均推理时间降低 27.3% 和 25.5%。不同路由阈值下，方法在质量—延迟权衡上形成较优的 Pareto 前沿，说明提前终止主要节省了证据已局部充分的查询，而没有明显牺牲困难查询的回答质量。","reading_guide":"泛读即可。建议重点看引言中两类记忆系统的对比、图 1 的框架设计，以及方法部分对共享检索前缀、sufficiency router 和检索引导续算的描述。实验部分关注质量—延迟曲线和阈值消融。预计阅读时间约 20–30 分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Long-horizon agent memory systems use a fixed processing depth: lightweight retrieval may miss temporal, causal, or cross-step dependencies, while running full memory research for every query adds unnecessary latency and inference cost.","importance":"The work is mainly an agent-memory and inference-time compute allocation paper rather than a pretraining paper. Its relevance to foundation-model research is indirect: the shared retrieval prefix followed by a learned sufficiency gate is a concrete instance of adaptive computation and conditional reasoning, useful when thinking about when a model should stop thinking or retrieve more context.","technical_approach":"Router-Mem frames memory access as evidence-conditioned progressive execution. Every query first runs a shared low-cost retrieval prefix to obtain initial evidence. A lightweight sufficiency router, trained with evidence-level supervision and rationale distillation, makes a single-token decision about whether that evidence is enough. If sufficient, the system answers immediately and skips the remaining pipeline. If not, it reuses the retrieval hits as localization signals, expands relevant memory blocks, and runs deeper analysis and evidence aggregation. The fast and slow paths share the retrieval prefix, so the slow path does not restart search from scratch.","experiments":"On AMA-Bench and BEAM, Router-Mem scores 55.17% and 38.77% while reducing average inference time by 27.3% and 25.5% relative to full memory processing. Across routing thresholds, it maintains a favorable quality–latency Pareto frontier, suggesting that early termination saves compute mainly on locally answerable queries without substantially hurting harder cases.","reading_guide":"Skim. trajectory and gradually push visitation into states where teacher guidance is no longer reliable; purely local criteria such as teacher-student KL can identify candidate intervention points, but they cannot reliably determine whether teacher intervention at those points will actually help or hurt future returns.","importance":"The main relevance is to post-training for agentic policies, but two observations are useful more broadly for pretrain-style thinking about training-signal selection. First, it moves from per-step local filtering to a short-horizon future-validation of whether a candidate supervision signal improves the student’s future trajectory distribution, a pattern that parallels data-value estimation and curation in pretraining. Second, it quantifies how quickly student deviation erodes the usefulness of teacher guidance under on-policy drift. The method is incremental over existing OPD work, but the empirical characterization of intervention efficacy is informative.","technical_approach":"FutureBridge-OPD (FTB) operates in two stages. It first selects candidate intervention points using teacher-student disagreement (high-KL positions), similar in spirit to prior filtering or reweighting schemes. At each candidate point, it then executes a short teacher bridge by substituting the teacher action for the student action, and lets the same student policy continue rolling out from that bridged state. The induced future continuation is used to check whether the bridge increases the density of teacher-preferred / positive distillation signals downstream; only validated bridges are kept as distillation supervision. Unlike TCOD or Guided-OPD, which reshape the student state distribution through teacher prefixes, curriculum schedules, or adaptive rollout depth, FTB does not force teacher takeover. Unlike pure KL-based filtering, it evaluates supervision value by its effect on the future student trajectory rather than by local disagreement alone.","experiments":"Evaluation is on ALFWorld, WebShop, and ScienceWorld. A diagnostic on 1,000 failed trajectories shows that replacing student actions at high-KL positions improves environment rewards on about 31% of trajectories, versus only 6% at random positions; even at high-KL positions, about 19% of teacher interventions degrade trajectory performance. After adopting a teacher action, the share of teacher-preferred tokens in the student continuation increases by about 10.8% in relative terms. End-to-end, FTB improves average success rate by 16.6 points over vanilla OPD and 7.6 points over TCOD-B2F, with consistent gains across student scales and teacher settings.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. It has direct value for reliability engineering in large-scale agent deployment, and offers a new data source idea for failure signal collection in post-pretraining alignment stages.","technical_approach":"The framework has three core components: 1) Three base telemetry channels (semantic embeddings of step outputs, token-level uncertainty aggregates, action metadata) totaling 43 dimensions, plus an additional content-grounding channel to close content blind spots. 2) A core detector using an echo state network (ESN) ensemble with CUSUM alarms: each channel uses a fixed sparse random recurrent map (only a ridge regression readout layer is trained) for next-step prediction, ensemble prediction error is used as the anomaly score, and CUSUM accumulates persistent shifts to trigger alarms. 3) Per-stream dual-budget thresholds and supervised fusion weights to adapt to different deployment false-alarm budgets.","experiments":"Validated on 2,823 agent episodes across 25 datasets, 3 frameworks (bespoke, LangGraph, AutoGen), 4 models (Qwen2.5 7B/3B, Llama3.1 8B, Gemini-2.5-Flash), including 770 episodes with real tool calls. When a model produces highly diverse attack trajectories, adding more rules or retraining the monitor cannot solve the coverage problem by itself. The result is relevant whenever fixed filters, invariants, or pattern-based safety policies meet high-entropy model behavior.","technical_approach":"The paper proves an entropy-coverage bound: for a monitor with k fixed LTL invariants, recall is upper-bounded by the total probability mass of the k most frequent trigger-completion patterns in the attack distribution. Low-entropy attacks concentrate on a few patterns, so a fixed invariant set can achieve high recall. High-entropy attacks spread across many structurally different patterns, so any tractable fixed set covers only a small fraction. The authors also propose a pre-deployment entropy test that estimates attack-distribution entropy from 50–100 trajectories and predicts whether an FSA monitor is likely to provide meaningful coverage on a given backend.","experiments":"The empirical study covers eight frontier LLM architectures and 847 adversarial trajectories.
